<script>/* Keep this independent of the app so even a parse failure gets a visible error. */
(function () {
  var painted=false,timer;
  function fail(error) {
    if(painted)return;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    document.getElementById('startupError').hidden=false;
    document.getElementById('startupErrorDetail').textContent=String(error&&error.message||error||'Startup stopped before the homepage could open.').slice(0,500);
    document.getElementById('connectionText').textContent='Page startup error';
    document.getElementById('requestProgress').hidden=true;
  }
  function onError(event){if(event.message)fail(event.message);}
  function onRejection(event){fail(event.reason);}
  window.addEventListener('error',onError);
  window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',onRejection);
  document.getElementById('reloadStartup').addEventListener('click',function(){window.location.reload();});
  window.TribeStartup={fail:fail,painted:function(){
    painted=true;clearTimeout(timer);
    document.getElementById('startupError').hidden=true;
    window.removeEventListener('error',onError);
    window.removeEventListener('unhandledrejection',onRejection);
  }};
  timer=setTimeout(function(){fail('The page scripts did not start. Check that the complete Index.html file was deployed.');},12000);
})();
</script>
<script>/* Shared domain logic. All live calls are authorized again in Apps Script. */
var Tribe = (function () {
  var presets = {
    neon: 'linear-gradient(120deg,#17230e,#a4e638 48%,#1b422b)',
    aurora: 'linear-gradient(120deg,#25135c,#9465fa 42%,#54d7c5)',

    ember: 'linear-gradient(120deg,#301019,#f46439,#ffce7a)',

  };
  function config() { return {
    id:'main', logo:'', title:'The Monke Tribe', tagline:'Good games. Great people. Monke energy.',

    content:{rules:'01  Be good to your fellow monkes.\nTreat everyone with respect. No harassment, hate, threats, or bullying.\n\n02  Keep it safe.\nDo not share private information, explicit content, scams, or suspicious links.\n\n03  Keep the jungle clean.\nNo spam, point farming, or exploits. Post in the right channel.\n\n04  Play fair.\nRespect the staff and report problems. Owners may remove content or suspend accounts.',upcoming:'On the horizon\nMore community events, cosmetics, and minigames.\n\nThe owner can publish the next update here.',updates:'v1.0 · Welcome to the tribe\nThe jungle is open. Public channels, DMs and group conversations, slots, fictional stocks, MonkeGPT, a points shop, roles, profiles, and reactive wallpapers are here.',credits:'The Monke Tribe\nCreated for the tribe.\n\nMonke image supplied by the site owner.\nWebsite and minigames built with assistance from OpenAI.\nPassword hashing: bcrypt.js by Daniel Wirtz, Apache-2.0 (with BSD-licensed portions).\nIcons: Lucide, ISC license.\nThis is an independent community website.'}
  }; }
  function catalog() { return [
    {id:'banner-aurora',name:'Northern lights',type:'banner',price:180,value:'aurora',animated:true,description:'A little cosmic energy for your profile.',active:true},
    {id:'banner-neon',name:'Jungle pulse',type:'banner',price:120,value:'neon',animated:true,description:'The signature tribe glow.',active:true},
    {id:'color-lime',name:'Lime legend',type:'color',price:80,value:'#b9f451',description:'Stand out in every conversation.',active:true},
    {id:'frame-orbit',name:'Orbit frame',type:'frame',price:160,value:'orbit',description:'Give your avatar its own atmosphere.',active:true},
    {id:'font-mono',name:'Terminal monke',type:'font',price:100,value:'mono',description:'A fresh typeface for your display name.',active:true},
    {id:'avatar-upload',name:'Your own face',type:'avatar',price:200,value:'upload',description:'Unlock custom profile picture uploads.',active:true},
    {id:'banner-sunset',name:'After hours',type:'banner',price:140,value:'sunset',animated:false,description:'Warm skies. Late-night conversations.',active:true},
    {id:'badge-banana',name:'Certified banana',type:'badge',price:70,value:'🍌',description:'A badge with excellent taste.',active:true},
    {id:'color-purple',name:'Purple haze',type:'color',price:80,value:'#bda1ff',description:'A violet tint for your name.',active:true}
  ]; }
  function fail(s){var e=new Error(s);e.name='TribeError';throw e;}
  function str(v,max,min){v=String(v==null?'':v).trim();if(v.length>(max||5000)||v.length<(min||0))fail('Please check the length of your entry.');return v;}
  function hex(v){if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(v||''))fail('Choose a valid color.');return v;}
  function url(v){v=str(v,2048,8);if(!/^https:\/\/[^\s/@]+(?:[^\s]*)$/i.test(v))fail('Use a complete HTTPS link.');return v;}
  function image(v){if(/^data:image\/(png|jpeg|webp|gif);base64,[a-z0-9+/=]+$/i.test(v||'')&&v.length<=30000)return v;return url(v);}
  function role(c,u){return c.roles.find(function(r){return r.id===u.role;})||c.roles[c.roles.length-1];}
  function admin(u){return !!u&&['owner','coowner'].indexOf(u.role)>=0;}
  function protect(u){return admin(u);}
  function member(u){if(!u)fail('Log in to join in.');if(u.banned)fail('This account is suspended.');if(u.role==='pending')fail('Your account is waiting for a role from the owner or co-owner.');}
  function pub(u){var x={};['id','username','displayName','role','points','avatar','bio','equipped','joined','lastSeen','banned'].forEach(function(k){x[k]=u[k];});x.collectionCount=(u.inventory||[]).length;return x;}
  function me(u){if(!u)return null;var x=pub(u);['inventory','prefs','daily','dailyClaims','dailyTotal','earned','mutedUntil'].forEach(function(k){x[k]=u[k];});return x;}
  function user(id,name,now){return {id:id,username:name.toLowerCase(),displayName:name,role:'pending',points:50,avatar:'',bio:'',equipped:{},inventory:[],prefs:{wallpaper:'site',motion:true,glow:true,particles:40,monke:true},joined:now,lastSeen:now,banned:false,mutedUntil:0,daily:'',dailyClaims:0,dailyTotal:0,earned:{date:'',chat:0,mini:0,external:0},lastMessage:0,lastReward:0};}
  // A missed calendar day pauses progress; it never resets the reward track.
  // Legacy records retained only the most recent claim date, so carry one day forward.
  function dailyReward(day){return day<=2?10:day*5;}
  function dailyStatus(u,now){
    var date=new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0,10);
    var completed=u?(Number.isSafeInteger(u.dailyClaims)&&u.dailyClaims>=0?u.dailyClaims:(u.daily?1:0)):0;
    var total=u?(Number.isSafeInteger(u.dailyTotal)&&u.dailyTotal>=0?u.dailyTotal:(u.daily?15:0)):0;
    var claimed=!!u&&!!u.daily&&u.daily>=date;
    return {completed:completed,total:total,claimed:claimed,day:completed+1,reward:dailyReward(completed+1),date:date,nextAt:Date.parse(date+'T00:00:00.000Z')+86400000};
  }
  function run(ctx,action,p){
    p=p||{};
    if(typeof TribeSocial!=='undefined'&&!ctx.skipSocial){
      if(!ctx.readOnly){TribeSocial.recover(ctx);if(ctx.user)ctx.user=ctx.db.get('Users',ctx.user.id);}
      if(TribeSocial.handles(action))return TribeSocial.run(ctx,action,p);
      if(TribeSocial.needsApproval(ctx,action,p))return TribeSocial.queue(ctx,action,p);
    }
    if(typeof TribeArcade!=='undefined'&&TribeArcade.handles(action))return TribeArcade.run(ctx,action,p);
    if(typeof TribeExpansion!=='undefined'&&TribeExpansion.handles(action))return TribeExpansion.run(ctx,action,p);
    p=p||{};var db=ctx.db,u=ctx.user,now=ctx.now||Date.now(),c=db.get('Config','main')||config();
    function saveUser(){db.put('Users',u);}
    function requireAdmin(){member(u);if(!admin(u))fail('Only the owner and co-owner can do that.');}
    function audit(kind,detail){db.put('Audit',{id:ctx.id(),actor:u.id,kind:kind,detail:detail,at:now});}
    function allowThread(id){member(u);if(['general','gaming','off-topic'].indexOf(id)>=0)return true;var t=db.get('Threads',id);if(!t||t.members.indexOf(u.id)<0)fail('You do not have access to that conversation.');return t;}
    function earn(kind,amount,cap){var d=new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0,10);if(u.earned.date!==d)u.earned={date:d,chat:0,mini:0,external:0};amount=Math.max(0,Math.min(amount,cap-u.earned[kind]));u.earned[kind]+=amount;u.points+=amount;saveUser();return amount;}
    function getItem(id){var s=db.get('Shop',id);if(!s)fail('That item is unavailable.');return s;}
    function target(){var t=db.get('Users',p.userId);if(!t)fail('Member not found.');return t;}
    function boot(){var safe=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(c));delete safe.inviteCode;return {config:safe,me:me(u),members:db.all('Users').map(pub),shop:db.all('Shop'),games:db.all('Games').filter(function(g){return g.active!==false||(u&&u.role==='owner');}).map(TribeExpansion.gamePublic),threads:p.threads!==false&&u&&u.role!=='pending'?db.all('Threads').filter(function(t){return t.members.indexOf(u.id)>=0;}):[],preview:p.preview!==false&&u&&u.role!=='pending'?db.all('Messages').filter(function(m){return m.thread==='general'&&!m.deleted;}).slice(-4):[],serverTime:now,needsOwner:!db.all('Users').some(function(a){return a.role==='owner';})};}
    function cosmetics(people){var ids={};people.forEach(function(person){Object.keys(person.equipped||{}).forEach(function(k){var id=person.equipped[k];if(id)ids[id]=true;});});return Object.keys(ids).map(function(id){return db.get('Shop',id);}).filter(Boolean);}
    function recentMessages(test,limit){return db.recent?db.recent('Messages',test,limit):db.all('Messages').filter(test).slice(-limit);}
    if(action==='boot'&&p.compact){
      var safe=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(c));delete safe.inviteCode;
      return {config:safe,me:me(u),members:u?[pub(u)]:[],shop:u?cosmetics([u]):[],games:[],threads:[],preview:[],loaded:{members:false,shop:false,games:false},serverTime:now,needsOwner:ctx.needsOwner?ctx.needsOwner():!db.all('Users').some(function(a){return a.role==='owner';})};
    }
    if(action==='boot')return boot();
    if(action==='pageData'){
      if(!Array.isArray(p.parts)||p.parts.length>3||p.parts.some(function(k){return ['members','shop','games'].indexOf(k)<0;}))fail('Unknown page data.');
      var data={};p.parts.forEach(function(k){if(k==='members')data.members=db.all('Users').map(pub);if(k==='shop')data.shop=db.all('Shop');if(k==='games')data.games=db.all('Games').filter(function(g){return g.active!==false||(u&&u.role==='owner');}).map(TribeExpansion.gamePublic);});return data;
    }
    if(action==='heartbeat'){if(!u)fail('Log in first.');return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='preferences'){if(!u)fail('Log in to save your preferences.');var v=p.prefs||{};if(['site','particles','grid','aurora','rain','still','custom'].indexOf(v.wallpaper)<0)fail('Unknown wallpaper.');u.prefs={wallpaper:v.wallpaper,motion:!!v.motion,glow:!!v.glow,monke:!!v.monke,particles:Math.max(10,Math.min(80,Number(v.particles)||40)),custom:v.custom?image(v.custom):''};saveUser();return {me:me(u)};}
    if(action==='profile'){if(!u)fail('Log in first.');u.displayName=str(p.displayName,24,2);u.bio=str(p.bio,180);if(p.avatar!==undefined){if(p.avatar&&!u.inventory.some(function(id){return getItem(id).type==='avatar';})&&!admin(u))fail('Unlock custom profile pictures in the shop first.');u.avatar=p.avatar?image(p.avatar):'';}saveUser();return {me:me(u)};}
    member(u);
    if(action==='activity'){
      var preview=p.preview?recentMessages(function(m){return m.thread==='general'&&!m.deleted;},4):[];
      var authorIds={};preview.forEach(function(m){authorIds[m.author]=true;});
      var authors=Object.keys(authorIds).map(function(id){return db.get('Users',id);}).filter(Boolean).map(pub);
      return {threads:p.threads?db.all('Threads').filter(function(t){return t.members.indexOf(u.id)>=0;}):[],preview:preview,members:authors,shop:cosmetics(authors)};
    }
    if(action==='messages'){allowThread(p.thread);var ms=recentMessages(function(m){return m.thread===p.thread&&(!p.before||m.at<p.before);},61);return {messages:ms.slice(-60),hasMore:ms.length>60};}
    if(action==='send'){allowThread(p.thread);if(u.mutedUntil>now)fail('You are muted until '+new Date(u.mutedUntil).toLocaleString()+'.');var body=str(p.text,1800,1);if(now-u.lastMessage<1800)fail('A little slower, monke.');var m={id:ctx.id(),thread:p.thread,author:u.id,text:body,at:now,reactions:{},deleted:false};db.put('Messages',m);u.lastMessage=now;var gain=0;if(['general','gaming','off-topic'].indexOf(p.thread)>=0&&body.length>=8&&now-u.lastReward>=60000&&body!==u.lastRewardText){u.lastReward=now;u.lastRewardText=body;gain=earn('chat',2,60);}saveUser();return {message:m,earned:gain,me:me(u)};}
    if(action==='react'){var msg=db.get('Messages',p.id);if(!msg||msg.deleted)fail('Message unavailable.');allowThread(msg.thread);if(['🍌','🔥','💚','😂'].indexOf(p.emoji)<0)fail('Unknown reaction.');var ids=msg.reactions[p.emoji]||[];var ix=ids.indexOf(u.id);if(ix>=0)ids.splice(ix,1);else ids.push(u.id);msg.reactions[p.emoji]=ids;db.put('Messages',msg);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='deleteMessage'){var mm=db.get('Messages',p.id);if(!mm)fail('Message unavailable.');allowThread(mm.thread);if(mm.author!==u.id&&(!role(c,u).moderate||['general','gaming','off-topic'].indexOf(mm.thread)<0))fail('You cannot remove that message.');mm.deleted=true;mm.text='';mm.reactions={};db.put('Messages',mm);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='dmCreate'){var ids=Array.from(new Set([u.id].concat(p.members||[])));if(ids.length<2||ids.length>12)fail('Choose 1–11 other members.');ids.forEach(function(id){var t=db.get('Users',id);if(!t||t.role==='pending'||t.banned)fail('That member cannot be added.');});if(ids.length===2){var old=db.all('Threads').find(function(t){return !t.group&&t.members.length===2&&ids.every(function(id){return t.members.indexOf(id)>=0;});});if(old)return {thread:old};}var thread={id:ctx.id(),name:ids.length>2?str(p.name,40,2):'',group:ids.length>2,members:ids,creator:u.id,at:now};db.put('Threads',thread);return {thread:thread};}
    if(action==='dmAdd'){var th=allowThread(p.thread);if(th===true||th.creator!==u.id||!th.group)fail('Only the group creator can add members.');var t=target();if(t.role==='pending'||t.banned||th.members.length>=12)fail('This member cannot be added.');if(th.members.indexOf(t.id)<0)th.members.push(t.id);db.put('Threads',th);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='dmLeave'){var th2=allowThread(p.thread);if(th2===true||!th2.group)fail('Only groups can be left.');th2.members=th2.members.filter(function(id){return id!==u.id;});if(th2.creator===u.id)th2.creator=th2.members[0]||'';db.put('Threads',th2);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='buy'){if(typeof TribeSocial!=='undefined'&&TribeSocial.heldItem(db,u.id,p.id))fail('This cosmetic is reserved in an open trade.');var item=getItem(p.id);if(!item.active)fail('That item is not for sale.');if(u.inventory.indexOf(item.id)>=0)fail('You already own this item.');if(u.points<item.price)fail('You need '+(item.price-u.points)+' more points.');u.points-=item.price;u.inventory.push(item.id);saveUser();return {me:me(u)};}
    if(action==='equip'){if(['banner','color','font','frame','badge'].indexOf(p.type)<0)fail('Unknown cosmetic type.');if(p.id){var it=getItem(p.id);if(it.type!==p.type||u.inventory.indexOf(p.id)<0)fail('You do not own this item.');}u.equipped[p.type]=p.id||'';saveUser();return {me:me(u)};}
    if(action==='daily'){
      var progress=dailyStatus(u,now);
      if(progress.claimed)fail('Come back tomorrow for your next reward.');
      // Both the day and amount are calculated from the stored account, never the request.
      var amount=progress.reward;
      if(!Number.isSafeInteger(u.points+amount)||!Number.isSafeInteger(progress.total+amount))fail('This balance needs an owner review before more points can be added.');
      u.daily=progress.date;u.dailyClaims=progress.completed+1;u.dailyTotal=progress.total+amount;u.points+=amount;
      saveUser();return {earned:amount,day:u.dailyClaims,me:me(u),serverTime:now};
    }
    if(action==='externalStart'){var g=db.get('Games',p.id);if(!g||g.active===false||g.kind==='quiz')fail('External game unavailable.');u.external={id:g.id,at:now,nonce:ctx.id()};saveUser();return {visit:u.external};}
    if(action==='externalClaim'){var ex=u.external;if(!ex||p.nonce!==ex.nonce||now-ex.at<120000||now-ex.at>7200000)fail('Spend at least 2 minutes at a game before claiming.');u.external=null;return {earned:earn('external',5,20),me:me(u)};}
    requireAdmin();
    if(action==='adminData')return {users:db.all('Users').map(function(t){return Object.assign(pub(t),{mutedUntil:t.mutedUntil,deleting:!!t.deleting});}),audit:db.all('Audit').slice(-80).reverse(),inviteCode:c.inviteCode,registration:c.registration};
    if(action==='assignRole'){var t=target();if(t.role==='owner'||t.id===u.id)fail('The owner and your own role are protected.');var rr=c.roles.find(function(r){return r.id===p.role;});if(!rr||p.role==='owner')fail('Choose an assignable role.');if(u.role!=='owner'&&(t.role==='coowner'||p.role==='coowner'))fail('Only the owner can manage co-owners.');t.role=p.role;db.put('Users',t);audit('Role changed',t.username+' → '+rr.name);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='points'){var t=target();if(protect(t))fail('Owner and co-owner balances cannot be edited.');var n=Number(p.amount);if(!Number.isInteger(n)||Math.abs(n)>100000||n===0||t.points+n<0)fail('Enter a whole number that leaves a nonnegative balance.');var reason=str(p.reason,160,3);t.points+=n;db.put('Users',t);audit('Points',t.username+': '+(n>0?'+':'')+n+' · '+reason);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='moderate'){var t=target();if(protect(t)||t.id===u.id)fail('This account is protected.');if(p.kind==='ban')t.banned=!!p.value;else if(p.kind==='mute')t.mutedUntil=p.value?now+3600000:0;else fail('Unknown moderation action.');db.put('Users',t);audit('Moderation',t.username+' · '+p.kind+' '+!!p.value);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='roleSave'){if(c.roles.length>=20)fail('Role limit reached.');var rn=str(p.name,24,2),rid='role-'+ctx.id();c.roles.push({id:rid,name:rn,color:hex(p.color),games:!!p.games,moderate:!!p.moderate});db.put('Config',c);audit('Role created',rn);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='contentSave'){if(['rules','upcoming','updates','credits'].indexOf(p.key)<0)fail('Unknown page.');c.content[p.key]=str(p.text,6000);db.put('Config',c);audit('Page edited',p.key);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='themeSave'){var v=p.theme||{};['accent','secondary','bg','surface','text','muted'].forEach(function(k){c.theme[k]=hex(v[k]);});c.theme.radius=Math.max(0,Math.min(28,Number(v.radius)||0));c.theme.sidebar=Math.max(200,Math.min(290,Number(v.sidebar)||232));if(['sans','mono','serif','rounded'].indexOf(v.font)<0)fail('Unknown font.');c.theme.font=v.font;c.title=str(p.title,32,3);c.tagline=str(p.tagline,100);if(['particles','grid','aurora','rain','still'].indexOf(p.wallpaper)<0)fail('Unknown wallpaper.');c.wallpaper=p.wallpaper;if(p.logo!==undefined)c.logo=p.logo?image(p.logo):'';db.put('Config',c);audit('Theme updated',c.title);return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='settingsSave'){c.registration=!!p.registration;c.inviteCode=str(p.inviteCode,64);db.put('Config',c);audit('Signup settings','Updated');return {ok:true};}
    if(action==='shopSave'){var item={id:p.id||ctx.id(),name:str(p.name,40,2),description:str(p.description,160),price:Number(p.price),type:p.type,active:p.active!==false,animated:!!p.animated,value:str(p.value,30000,1)};if(!Number.isInteger(item.price)||item.price<0||item.price>100000)fail('Choose a valid price.');if(['banner','color','font','frame','avatar','badge'].indexOf(item.type)<0)fail('Unknown item type.');if(item.type==='banner'&&!presets[item.value])item.value=image(item.value);if(item.type==='color')hex(item.value);if(item.type==='font'&&['sans','mono','serif','rounded'].indexOf(item.value)<0)fail('Font must be sans, mono, serif, or rounded.');if(item.type==='frame'&&['orbit','glow','square'].indexOf(item.value)<0)fail('Frame must be orbit, glow, or square.');if(item.type==='avatar')item.value='upload';if(item.type==='badge')item.value=str(item.value,8,1);if(p.id){var old=getItem(p.id);if(old.type!==item.type)fail('An existing item cannot change type.');}db.put('Shop',item);audit('Shop updated',item.name);return {ok:true};}
    fail('Unknown action.');
  }
  return {dailyReward:dailyReward,dailyStatus:dailyStatus,config:config,catalog:catalog,run:run,user:user,pub:pub,me:me,role:role,admin:admin,presets:presets};
})();

/* v1.4 points economy. Live writes run under the Apps Script lock.
   Each balance mutation and its receipt are persisted in ONE user record. */
var TribeExpansion = (function () {
  var actions=['announcementSave','marketData','stockSave','stockTrade','betCreate','betResolve','betPlace','betClaim','randomBet','slotSpin','gameSave','gameEditorData','quizStart','quizFinish','gameStart','gameFinish'];
  var symbols=['7','BAR','♥','♦','♠','🍌'];
  function fail(message){var e=new Error(message);e.name='TribeError';throw e;}
  function text(value,max,min){var s=String(value==null?'':value).trim();if(s.length>max||s.length<(min||0))fail('Please check the length of your entry.');return s;}
  function integer(value,min,max){var n=Number(value);if(!Number.isSafeInteger(n)||n<min||n>max)fail('Enter a whole number from '+min+' to '+max+'.');return n;}
  function member(u){if(!u||u.banned||u.role==='pending')fail('Log in with an approved account to join in.');}
  function owner(u){member(u);if(u.role!=='owner')fail('Only the OWNER can do that.');}
  function gamePublic(g){var out={};['id','name','description','url','category','color','emoji','active','kind','reward','revision'].forEach(function(k){if(g[k]!==undefined)out[k]=g[k];});out.questionCount=(g.questions||[]).length;return out;}
  function run(ctx,action,p){
    p=p||{};var db=ctx.db,u=ctx.user,now=ctx.now||Date.now(),date=new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0,10);
    function save(){db.put('Users',u);}
    function adjust(amount){if(!Number.isSafeInteger(u.points)||!Number.isSafeInteger(u.points+amount)||u.points+amount<0||u.points+amount>1000000000)fail('Not enough points, or the balance limit would be exceeded.');u.points+=amount;}
    function audit(kind,detail){db.put('Audit',{id:ctx.id(),actor:u.id,kind:kind,detail:detail,at:now});}
    function transaction(signature,fn){
      var requestId=text(p.requestId,70,19);
      if(!new RegExp('^'+date+':[a-zA-Z0-9-]{8,50}$').test(requestId))fail('This request expired. Refresh and try again.');
      var ledger=u.economyReceipts&&u.economyReceipts.date===date?u.economyReceipts:{date:date,items:[]};
      var prior=ledger.items.find(function(r){return r.id===requestId;});
      if(prior){if(prior.signature!==signature)fail('That request was already used for another action.');return Object.assign({},prior.result,{me:Tribe.me(u),replayed:true});}
      if(ledger.items.length>=60)fail('You reached today’s 60 spins, trades, and bets. Come back tomorrow.');
      var result=fn();
      ledger.items.push({id:requestId,signature:signature,result:result});u.economyReceipts=ledger;save();
      return Object.assign({},result,{me:Tribe.me(u)});
    }
    function market(){
      var m=db.get('Config','market-v140');
      if(!m)m={id:'market-v140',stocks:[['BAN','Banana Co.',20,'🍌'],['VINE','Vine Network',35,'🌿'],['COCO','Coconut Labs',12,'🥥']].map(function(s){return {id:s[0],symbol:s[0],name:s[1],price:s[2],emoji:s[3],mode:'random',revision:1,at:now,history:[{at:now,price:s[2]}],active:true};})};
      var changed=!db.get('Config','market-v140');
      m.stocks.forEach(function(s){if(s.mode==='random'&&s.active&&now-s.at>=900000){s.price=Math.max(1,Math.min(100000,Math.round(s.price*(.90+ctx.random()*.20))));s.at=now;s.revision++;s.history.push({at:now,price:s.price});s.history=s.history.slice(-24);changed=true;}});
      if(changed)db.put('Config',m);return m;
    }
    function betRecord(){var b=db.get('Config',text(p.id,90,3));if(!b||b.type!=='community-bet')fail('This bet is unavailable.');return b;}
    if(action==='gameStart'||action==='gameFinish')fail('The old minigames were retired. Open Minigames or Games.');
    if(action==='announcementSave'){
      member(u);if(!Tribe.admin(u))fail('Only the owner and co-owner can publish announcements.');
      var c=db.get('Config','main')||Tribe.config();
      c.announcement=p.active===false?null:{title:text(p.title,90,2),body:text(p.body,1200,2),author:u.displayName,at:now};
      db.put('Config',c);audit('Announcement',c.announcement?'Published: '+c.announcement.title:'Announcement removed');return {announcement:c.announcement};
    }
    member(u);
    if(action==='marketData'){
      var m=market(),positions=u.betPositions||{},all=db.all('Config').filter(function(b){return b.type==='community-bet';});
      var bets=all.filter(function(b){return b.status==='open'||positions[b.id];}).concat(all.filter(function(b){return b.status!=='open'&&!positions[b.id];}).sort(function(a,b){return b.at-a.at;}).slice(0,12));
      return {stocks:m.stocks,bets:bets,holdings:u.holdings||{},positions:positions,me:Tribe.me(u),serverTime:now};
    }
    if(action==='stockSave'){
      owner(u);var m=market(),symbol=text(p.symbol,6,1).toUpperCase();if(!/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9]{0,5}$/.test(symbol))fail('Use 1–6 letters or numbers for the ticker.');
      var s=m.stocks.find(function(x){return x.id===symbol;});if(!s&&m.stocks.length>=20)fail('The market holds up to 20 stocks. Edit an existing one.');
      var price=integer(p.price,1,100000),name=text(p.name,40,2),mode=p.mode;if(['random','owner'].indexOf(mode)<0)fail('Choose automatic or owner pricing.');
      s=s||{id:symbol,symbol:symbol,history:[],revision:0};s.name=name;s.price=price;s.emoji=text(p.emoji,6)||'📈';s.mode=mode;s.active=p.active!==false;s.at=now;s.revision++;
      s.history.push({at:now,price:price});s.history=s.history.slice(-24);if(!m.stocks.some(function(x){return x.id===symbol;}))m.stocks.push(s);
      db.put('Config',m);audit('Stock updated',symbol+' · '+price+' points · '+mode);return {ok:true};
    }
    if(action==='stockTrade'){
      var qty=integer(p.quantity,1,10000),side=text(p.side,4),stockId=text(p.id,6,1);if(['buy','sell'].indexOf(side)<0)fail('Choose buy or sell.');
      return transaction('stock:'+stockId+':'+side+':'+qty+':'+p.revision+':'+p.price,function(){
        var s=market().stocks.find(function(x){return x.id===stockId;});if(!s)fail('Stock unavailable.');
        if(s.revision!==Number(p.revision)||s.price!==Number(p.price))fail('The price changed. Refresh the market and review your order again.');
        if(!s.active&&side==='buy')fail('Buying this stock is paused. Existing shares can still be sold.');
        var holdings=u.holdings||{},h=holdings[s.id]||{quantity:0,cost:0},total=qty*s.price;
        if(side==='buy'){if(h.quantity+qty>10000)fail('You can hold up to 10,000 shares of one stock.');adjust(-total);h.quantity+=qty;h.cost+=total;}
        else {if(h.quantity<qty)fail('You do not own that many shares.');adjust(total);h.cost=Math.round(h.cost*(h.quantity-qty)/h.quantity);h.quantity-=qty;}
        if(h.quantity)holdings[s.id]=h;else delete holdings[s.id];u.holdings=holdings;
        return {kind:'trade',side:side,symbol:s.symbol,quantity:qty,total:total};
      });
    }
    if(action==='slotSpin'){
      var stake=integer(p.stake,1,100);
      return transaction('slot:'+stake,function(){
        if(now-(u.lastSlotAt||0)<2000)fail('Wait for the current spin to finish.');
        var reels=[0,0,0].map(function(){return Math.floor(ctx.random()*6);}),triple=reels[0]===reels[1]&&reels[1]===reels[2];
        var multiplier=triple?(reels[0]===0?25:10):(reels[0]===reels[1]||reels[1]===reels[2]||reels[0]===reels[2]?1:0),payout=stake*multiplier;
        if(u.points<stake)fail('You need '+stake+' points to spin.');adjust(payout-stake);u.lastSlotAt=now;
        return {kind:'slot',reels:reels,stake:stake,payout:payout,multiplier:multiplier};
      });
    }
    if(action==='randomBet'){
      var stake=integer(p.stake,1,100),choice=text(p.choice,8);if(['sun','moon'].indexOf(choice)<0)fail('Choose sun or moon.');
      return transaction('flip:'+stake+':'+choice,function(){
        if(u.points<stake)fail('Not enough points for that bet.');if(now-(u.lastFlipAt||0)<2000)fail('Wait a moment before flipping again.');
        var result=ctx.random()<.5?'sun':'moon',payout=result===choice?Math.floor(stake*1.9):0;adjust(payout-stake);u.lastFlipAt=now;
        return {kind:'flip',outcome:result,stake:stake,payout:payout};
      });
    }
    if(action==='betCreate'){
      owner(u);if(db.all('Config').filter(function(b){return b.type==='community-bet'&&b.status==='open';}).length>=12)fail('Close or cancel an existing bet first (12 open at a time).');
      var closeAt=Number(p.closeAt);if(!Number.isFinite(closeAt)||closeAt<now+60000||closeAt>now+30*86400000)fail('Choose a closing time between 1 minute and 30 days from now.');
      var a=text(p.optionA,30,1),b=text(p.optionB,30,1);if(a.toLowerCase()===b.toLowerCase())fail('Use two different choices.');
      var bet={id:'bet-'+ctx.id(),type:'community-bet',title:text(p.title,100,3),description:text(p.description,350),options:[a,b],multiplier:integer(p.multiplier,1,10),maxStake:integer(p.maxStake,1,1000),closeAt:closeAt,status:'open',at:now,author:u.displayName};
      db.put('Config',bet);audit('Bet created',bet.title);return {bet:bet};
    }
    if(action==='betResolve'){
      owner(u);var b=betRecord();if(b.status!=='open')fail('That bet has already been settled.');
      if(p.cancel){b.status='cancelled';b.result=null;}else{if(now<b.closeAt)fail('Wait until betting closes before choosing the winner.');b.result=integer(p.result,0,1);b.status='settled';}
      b.settledAt=now;db.put('Config',b);audit('Bet '+b.status,b.title);return {bet:b};
    }
    if(action==='betPlace'){
      var stake=integer(p.stake,1,1000),choice=integer(p.choice,0,1),id=text(p.id,90,3);
      return transaction('bet:'+id+':'+stake+':'+choice,function(){
        var b=betRecord();if(b.status!=='open'||now>=b.closeAt)fail('Betting has closed.');if(stake>b.maxStake)fail('Maximum stake: '+b.maxStake+' points.');
        var positions=u.betPositions||{};if(positions[b.id])fail('You have already placed a bet on this event.');
        if(Object.keys(positions).length>=24)fail('Claim your settled bets before joining more events.');
        adjust(-stake);positions[b.id]={choice:choice,stake:stake,at:now};u.betPositions=positions;return {kind:'bet',id:b.id,stake:stake};
      });
    }
    if(action==='betClaim'){
      var b=betRecord(),position=(u.betPositions||{})[b.id];if(!position)fail('There is no unclaimed bet here.');if(position.claimed)return {payout:position.payout,me:Tribe.me(u),replayed:true};
      if(b.status==='open')fail('The owner has not settled this event yet.');
      var payout=b.status==='cancelled'?position.stake:position.choice===b.result?position.stake*b.multiplier:0;
      adjust(payout);position.claimed=true;position.payout=payout;
      // Keep a bounded history; a settled event can never accept another stake.
      var keys=Object.keys(u.betPositions).filter(function(id){return u.betPositions[id].claimed;});while(keys.length>12)delete u.betPositions[keys.shift()];save();return {payout:payout,me:Tribe.me(u)};
    }
    if(action==='gameEditorData'){owner(u);var g=db.get('Games',p.id);if(!g)fail('Game unavailable.');return {game:g};}
    if(action==='gameSave'){
      owner(u);var old=p.id?db.get('Games',p.id):null;if(p.id&&!old)fail('Game unavailable.');if(!old&&db.all('Games').length>=60)fail('Edit an existing game (60 game limit).');
      var kind=p.kind||'link';if(['link','quiz'].indexOf(kind)<0)fail('Choose a link or a quiz game.');
      var g={id:old?old.id:ctx.id(),kind:kind,name:text(p.name,40,2),description:text(p.description,160),category:text(p.category,24)||'Community',emoji:text(p.emoji,6)||'🎮',color:p.color||'#b9f451',active:p.active!==false,addedBy:u.id,revision:(old&&old.revision||0)+1};
      if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(g.color))fail('Choose a valid color.');
      if(kind==='link'){g.url=text(p.url,2048,8);if(!/^https:\/\/[^\s/@]+(?:[^\s]*)$/i.test(g.url))fail('Use a complete HTTPS game link.');}
      else {g.reward=integer(p.reward,0,50);if(!Array.isArray(p.questions)||p.questions.length<1||p.questions.length>5)fail('Add 1–5 questions.');g.questions=p.questions.map(function(q){if(!Array.isArray(q.options)||q.options.length!==3)fail('Each question needs three answers.');var options=q.options.map(function(o){return text(o,100,1);});if(new Set(options.map(function(o){return o.toLowerCase();})).size!==3)fail('Use three different answers.');return {text:text(q.text,200,3),options:options,answer:integer(q.answer,0,2)};});}
      db.put('Games',g);audit('Game saved',g.name+' · '+kind);return {ok:true};
    }
    if(action==='quizStart'){
      var g=db.get('Games',p.id);if(!g||g.kind!=='quiz'||!g.active)fail('Quiz unavailable.');
      var rewards=u.quizRewards&&u.quizRewards.date===date?u.quizRewards:{date:date,total:0,ids:[]};
      if(rewards.ids.indexOf(g.id)>=0)fail('You have already played this quiz today. Come back tomorrow.');
      if(u.quizRun&&now-u.quizRun.at<3000)fail('Finish the current quiz before starting another.');
      u.quizRun={id:ctx.id(),game:g.id,at:now,reward:g.reward,questions:g.questions};save();
      return {runId:u.quizRun.id,name:g.name,reward:Math.min(g.reward,100-rewards.total),questions:g.questions.map(function(q){return {text:q.text,options:q.options};})};
    }
    if(action==='quizFinish'){
      if(u.quizLast&&u.quizLast.runId===p.runId)return Object.assign({},u.quizLast.result,{me:Tribe.me(u),replayed:true});
      var r=u.quizRun;if(!r||r.id!==p.runId||now-r.at>1800000)fail('That quiz has ended or expired.');
      if(!Array.isArray(p.answers)||p.answers.length!==r.questions.length)fail('Answer every question.');
      var answers=p.answers.map(function(a){return integer(a,0,2);}),rewards=u.quizRewards&&u.quizRewards.date===date?u.quizRewards:{date:date,total:0,ids:[]};
      if(rewards.ids.indexOf(r.game)>=0)fail('You already played this quiz today.');
      var correct=answers.filter(function(a,i){return a===r.questions[i].answer;}).length,gain=Math.min(Math.floor(correct/r.questions.length*r.reward),100-rewards.total);
      adjust(gain);rewards.total+=gain;rewards.ids.push(r.game);u.quizRewards=rewards;u.quizRun=null;u.quizLast={runId:r.id,result:{correct:correct,total:answers.length,earned:gain}};save();return Object.assign({},u.quizLast.result,{me:Tribe.me(u)});
    }
    fail('Unknown community action.');
  }
  return {handles:function(a){return actions.indexOf(a)>=0;},run:run,gamePublic:gamePublic,symbols:symbols};
})();

/* Original Monke Arena rules, shared by both browsers and the settlement server.
   Only bounded input frames are replayed; clients never submit damage or a winner. */
function createDuelCore_(){
 'use strict';
 var alphabet='ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_';
 var map=['111111111111111','100000000000001','100220000022001','100000000000001','100001000100001','100001000100001','100000000000001','100200020002001','100000000000001','100001000100001','100001000100001','100000000000001','100220000022001','100000000000001','111111111111111'];
 var guns=[{name:'Jungle Carbine',short:'CARBINE',damage:18,interval:3,ammo:24,reload:26,spread:[0],range:18,color:'#b9f451'},{name:'Coconut Scatter',short:'SCATTER',damage:11,interval:12,ammo:6,reload:34,spread:[-.12,-.06,0,.06,.12],range:8,color:'#ffb36b'},{name:'Vine Marksman',short:'MARKSMAN',damage:60,interval:16,ammo:4,reload:42,spread:[0],range:24,color:'#adadff'}];
 var tickMs=50,maxFrames=1800;
 function encode(v){v=v>>>0;return alphabet[v&63]+alphabet[(v>>>6)&63]+alphabet[(v>>>12)&63]+alphabet[(v>>>18)&63];}
 function decode(s){if(typeof s!=='string'||s.length!==4||!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{4}$/.test(s))throw Error('Invalid input frame.');var n=0;for(var i=0;i<4;i++)n|=alphabet.indexOf(s[i])<<(i*6);if(n>=(1<<21))throw Error('Invalid input flags.');return n;}
 function input(keys,yaw,weapon){var bits=0;if(keys.forward)bits|=1;if(keys.back)bits|=2;if(keys.left)bits|=4;if(keys.right)bits|=8;if(keys.fire)bits|=16;if(keys.reload)bits|=32;if(keys.sprint)bits|=64;bits|=(Math.max(0,Math.min(2,weapon|0))<<7);bits|=((Math.round((((yaw%(Math.PI*2))+Math.PI*2)%(Math.PI*2))*4096/(Math.PI*2))&4095)<<9);return encode(bits);}
 function wall(x,y){var xx=Math.floor(x),yy=Math.floor(y);return !map[yy]||!map[yy][xx]||map[yy][xx]!=='0';}
 function quant(n){return Math.round(n*100000)/100000;}
 function trig(a){return [quant(Math.cos(a)),quant(Math.sin(a))];}
 function spawn(i,round){var side=(i+round)%2;return {x:side?12.5:2.5,y:7.5,yaw:side?Math.PI:0,hp:100,weapon:0,ammo:[24,6,4],cooldown:0,reloading:0,reloadWeapon:0,flash:0};}
 function initial(){return {version:1,frame:0,round:0,wins:[0,0],pause:0,players:[spawn(0,0),spawn(1,0)],done:false,winner:null,events:[]};}
 function clear(x,y){var r=.22;return !wall(x-r,y-r)&&!wall(x+r,y-r)&&!wall(x-r,y+r)&&!wall(x+r,y+r);}
 function ray(x,y,a,max){var t=trig(a);for(var d=0;d<max;d+=.025){if(wall(x+t[0]*d,y+t[1]*d))return d;}return max;}
 function hit(p,target,angle,range){var t=trig(angle),dx=target.x-p.x,dy=target.y-p.y,along=dx*t[0]+dy*t[1],across=Math.abs(dx*t[1]-dy*t[0]);return along>0&&along<=range&&across<.28&&ray(p.x,p.y,angle,along)>=along-.03;}
 function finish(s){s.done=true;s.winner=s.wins[0]!==s.wins[1]?(s.wins[0]>s.wins[1]?0:1):s.players[0].hp!==s.players[1].hp?(s.players[0].hp>s.players[1].hp?0:1):null;}
 function step(s,a,b){
  if(s.done)return s;var inputs=[decode(a),decode(b)];s.frame++;s.events=[];
  if(s.pause){s.pause--;if(!s.pause){s.round++;s.players=[spawn(0,s.round),spawn(1,s.round)];}if(s.frame>=maxFrames)finish(s);return s;}
  s.players.forEach(function(p,i){var n=inputs[i];p.yaw=((n>>>9)&4095)*Math.PI*2/4096;p.weapon=Math.min(2,(n>>>7)&3);p.flash=Math.max(0,p.flash-1);p.cooldown=Math.max(0,p.cooldown-1);
   if(p.reloading){p.reloading--;if(!p.reloading)p.ammo[p.reloadWeapon]=guns[p.reloadWeapon].ammo;}
   var forward=(n&1?1:0)-(n&2?1:0),side=(n&8?1:0)-(n&4?1:0),len=Math.hypot(forward,side)||1,t=trig(p.yaw),speed=(n&64?4.2:3.1)*.05/len;
   var dx=(t[0]*forward-t[1]*side)*speed,dy=(t[1]*forward+t[0]*side)*speed;
   if(clear(p.x+dx,p.y))p.x=quant(p.x+dx);if(clear(p.x,p.y+dy))p.y=quant(p.y+dy);
   if(((n&32)||!p.ammo[p.weapon])&&!p.reloading&&p.ammo[p.weapon]<guns[p.weapon].ammo){p.reloading=guns[p.weapon].reload;p.reloadWeapon=p.weapon;}
  });
  var damage=[0,0];s.players.forEach(function(p,i){var n=inputs[i],g=guns[p.weapon];if((n&16)&&!p.cooldown&&!p.reloading&&p.ammo[p.weapon]>0){p.ammo[p.weapon]--;p.cooldown=g.interval;p.flash=2;s.events.push({type:'shot',player:i});g.spread.forEach(function(offset){if(hit(p,s.players[1-i],p.yaw+offset,g.range))damage[1-i]+=g.damage;});}});
  s.players.forEach(function(p,i){p.hp=Math.max(0,p.hp-damage[i]);if(damage[i])s.events.push({type:'hit',player:i,damage:damage[i]});});
  if(s.players.some(function(p){return p.hp<=0;})){if(s.players[0].hp>0)s.wins[0]++;if(s.players[1].hp>0)s.wins[1]++;if(Math.max(s.wins[0],s.wins[1])>=3)finish(s);else s.pause=35;}
  if(s.frame>=maxFrames)finish(s);return s;
 }
 function replay(log){if(typeof log!=='string'||log.length%8||log.length>maxFrames*8||!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]*$/.test(log))throw Error('Invalid match record.');var s=initial();for(var f=0;f<log.length&&!s.done;f+=8)step(s,log.slice(f,f+4),log.slice(f+4,f+8));return s;}
 return {map:map,guns:guns,tickMs:tickMs,maxFrames:maxFrames,initial:initial,step:step,replay:replay,input:input,decode:decode,encode:encode,wall:wall,ray:ray};
}
var DuelCore=createDuelCore_();

/* v1.5 governance, exchanges and wagers. All live mutations run under the GAS lock.
   Cross-record changes use a durable redo journal: finish interrupted commits before
   any subsequent mutation. Journal entries SET chosen fields, never replay a debit. */
var TribeSocial=(function(){
 'use strict';
 var actions=['socialState','tradeCreate','tradeAccept','tradeCancel','approvalList','approvalGet','approvalDecide','accountDelete','duelCreate','duelAccept','duelCancel','duelSignal','duelReady','duelSync','duelForfeit'];
 var approvalKeys={themeSave:['theme','title','tagline','wallpaper','logo'],contentSave:['key','text'],settingsSave:['registration','inviteCode'],roleSave:['name','color','games','moderate'],shopSave:['id','name','description','price','type','active','animated','value'],announcementSave:['active','title','body'],assignRole:['userId','role'],points:['userId','amount','reason'],moderate:['userId','kind','value'],deleteMessage:['id']};
 var labels={themeSave:'Change site appearance',contentSave:'Edit a community page',settingsSave:'Change signup settings',roleSave:'Create a role',shopSave:'Change a shop item',announcementSave:'Publish or remove announcement',assignRole:'Assign a member role',points:'Change member points',moderate:'Moderate a member',deleteMessage:'Remove a community message'};
 function clone(v){return v==null?v:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));}
 function fail(s){var e=new Error(s);e.name='TribeError';throw e;}
 function member(u){if(!u||u.banned||u.deleting||u.role==='pending')fail('Log in with an approved account.');}
 function owner(u){member(u);if(u.role!=='owner')fail('Only the OWNER can do that.');}
 function text(v,max,min){v=String(v==null?'':v).trim();if(v.length>max||v.length<(min||0))fail('Please check your entry.');return v;}
 function integer(v,min,max){v=Number(v);if(!Number.isSafeInteger(v)||v<min||v>max)fail('Choose a whole number from '+min+' to '+max+'.');return v;}
 function flush(db){if(db.flush)db.flush();}
 function setWrite(table,id,fields){return {table:table,id:id,fields:clone(fields)};}
 function recordWrite(table,r){return setWrite(table,r.id,r);}
 function recover(ctx){
  var db=ctx.db;
  db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='social-tx'&&r.status==='pending';}).forEach(function(tx){
   (tx.parts||[]).forEach(function(id){var part=db.get('Config',id);if(!part||!part.write)fail('An interrupted transfer needs owner review.');var w=part.write;if(w.remove)db.remove(w.table,w.id);else{var r=clone(db.get(w.table,w.id))||{id:w.id};Object.keys(w.fields).forEach(function(k){r[k]=clone(w.fields[k]);});db.put(w.table,r);}flush(db);});
   tx.status='done';tx.doneAt=ctx.now||Date.now();db.put('Config',tx);flush(db);
   tx.parts.forEach(function(id){db.remove('Config',id);});tx.parts=[];db.put('Config',tx);flush(db);
  });
  var records=db.all('Config'),headers={};records.filter(function(r){return r.type==='social-tx';}).forEach(function(tx){headers[tx.id]=true;if(tx.status==='done'&&tx.parts&&tx.parts.length){tx.parts.forEach(function(id){db.remove('Config',id);});tx.parts=[];db.put('Config',tx);}});
  records.filter(function(r){return r.type==='social-tx-part'&&r.at<(ctx.now||Date.now())-86400000&&!headers[r.id.replace(/-p[0-9]+$/,'')];}).forEach(function(r){db.remove('Config',r.id);});
 }
 function commit(ctx,writes){
  if(!writes.length)return;var db=ctx.db,id='tx-'+ctx.id(),parts=[];
  writes.forEach(function(w,i){var part={id:id+'-p'+i,type:'social-tx-part',at:ctx.now,write:w};if(JSON.stringify(part).length>46000)fail('This change is too large. Use a smaller image.');parts.push(part);});
  parts.forEach(function(p){db.put('Config',p);});flush(db);
  db.put('Config',{id:id,type:'social-tx',status:'pending',at:ctx.now,parts:parts.map(function(p){return p.id;})});flush(db);recover(ctx);
  // Completed journal headers carry no personal data or credentials.
  var old=db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='social-tx'&&r.status==='done';}).sort(function(a,b){return b.at-a.at;});
  old.slice(60).forEach(function(r){db.remove('Config',r.id);});
 }
 function shadow(db){
  var cache={},changed={};function table(n){if(!cache[n]){cache[n]={};db.all(n).forEach(function(r){cache[n][r.id]=clone(r);});}return cache[n];}
  var s={all:function(n){return Object.keys(table(n)).map(function(id){return table(n)[id];}).filter(Boolean);},get:function(n,id){return table(n)[id]||null;},put:function(n,r){table(n)[r.id]=clone(r);changed[n+':'+r.id]={table:n,id:r.id};},remove:function(n,id){table(n)[id]=null;changed[n+':'+id]={table:n,id:id};}};
  s.writes=function(){return Object.keys(changed).map(function(k){var a=changed[k],before=db.get(a.table,a.id),after=s.get(a.table,a.id);if(!after)return {table:a.table,id:a.id,remove:true};var fields={};Object.keys(after).forEach(function(key){if(!before||JSON.stringify(before[key])!==JSON.stringify(after[key]))fields[key]=clone(after[key]);});return setWrite(a.table,a.id,fields);});};return s;
 }
 function preview(ctx,action,p){var sd=shadow(ctx.db);Tribe.run({db:sd,user:sd.get('Users',ctx.user.id),now:ctx.now,id:ctx.id,random:ctx.random,skipSocial:true},action,clone(p));return sd.writes();}
 function fingerprint(ctx,v){var s=JSON.stringify(v);if(ctx.hash)return ctx.hash(s);var a=2166136261,b=5381;for(var i=0;i<s.length;i++){a=Math.imul(a^s.charCodeAt(i),16777619);b=Math.imul(b,33)^s.charCodeAt(i);}return (a>>>0).toString(16)+(b>>>0).toString(16)+':'+s.length;}
 function baseline(ctx,action,p){var db=ctx.db,c=db.get('Config','main'),u=p.userId?db.get('Users',p.userId):null;switch(action){case 'themeSave':return [c.theme,c.title,c.tagline,c.logo,c.wallpaper];case 'contentSave':return c.content[p.key];case 'settingsSave':return [c.registration,c.inviteCode];case 'roleSave':return c.roles;case 'announcementSave':return c.announcement||null;case 'shopSave':return p.id?db.get('Shop',p.id):null;case 'assignRole':return [u&&u.role,c.roles];case 'points':return u?[u.role,u.points]:null;case 'moderate':return u?[u.role,u.banned,u.mutedUntil]:null;case 'deleteMessage':var m=db.get('Messages',p.id);return m?[m.author,m.thread,m.text,m.deleted]:null;}return null;}
 function needsApproval(ctx,action,p){if(!ctx.user||ctx.user.role!=='coowner'||!approvalKeys[action])return false;if(action==='deleteMessage'){var m=ctx.db.get('Messages',p.id);return !!m&&m.author!==ctx.user.id;}return true;}
 function approvalNotice(ctx,r,message){var own=ctx.db.all('Users').find(function(u){return u.role==='owner'&&!u.banned;});if(!own)fail('The owner account is unavailable.');var thread={id:'owner-approvals-'+own.id,name:'Owner approvals',system:true,group:false,members:[own.id],creator:own.id,at:r.at};var m={id:'notice-'+ctx.id(),thread:thread.id,author:'tribe-system',system:true,approvalId:r.id,text:message,at:ctx.now,reactions:{},deleted:false};return [recordWrite('Threads',thread),recordWrite('Messages',m)];}
 function queue(ctx,action,p){
  member(ctx.user);var data={};approvalKeys[action].forEach(function(k){if(p[k]!==undefined)data[k]=clone(p[k]);});preview(ctx,action,data);
  if(ctx.db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='owner-approval'&&r.actor===ctx.user.id&&r.status==='pending';}).length>=15)fail('Wait for the owner to review your existing requests.');
  var r={id:'approval-'+ctx.id(),type:'owner-approval',actor:ctx.user.id,actorName:ctx.user.displayName,action:action,label:labels[action],payload:data,baseline:fingerprint(ctx,baseline(ctx,action,data)),at:ctx.now,expires:ctx.now+86400000,status:'pending'};
  commit(ctx,[recordWrite('Config',r)].concat(approvalNotice(ctx,r,r.actorName+' requests approval: '+r.label+'. Review the proposed change before approving.')));
  return {approvalQueued:true,id:r.id,message:'Request sent to the OWNER in DMs. Nothing changes until it is approved.'};
 }
 function approvalPublic(r,full){var x=clone(r);delete x.baseline;if(!full)delete x.payload;return x;}
 function assets(db,id){var u=clone(db.get('Users',id));if(!u)fail('This account is no longer available.');return u;}
 function heldItem(db,userId,item){return db.all('Config').some(function(r){return r.type==='player-trade'&&r.status==='open'&&r.from===userId&&r.offer.items.indexOf(item)>=0;});}
 function hasItem(db,u,id){return (u.inventory||[]).indexOf(id)>=0||heldItem(db,u.id,id);}
 function changeAssets(u,points,remove,add,settlement){
  var n=u.points+points;if(!Number.isSafeInteger(n)||n<0||(!settlement&&n>1000000000))fail('Not enough points, or the balance limit would be exceeded.');
  var inv=(u.inventory||[]).slice(),equipped=clone(u.equipped||{});remove.forEach(function(id){var i=inv.indexOf(id);if(i<0)fail('A selected cosmetic is no longer owned.');inv.splice(i,1);Object.keys(equipped).forEach(function(k){if(equipped[k]===id)equipped[k]='';});});
  add.forEach(function(id){if(inv.indexOf(id)>=0)fail('A player already owns a cosmetic in this offer.');inv.push(id);});
  return setWrite('Users',u.id,{points:n,inventory:inv,equipped:equipped});
 }
 function bundle(db,p){p=p||{};var ids=p.items||[];if(!Array.isArray(ids)||ids.length>8||new Set(ids).size!==ids.length)fail('Choose up to 8 different cosmetics.');ids=ids.map(function(id){var item=db.get('Shop',text(id,100,1));if(!item||['banner','color','frame','font','badge'].indexOf(item.type)<0)fail('Only cosmetic items can be traded; upload permissions stay with the account.');return item.id;});return {points:integer(p.points||0,0,100000),items:ids};}
 function ownBundle(u,b){if(u.points<b.points||b.items.some(function(id){return (u.inventory||[]).indexOf(id)<0;}))fail('Not enough points or a selected cosmetic is no longer owned.');}
 function cancelTrade(ctx,r,reason){if(r.status!=='open')return r;var u=assets(ctx.db,r.from);r=clone(r);r.status=reason||'cancelled';r.closedAt=ctx.now;commit(ctx,[changeAssets(u,r.offer.points,[],r.offer.items,true),recordWrite('Config',r)]);return r;}
 function duelActive(r){return ['challenged','connecting','playing'].indexOf(r.status)>=0;}
 function settleDuel(ctx,r,winner,reason){
  if(!duelActive(r))return r;r=clone(r);var writes=[];r.status=winner==null?'refunded':'finished';r.winner=winner;r.reason=reason;r.closedAt=ctx.now;
  r.players.forEach(function(id,i){if(!r.funded[i])return;var u=assets(ctx.db,id),pay=winner==null?r.stake:winner===i?r.stake*(r.funded[0]+r.funded[1]):0;if(pay)writes.push(changeAssets(u,pay,[],[],true));});
  r.signals=[null,null];r.reports=['',''];writes.push(recordWrite('Config',r));commit(ctx,writes);return r;
 }
 function expires(ctx){ctx.db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-trade'&&r.status==='open'&&r.expires<=ctx.now;}).forEach(function(r){cancelTrade(ctx,r,'expired');});ctx.db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-duel'&&duelActive(r);}).forEach(function(r){if(r.status!=='playing'&&r.expires<=ctx.now)settleDuel(ctx,r,null,'Connection or invitation expired.');else if(r.status==='playing'&&ctx.now>r.startAt+180000)settleDuel(ctx,r,null,'Match verification timed out.');});}
 function duelPublic(r,u){var d=clone(r);delete d.reports;if(!u||d.players.indexOf(u.id)<0){delete d.signals;delete d.ready;delete d.lastPing;}return d;}
 function getDuel(ctx,id){var r=ctx.db.get('Config',text(id,170,1));if(!r||r.type!=='player-duel'||r.players.indexOf(ctx.user.id)<0)fail('This duel is private to its two players.');return clone(r);}
 function inDuel(db,id,except){return db.all('Config').some(function(r){return r.type==='player-duel'&&r.id!==except&&duelActive(r)&&r.players.indexOf(id)>=0&&r.funded[r.players.indexOf(id)];});}
 function run(ctx,action,p){
  var db=ctx.db,u=ctx.user,now=ctx.now;p=p||{};member(u);
  if(action==='approvalList'){if(!Tribe.admin(u))fail('Staff only.');return {requests:db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='owner-approval'&&(u.role==='owner'||r.actor===u.id);}).sort(function(a,b){return b.at-a.at;}).slice(0,50).map(function(r){return approvalPublic(r,false);})};}
  if(action==='approvalGet'||action==='approvalDecide'){
   var r=db.get('Config',text(p.id,160,1));if(!r||r.type!=='owner-approval'||(u.role!=='owner'&&r.actor!==u.id))fail('Approval request unavailable.');r=clone(r);if(action==='approvalGet')return {request:approvalPublic(r,true)};owner(u);
   if(r.status!=='pending')return {request:approvalPublic(r,false),alreadyDecided:true};
   if(p.decision!=='approve'&&p.decision!=='reject')fail('Choose approve or reject.');var writes=[];
   if(p.decision==='approve'){
    var actor=db.get('Users',r.actor);if(!actor||actor.role!=='coowner'||actor.banned||actor.deleting)fail('The requester no longer has co-owner access. Reject this request.');
    if(now>r.expires)fail('This request expired. Reject it and ask for a fresh request.');
    if(fingerprint(ctx,baseline(ctx,r.action,r.payload))!==r.baseline)fail('The affected data changed since this request. Reject it and request a fresh change.');
    writes=preview(Object.assign({},ctx,{user:actor}),r.action,r.payload);r.status='approved';
   }else r.status='rejected';
   r.reviewedBy=u.id;r.reviewedAt=now;writes.push(recordWrite('Config',r));commit(ctx,writes);return {request:approvalPublic(r,false)};
  }
  if(action==='accountDelete')return deleteAccount(ctx,p);
  expires(ctx);
  if(action==='socialState')return {trades:db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-trade'&&(r.from===u.id||r.to===u.id);}).sort(function(a,b){return b.at-a.at;}).slice(0,40),duels:db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-duel'&&r.players.indexOf(u.id)>=0;}).sort(function(a,b){return b.at-a.at;}).slice(0,30).map(function(r){return duelPublic(r,u);}),me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id)),serverTime:now};
  if(action==='tradeCreate'){
   var rid=text(p.requestId,80,8);if(!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(rid))fail('Invalid request ID.');var id='trade-'+u.id+'-'+rid,prior=db.get('Config',id);if(prior){if(prior.to!==p.to||JSON.stringify(prior.offer)!==JSON.stringify(bundle(db,p.offer))||JSON.stringify(prior.want)!==JSON.stringify(bundle(db,p.want)))fail('This request ID belongs to a different offer.');return {trade:prior,me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id))};}
   if(db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-trade'&&r.from===u.id&&r.at>now-86400000;}).length>=20)fail('You can create 20 offers per day.');
   var to=assets(db,text(p.to,100,1));member(to);if(to.id===u.id)fail('Choose another player.');var from=assets(db,u.id),offer=bundle(db,p.offer),want=bundle(db,p.want);ownBundle(from,offer);
   if(!offer.points&&!offer.items.length&&!want.points&&!want.items.length)fail('Add points or cosmetics to the offer.');
   if(offer.items.some(function(i){return hasItem(db,to,i);})||want.items.some(function(i){return hasItem(db,from,i);}))fail('A player already owns or has reserved one of these cosmetics.');
   if(db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-trade'&&r.from===u.id&&r.status==='open';}).length>=3)fail('You can have 3 outgoing offers open at once.');
   var r={id:id,type:'player-trade',from:u.id,to:to.id,offer:offer,want:want,at:now,expires:now+86400000,status:'open'};
   commit(ctx,[changeAssets(from,-offer.points,offer.items,[]),recordWrite('Config',r)]);return {trade:r,me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id))};
  }
  if(action==='tradeAccept'||action==='tradeCancel'){
   var r=db.get('Config',text(p.id,190,1));if(!r||r.type!=='player-trade'||[r.from,r.to].indexOf(u.id)<0)fail('This trade is private.');r=clone(r);
   if(r.status!=='open')return {trade:r,me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id))};
   if(action==='tradeCancel'){r=cancelTrade(ctx,r,u.id===r.to?'declined':'cancelled');return {trade:r,me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id))};}
   if(r.to!==u.id)fail('Only the recipient can accept this offer.');var from=assets(db,r.from),to=assets(db,r.to);member(from);member(to);ownBundle(to,r.want);
   if(r.offer.items.some(function(i){return hasItem(db,to,i);})||r.want.items.some(function(i){return hasItem(db,from,i);}))fail('An offered cosmetic is already owned or reserved. Cancel and make a fresh offer.');
   r.status='completed';r.closedAt=now;commit(ctx,[changeAssets(from,r.want.points,[],r.want.items),changeAssets(to,r.offer.points-r.want.points,r.want.items,r.offer.items),recordWrite('Config',r)]);return {trade:r,me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id))};
  }
  if(action==='duelCreate'){
   var rid=text(p.requestId,80,8);if(!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(rid))fail('Invalid request ID.');var id='duel-'+u.id+'-'+rid,prior=db.get('Config',id);if(prior){if(prior.players[1]!==p.to||prior.stake!==Number(p.stake))fail('This request ID belongs to a different challenge.');return {duel:duelPublic(prior,u),me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id)),serverTime:now};}
   if(db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-duel'&&r.players[0]===u.id&&r.at>now-86400000;}).length>=20)fail('You can send 20 challenges per day.');
   var other=assets(db,text(p.to,100,1));member(other);if(other.id===u.id)fail('Challenge another player.');if(inDuel(db,u.id)||inDuel(db,other.id))fail('One player already has a funded duel.');var stake=integer(p.stake,1,1000),from=assets(db,u.id);
   var r={id:id,type:'player-duel',players:[u.id,other.id],funded:[1,0],stake:stake,at:now,expires:now+300000,status:'challenged',rules:1,signals:[null,null],ready:[false,false],lastPing:[now,0],reports:['','']};
   commit(ctx,[changeAssets(from,-stake,[],[]),recordWrite('Config',r)]);return {duel:duelPublic(r,u),me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id)),serverTime:now};
  }
  if(action.indexOf('duel')===0){
   var r=getDuel(ctx,p.id),slot=r.players.indexOf(u.id);
   if(action==='duelAccept'){
    if(slot!==1)fail('Only the challenged player can accept.');if(r.status!=='challenged')return {duel:duelPublic(r,u),serverTime:now};
    member(assets(db,r.players[0]));if(inDuel(db,u.id,r.id))fail('You already have another funded duel.');r.funded[1]=1;r.status='connecting';r.expires=now+180000;r.lastPing[1]=now;
    commit(ctx,[changeAssets(assets(db,u.id),-r.stake,[],[]),recordWrite('Config',r)]);
   }else if(action==='duelCancel'){
    if(r.status==='playing')fail('Use Forfeit after the match starts.');r=settleDuel(ctx,r,null,'Invitation cancelled or connection stopped.');
   }else if(action==='duelForfeit'){
    if(r.status==='playing')r=settleDuel(ctx,r,1-slot,'Opponent forfeited.');else r=settleDuel(ctx,r,null,'Match cancelled before play.');
   }else if(action==='duelSignal'){
    if(r.status!=='connecting')fail('This duel is not accepting a connection.');var signal=p.signal;if(!signal||signal.type!==(slot===0?'offer':'answer')||typeof signal.sdp!=='string'||signal.sdp.length>18000)fail('Invalid peer connection.');
    if(r.signals[slot]&&JSON.stringify(r.signals[slot])!==JSON.stringify(signal))fail('The connection was already submitted. Cancel and challenge again.');r.signals[slot]={type:signal.type,sdp:signal.sdp};r.lastPing[slot]=now;db.put('Config',r);
   }else if(action==='duelReady'){
    if(r.status==='connecting'){r.ready[slot]=true;r.lastPing[slot]=now;if(r.ready.every(Boolean)){r.status='playing';r.startAt=now+5000;r.expires=r.startAt+180000;r.signals=[null,null];}db.put('Config',r);}
   }else if(action==='duelSync'){
    if(duelActive(r)){r.lastPing[slot]=now;
     if(r.status==='playing'&&p.log!==undefined){var log=String(p.log);try{for(var at=r.reports[slot].length;at<log.length;at+=4)DuelCore.decode(log.slice(at,at+4));}catch(e){fail('Invalid match input.');}if(log.length%8||log.length>DuelCore.maxFrames*8||!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]*$/.test(log)||log.indexOf(r.reports[slot])!==0)fail('Invalid or rewritten match record.');if(log.length/8>Math.max(0,Math.floor((now-r.startAt)/DuelCore.tickMs)+12))fail('The match record ran faster than real time.');r.reports[slot]=log;}
     db.put('Config',r);flush(db);
     if(r.status==='playing'){
      var a=r.reports[0],b=r.reports[1],len=Math.min(a.length,b.length),shared=a.slice(0,len);
      if(shared!==b.slice(0,len))r=settleDuel(ctx,r,null,'Match records differed. Stakes refunded.');
      else if(len){var result=DuelCore.replay(shared);if(result.done)r=settleDuel(ctx,r,result.winner,result.winner==null?'Draw. Stakes refunded.':'Verified arena result.');}
      if(r.status==='playing'&&now-r.lastPing[1-slot]>60000&&now-r.startAt>60000)r=settleDuel(ctx,r,slot,'Opponent disconnected for over 60 seconds.');
     }
    }
   }
   return {duel:duelPublic(r,u),me:Tribe.me(db.get('Users',u.id)),serverTime:now};
  }
  fail('Unknown social action.');
 }
 function deleteAccount(ctx,p){
  owner(ctx.user);var db=ctx.db,t=db.get('Users',text(p.userId,100,1));if(!t)return {deleted:true};if(t.role==='owner'||t.id===ctx.user.id)fail('The OWNER account cannot delete itself.');if(text(p.confirm,40,1)!==t.username)fail('Type the exact username to confirm deletion.');
  var id=t.id;t=clone(t);t.banned=true;t.deleting=true;db.put('Users',t);flush(db);
  db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-trade'&&r.status==='open'&&(r.from===id||r.to===id);}).forEach(function(r){cancelTrade(ctx,r,'account deleted');});
  db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='player-duel'&&duelActive(r)&&r.players.indexOf(id)>=0;}).forEach(function(r){settleDuel(ctx,r,null,'Account deleted. Stakes refunded.');});
  var relatedThreads=db.all('Threads').filter(function(th){return th.members.indexOf(id)>=0;});
  var writes=[];db.all('Sessions').filter(function(s){return s.user===id;}).forEach(function(s){writes.push({table:'Sessions',id:s.id,remove:true});});
  var messages=db.all('Messages').filter(function(m){return (m.author===id)||Object.keys(m.reactions||{}).some(function(k){return m.reactions[k].indexOf(id)>=0;});}).slice(0,40);
  messages.forEach(function(m){var fields={reactions:clone(m.reactions||{})};Object.keys(fields.reactions).forEach(function(k){fields.reactions[k]=fields.reactions[k].filter(function(x){return x!==id;});});if(m.author===id){fields.author='deleted-account';fields.text='';fields.deleted=true;}writes.push(setWrite('Messages',m.id,fields));});
  relatedThreads.slice(0,40).forEach(function(th){var members=th.members.filter(function(x){return x!==id;});if(!members.length)writes.push({table:'Threads',id:th.id,remove:true});else writes.push(setWrite('Threads',th.id,{members:members,creator:th.creator===id?members[0]:th.creator,name:!th.group&&members.length===1?'Deleted account':th.name}));});
  db.all('Config').filter(function(r){return r.type==='owner-approval'&&r.actor===id&&r.status==='pending';}).forEach(function(r){writes.push(setWrite('Config',r.id,{status:'cancelled',reviewedAt:ctx.now}));});
  var more=db.all('Messages').filter(function(m){return (m.author===id)||Object.keys(m.reactions||{}).some(function(k){return m.reactions[k].indexOf(id)>=0;});}).length>messages.length||relatedThreads.length>40;
  if(!more)writes.push({table:'Users',id:id,remove:true});commit(ctx,writes);return {deleted:!more,pending:more,userId:id};
 }
 return {handles:function(a){return actions.indexOf(a)>=0;},approvalActions:Object.keys(approvalKeys),needsApproval:needsApproval,queue:queue,run:run,recover:recover,heldItem:heldItem,commit:commit};
})();

/* Original skill arcade rules, shared by browser and Apps Script replay. */
function createArcadeCore_(){
 'use strict';
 var tiers=[{id:'easy',name:'Easy',reward:10},{id:'normal',name:'Normal',reward:20},{id:'hard',name:'Hard',reward:35},{id:'expert',name:'Expert',reward:60}];
 var games=[{id:'lock',name:'Lock Pop',tag:'TIMING',description:'Tap on the target. Reverse. Repeat. Pop every pin to open the lock.',color:'#c8f66b'},{id:'echo',name:'Color Echo',tag:'MEMORY',description:'Watch the colored pads, then repeat the pattern as it grows.',color:'#b8a2ff'},{id:'dodge',name:'Banana Dodge',tag:'REFLEXES',description:'Switch lanes and steer your banana through a shower of crates.',color:'#ffbf75'}];
 function rng(seed){var n=seed>>>0||1;return function(){n^=n<<13;n^=n>>>17;n^=n<<5;return (n>>>0)/4294967296;};}
 function challenge(game,difficulty,seed){var level=tiers.findIndex(function(t){return t.id===difficulty;});if(level<0||!games.some(function(g){return g.id===game;}))throw Error('Choose a game and difficulty.');var random=rng(seed),r={game:game,difficulty:difficulty,seed:seed,level:level,reward:tiers[level].reward,version:1};
  if(game==='lock'){r.goal=[10,16,23,30][level];r.speed=[1.65,1.95,2.4,2.85][level];r.tolerance=[.25,.21,.17,.135][level];r.gaps=Array.from({length:r.goal},function(){return 1.05+random()*1.7;});}
  if(game==='echo'){r.rounds=[3,4,5,6][level];r.base=[2,2,3,3][level];r.flash=[600,490,380,300][level];r.response=[10000,9000,8000,7000][level];r.sequence=Array.from({length:r.base+r.rounds-1},function(){return Math.floor(random()*4);});}
  if(game==='dodge'){r.duration=[18000,24000,30000,36000][level];r.fall=[1900,1650,1400,1200][level];r.interval=[1050,870,720,610][level];r.waves=[];for(var at=r.fall;at<r.duration-400;at+=r.interval){var lane=Math.floor(random()*3),blocked=[lane];if(level>1&&random()<.4)blocked.push((lane+1+Math.floor(random()*2))%3);r.waves.push({at:at,lanes:blocked});}}
  return r;
 }
 function validate(r,inputs,time){if(!Number.isInteger(time)||time<0||time>180000||!Array.isArray(inputs)||inputs.length>(r.game==='dodge'?600:100))throw Error('Invalid arcade run.');var last=-1;inputs.forEach(function(e){if(!e||!Number.isInteger(e.t)||e.t<=last||e.t>time||e.t<0)throw Error('Invalid input timing.');last=e.t;if(r.game!=='lock'&&(!Number.isInteger(e.v)||e.v<0||e.v>(r.game==='echo'?3:2)))throw Error('Invalid game input.');});}
 function lock(r,events,time){var last=0,angle=-Math.PI/2,dir=1,n=0,ended=0,reason='',won=false,terminal=false;for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++){var e=events[i],travel=(e.t-last)*r.speed/1000;if(n>=r.goal||terminal)throw Error('Input after a finished run.');if(Math.abs(travel-r.gaps[n])>r.tolerance){terminal=true;ended=Math.min(e.t,last+(r.gaps[n]+r.tolerance)*1000/r.speed);reason=travel<r.gaps[n]?'Too early. Catch the yellow target.':'The target slipped past.';}else{angle+=dir*travel;last=e.t;n++;dir*=-1;if(n===r.goal){terminal=true;won=true;ended=e.t;reason='Every pin popped. Lock open!';}}}
  var travel=(time-last)*r.speed/1000;if(!terminal&&travel>r.gaps[n]+r.tolerance){terminal=true;ended=last+(r.gaps[n]+r.tolerance)*1000/r.speed;reason='The target slipped past.';}
  return {terminal:terminal,won:won,endedAt:Math.round(ended),reason:reason,progress:n,total:r.goal,angle:angle+dir*travel,target:angle+dir*(r.gaps[n]||0),dir:dir};
 }
 function echo(r,events,time){var round=0,index=0,start=0,consumed=0;for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++){var e=events[i],length=r.base+round,listen=start+600+length*r.flash,deadline=listen+r.response;if(round>=r.rounds)throw Error('Input after a finished run.');if(e.t<listen)return {terminal:true,won:false,endedAt:e.t,reason:'Wait until the pattern finishes.',progress:round,total:r.rounds,consumed:i+1};if(e.t>deadline)return {terminal:true,won:false,endedAt:deadline,reason:'Time ran out. Give the pattern another try.',progress:round,total:r.rounds,consumed:i+1};if(e.v!==r.sequence[index])return {terminal:true,won:false,endedAt:e.t,reason:'That was a different pattern.',progress:round,total:r.rounds,consumed:i+1};index++;consumed++;if(index===length){round++;index=0;start=e.t+800;if(round===r.rounds)return {terminal:true,won:true,endedAt:e.t,reason:'Perfect recall. Every pattern complete!',progress:round,total:r.rounds,consumed:consumed};}}
  var length=r.base+round,listen=start+600+length*r.flash,deadline=listen+r.response,t=time-start-600,flashing=time<listen,step=Math.floor(t/r.flash),lit=flashing&&t>=0&&(t%r.flash)<r.flash*.68?r.sequence[step]:-1;
  return {terminal:time>deadline,won:false,endedAt:deadline,reason:'Time ran out. Give the pattern another try.',progress:round,total:r.rounds,round:round,index:index,length:length,listen:listen,deadline:deadline,flashing:flashing,lit:lit,stageStart:start};
 }
 function dodge(r,events,time){var lane=1,j=0;for(var i=0;i<r.waves.length;i++){var w=r.waves[i];if(w.at>time)break;while(j<events.length&&events[j].t<=w.at){if(Math.abs(events[j].v-lane)>1)throw Error('Move one lane at a time.');lane=events[j++].v;}if(w.lanes.indexOf(lane)>=0)return {terminal:true,won:false,endedAt:w.at,reason:'Crate collision. Try a different lane.',progress:Math.floor(w.at/1000),total:r.duration/1000,lane:lane};}
  while(j<events.length){if(Math.abs(events[j].v-lane)>1)throw Error('Move one lane at a time.');lane=events[j++].v;}
  return {terminal:time>=r.duration,won:time>=r.duration,endedAt:r.duration,reason:'Clean run. You made it through!',progress:Math.floor(Math.min(time,r.duration)/1000),total:r.duration/1000,lane:lane};
 }
 function evaluate(r,events,time){validate(r,events,time);var s=r.game==='lock'?lock(r,events,time):r.game==='echo'?echo(r,events,time):dodge(r,events,time);if(s.consumed!=null&&s.terminal&&s.consumed!==events.length)throw Error('Input after a finished run.');return s;}
 return {tiers:tiers,games:games,challenge:challenge,evaluate:evaluate};
}
var ArcadeCore=createArcadeCore_();

/* Free skill-game rewards: one authenticated user record stores balance + receipt. */
var TribeArcade=(function(){
 'use strict';
 var actions=['arcadeState','arcadeStart','arcadeFinish'];
 function fail(s){var e=new Error(s);e.name='TribeError';throw e;}
 function clone(v){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));}
 function run(ctx,action,p){
  var u=ctx.user,db=ctx.db,now=ctx.now,date=new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0,10);p=p||{};if(!u||u.role==='pending'||u.banned||u.deleting)fail('Log in with an approved account to play for coins.');u=clone(db.get('Users',u.id));
  var a=u.arcade||{date:date,earned:0,starts:0,receipts:[],startKeys:[],bests:{}};if(a.date!==date){a.date=date;a.earned=0;a.starts=0;}a.receipts=a.receipts||[];a.startKeys=a.startKeys||[];a.bests=a.bests||{};
  function status(){return {earned:a.earned,limit:300,remaining:Math.max(0,300-a.earned),startsRemaining:Math.max(0,40-a.starts),bests:a.bests,serverTime:now};}
  function save(){u.arcade=a;db.put('Users',u);}
  if(action==='arcadeState')return {arcade:status(),me:Tribe.me(u)};
  if(action==='arcadeStart'){
   var key=String(p.requestId||'');if(!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{8,80}$/.test(key))fail('Invalid run request.');var prior=a.startKeys.find(function(k){return k.id===key;});
   if(prior){if(prior.game!==p.game||prior.difficulty!==p.difficulty||prior.practice!==(p.practice===true))fail('That request belongs to a different game.');if(a.active&&a.active.id===prior.runId&&a.active.expires>now)return {run:clone(a.active),arcade:status()};fail('That attempt is already over. Start a fresh run.');}
   if(a.lastStartAt&&now-a.lastStartAt<1500)fail('Wait a moment before starting another run.');
   var seed=Math.floor(ctx.random()*4294967295)+1,c=ArcadeCore.challenge(p.game,p.difficulty,seed),eligible=p.practice!==true&&a.starts<40&&a.earned<300;
   a.active={id:ctx.id(),game:c.game,difficulty:c.difficulty,seed:seed,version:1,startedAt:now,expires:now+600000,eligible:eligible,reward:eligible?Math.min(c.reward,300-a.earned):0};a.lastStartAt=now;if(eligible)a.starts++;
   a.startKeys.push({id:key,game:c.game,difficulty:c.difficulty,practice:p.practice===true,runId:a.active.id});a.startKeys=a.startKeys.slice(-6);save();return {run:clone(a.active),arcade:status()};
  }
  if(action==='arcadeFinish'){
   var id=String(p.runId||''),receipt=a.receipts.find(function(r){return r.id===id;});if(receipt)return {result:clone(receipt.result),arcade:status(),me:Tribe.me(u),replayed:true};
   var r=a.active;if(!r||r.id!==id)fail('This attempt is no longer active. Start a fresh run.');if(now>r.expires)fail('This attempt expired. Start a fresh run.');if(r.version!==1)fail('Refresh to use the current arcade rules.');
   var c=ArcadeCore.challenge(r.game,r.difficulty,r.seed),grade=ArcadeCore.evaluate(c,p.inputs,p.duration);if(!grade.terminal)fail('Finish the challenge before collecting coins.');if(p.duration>now-r.startedAt+150)fail('That run finished too quickly. Wait a moment and retry collection.');
   var award=grade.won&&r.eligible?Math.min(r.reward,300-a.earned):0;if(!Number.isSafeInteger(u.points)||!Number.isSafeInteger(u.points+award))fail('Your balance needs owner review.');
   var record=a.bests[r.game+':'+r.difficulty]||{wins:0,bestMs:0,progress:0};record.progress=Math.max(record.progress,grade.progress);if(grade.won){record.wins++;record.bestMs=record.bestMs?Math.min(record.bestMs,grade.endedAt):grade.endedAt;}a.bests[r.game+':'+r.difficulty]=record;
   var result={runId:r.id,game:r.game,difficulty:r.difficulty,won:grade.won,earned:award,practice:!r.eligible,progress:grade.progress,total:grade.total,ms:grade.endedAt,reason:grade.reason};u.points+=award;a.earned+=award;a.active=null;a.receipts.push({id:r.id,result:result});a.receipts=a.receipts.slice(-8);save();return {result:result,arcade:status(),me:Tribe.me(u)};
  }
  fail('Unknown arcade action.');
 }
 return {handles:function(a){return actions.indexOf(a)>=0;},run:run};
})();
