Shop editing and try-on previews. Preview changes never mutate the account. */
let shopPreviewId='';
function shopTypeLabel(type){return {banner:'Profile banner',color:'Name color',font:'Name font',frame:'Avatar frame',avatar:'Custom avatar unlock',badge:'Profile badge'}[type]||'Cosmetic';}
function cosmeticProfilePreview(item){
 const base=state.me||Object.assign(Tribe.user('preview-guest','YourMonke',Date.now()),{role:'member',points:0,avatar:MONKE_IMAGE});
 const person={...base,equipped:{...base.equipped}};
 const cosmetic={...item,id:'__try_on__'};
 if(cosmetic.value&&cosmetic.type!=='avatar')person.equipped[cosmetic.type]=cosmetic.id;
 if(cosmetic.type==='avatar')person.avatar=person.avatar||MONKE_IMAGE;
 const items=[...state.shop.filter(x=>x.id!==cosmetic.id),cosmetic];
 return profileCard(person,false,items);
}
function renderShopPreview(item){
 if(!item)return '<div class="panel"><h3>Your next look</h3><p class="small">Pick a cosmetic to preview it on a profile.</p></div>';
 return `<div class="try-on-heading"><div><div class="eyebrow">TRY IT ON</div><h2>Your next look</h2></div><span class="pill neutral">PREVIEW</span></div>${cosmeticProfilePreview(item)}<div class="try-on-details"><span class="small muted">${esc(shopTypeLabel(item.type))}</span><h3>${esc(item.name)}</h3><p>${esc(item.description)}</p><div class="between"><b class="accent">◉ ${Number(item.price).toLocaleString()} points</b><span class="pill neutral">${state.me?.inventory.includes(item.id)?'OWNED':'IN THE SHOP'}</span></div></div><p class="preview-caption">${item.type==='avatar'?'Example photo. This unlock lets you upload your own profile picture.':'Preview only. Your current cosmetics stay equipped until you change them in Personalization.'}</p>`;
}
function renderShop(){
 const categories=['All','Banners','Name colors','Fonts','Frames','Avatars','Badges'],types={'Banners':'banner','Name colors':'color','Fonts':'font','Frames':'frame','Avatars':'avatar','Badges':'badge'};
 const items=state.shop.filter(i=>i.active&&(shopFilter==='All'||i.type===types[shopFilter]));
 const selected=items.find(i=>i.id===shopPreviewId)||items[0];shopPreviewId=selected?.id||'';
 const cards=items.map(i=>{const owned=state.me?.inventory.includes(i.id);return `<article class="shop-card ${i.id===shopPreviewId?'preview-selected':''}" data-shop-card="${esc(i.id)}"><button class="shop-art-button" type="button" data-action="previewShopItem" data-id="${esc(i.id)}" aria-label="Preview ${esc(i.name)} on a profile" aria-pressed="${i.id===shopPreviewId}">${shopArt(i)}</button><div class="shop-info"><h3>${esc(i.name)}</h3><p>${esc(i.description)}</p><button class="text-btn shop-try-button" type="button" data-action="previewShopItem" data-id="${esc(i.id)}">${icon('users')} Preview on profile</button><div class="shop-price"><span class="accent">◉ ${i.price}</span><button class="btn ${owned?'secondary':''} compact" data-action="${owned?'ownedItem':'buy'}" data-id="${esc(i.id)}">${owned?'Owned ✓':'Get item'}</button></div></div></article>`;}).join('');
 return `<div class="page-heading between"><div><div class="eyebrow">A LITTLE PERSONALITY GOES A LONG WAY</div><h1>The tribe shop<span class="accent">.</span></h1><p>Try a new look. Find your favorite. Make it yours.</p></div><div class="balance"><b class="coin">◉</b> ${(state.me?.points||0).toLocaleString()} <span>points</span></div></div><div class="tabs" role="group" aria-label="Shop categories">${categories.map(c=>`<button class="tab ${c===shopFilter?'active':''}" data-shop-filter="${esc(c)}">${c}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="shop-layout"><section aria-label="Shop items"><div class="cards-grid">${cards}</div>${!items.length?'<div class="empty"><h3>Nothing on this shelf yet.</h3><p>Check back for new drops.</p></div>':''}</section><aside id="shopProfilePreview" class="shop-profile-preview" aria-label="Cosmetic profile preview">${renderShopPreview(selected)}</aside></div>`;
}
function selectShopPreview(id){
 const item=state.shop.find(i=>i.id===id&&i.active);if(!item)return;
 shopPreviewId=id;
 const preview=$('#shopProfilePreview');if(!preview)return;
 preview.innerHTML=renderShopPreview(item);
 $$('[data-shop-card]').forEach(card=>{const selected=card.dataset.shopCard===id;card.classList.toggle('preview-selected',selected);$('.shop-art-button',card)?.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(selected));});
 if(matchMedia('(max-width: 1100px)').matches)preview.scrollIntoView?.({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
}
function showPurchase(id){
 if(!gate())return;const item=state.shop.find(i=>i.id===id);if(!item)return;
 showModal('A fresh look awaits',`<div class="purchase-layout"><section>${shopArt(item)}<div class="spacer"></div><h2>${esc(item.name)}</h2><p class="small" style="margin-top:7px">${esc(item.description)}</p><div class="divider"></div><div class="between"><span>Price</span><b class="accent">◉ ${item.price} points</b></div><p class="form-note">Your balance: ${state.me.points} points${state.me.points>=item.price?' · '+(state.me.points-item.price)+' after purchase':''}</p><form>${formError()}<button type="submit" class="btn full" ${state.me.points<item.price?'disabled':''}>${state.me.points<item.price?'Earn '+(item.price-state.me.points)+' more points':'Buy for '+item.price+' points'}</button></form></section><aside class="purchase-profile"><div class="eyebrow">ON YOUR PROFILE</div>${cosmeticProfilePreview(item)}<p class="preview-caption">${item.type==='avatar'?'An example of your own uploaded profile picture.':'Preview only. Equip it in Personalization after purchasing.'}</p></aside></div>`,async()=>{
  await call('buy',{id});closeModal();await sync();toast('Added to your collection. Equip it in Personalization.');
 });
 $('#dialog').classList.add('shop-purchase-dialog');
}
function readShopImageFile(file){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
 const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>resolve(String(reader.result));reader.onerror=()=>reject(new Error('That file could not be read. Please choose it again.'));reader.readAsDataURL(file);
});}
async function prepareShopBanner(file,onProgress=()=>{}){
 if(!file||!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp|gif)$/.test(file.type))throw new Error('Choose a PNG, JPG, WebP, or GIF image.');
 if(!file.size||file.size>8*1024*1024)throw new Error('Choose an image under 8 MB.');
 onProgress(10);
 if(file.type==='image/gif'){
  if(file.size>20*1024)throw new Error('To keep its animation, a GIF must be 20 KB or smaller. PNG, JPG, and WebP images are resized automatically.');
  const value=await readShopImageFile(file);
  if(value.length>30000)throw new Error('That GIF is too large. Choose a smaller GIF.');
  // Decode before accepting, including files with a misleading extension.
  await decodeShopImage(value);
  onProgress(100);return {value,animated:true,label:file.name+' · animation preserved'};
 }
 const source=URL.createObjectURL(file);
 try{
  const img=await decodeShopImage(source);
  if(!img.naturalWidth||!img.naturalHeight||img.naturalWidth*img.naturalHeight>32000000)throw new Error('Choose an image no larger than 32 megapixels.');
  onProgress(30);await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
  const canvas=document.createElement('canvas'),ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  if(!ctx)throw new Error('Your browser could not prepare the image. Try Chrome or Edge.');
  const sizes=[720,600,480,360,240],qualities=[.86,.7,.55,.4];
  for(let level=0;level<sizes.length;level++){
   const width=sizes[level],height=Math.round(width/3);canvas.width=width;canvas.height=height;
   const scale=Math.max(width/img.naturalWidth,height/img.naturalHeight),w=img.naturalWidth*scale,h=img.naturalHeight*scale;
   ctx.drawImage(img,(width-w)/2,(height-h)/2,w,h);
   for(const quality of qualities){
    const value=canvas.toDataURL('image/webp',quality);
    if(/^data:image\/(webp|png);base64,/.test(value)&&value.length<=30000){onProgress(100);return {value,animated:false,label:file.name+' · '+width+' × '+height+' · ready'};}
   }
   onProgress(40+level*12);await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
  }
  throw new Error('That image could not be made small enough. Choose a simpler image.');
 }finally{URL.revokeObjectURL(source);}
}
function decodeShopImage(source){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>resolve(img);img.onerror=()=>reject(new Error('That image could not be opened. Choose another file.'));img.src=source;});}
function showShopEditor(id){
 if(!Tribe.admin(state.me))throw new Error('Only the owner and co-owner can edit the shop.');
 const original=state.shop.find(item=>item.id===id)||{name:'',description:'',type:'banner',price:100,value:'',animated:false,active:true};
 const values={banner:'',color:'#b9f451',font:'sans',frame:'orbit',avatar:'upload',badge:'🍌',[original.type]:original.value};
 let bannerMode=Tribe.presets[values.banner]?values.banner:'upload',bannerImage=bannerMode==='upload'?values.banner:'',uploadLabel=bannerImage?'Current saved image. Choose a file to replace it.':'No image chosen yet.',preparing=false,uploadSequence=0,draft={...original};
 showModal(id?'Edit shop item':'Create a shop item',`<form id="shopEditorForm" class="shop-editor-layout"><section>${field('Item name','name',draft.name,'text','required maxlength="40" placeholder="Give this look a name"')}<div class="form-grid"><label class="field">Type<select name="type" ${id?'disabled':''}>${opts([['banner','Profile banner'],['color','Name color'],['font','Name font'],['frame','Avatar frame'],['avatar','Custom avatar unlock'],['badge','Profile badge']],draft.type)}</select></label>${field('Price in points','price',draft.price,'number','required min="0" max="100000" step="1"')}</div><div id="shopValueFields"></div><label class="field">Description<textarea name="description" maxlength="160" placeholder="Tell the tribe what makes it yours.">${esc(draft.description)}</textarea></label><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="active" ${draft.active?'checked':''}> For sale in the shop</label><p class="form-note">Existing buyers keep archived items. Edits update the cosmetic for everyone who owns it.</p>${formError()}<button id="saveShopItem" class="btn full" type="submit">Save item</button></section><aside class="shop-editor-preview"><div class="try-on-heading"><div><div class="eyebrow">LIVE PREVIEW</div><h3>On a profile</h3></div><span class="pill neutral">DRAFT</span></div><div id="adminShopPreview"></div><div class="try-on-details"><h3 id="shopDraftName"></h3><p id="shopDraftDescription"></p><b id="shopDraftPrice" class="accent"></b></div><p class="preview-caption">This preview uses your profile. Nothing changes until you save the item.</p></aside></form>`,async()=>{
  if(preparing)throw new Error('Wait for the image to finish preparing.');
  readDraft();if(draft.type==='banner'&&!draft.value)throw new Error('Choose a banner image or one of the built-in designs.');
  const payload={...draft};if(id)payload.id=id;
  await call('shopSave',payload);closeModal();await sync();await ensureData(['shop']);await loadAdmin();toast('Shop item saved. Everyone can preview it in the shop.');
 });
 $('#dialog').classList.add('shop-editor-dialog');
 const form=$('#shopEditorForm'),fields=$('#shopValueFields'),error=$('[data-form-error]',form),save=$('#saveShopItem');
 function active(){return form.isConnected&&$('#dialog').open&&$('#shopEditorForm')===form;}
 function renderFields(){
  const type=draft.type;
  if(type==='banner')fields.innerHTML=`<label class="field">Banner design<select name="bannerSource">${opts([['upload','Upload an image'],['neon','Jungle pulse'],['aurora','Northern lights'],['sunset','After hours'],['ocean','Ocean'],['ember','Ember'],['midnight','Midnight']],bannerMode)}</select></label><div id="bannerFileArea" class="banner-file-area" ${bannerMode==='upload'?'':'hidden'}><label class="field">Upload banner image<input id="shopBannerFile" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif"></label><p class="form-note">PNG, JPG, or WebP up to 8 MB. Fitted to a wide banner automatically. GIF up to 20 KB keeps its animation.</p><progress id="shopImageProgress" aria-label="Preparing banner image" value="0" max="100" hidden></progress><p id="shopImageStatus" class="small muted" role="status">${esc(uploadLabel)}</p></div><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="animated" ${draft.animated?'checked':''}> Animate this banner</label>`;
  else if(type==='color')fields.innerHTML=`<label class="color-row">Name color<input type="color" name="value" value="${esc(values.color)}"></label><p class="form-note">See the color on the profile name beside this form.</p>`;
  else if(type==='font')fields.innerHTML=`<label class="field">Name font<select name="value">${opts([['sans','Modern sans'],['mono','Terminal'],['serif','Editorial serif'],['rounded','Rounded']],values.font)}</select></label>`;
  else if(type==='frame')fields.innerHTML=`<label class="field">Avatar frame<select name="value">${opts([['orbit','Orbit'],['glow','Glow'],['square','Square']],values.frame)}</select></label>`;
  else if(type==='avatar')fields.innerHTML='<div class="notice">This item unlocks custom profile picture uploads. Buyers choose their own photo in Personalization → My account. The preview shows an example.</div><div class="spacer"></div>';
  else fields.innerHTML=field('Badge emoji','value',values.badge,'text','required maxlength="8"');
 }
 function readDraft(){
  const data=new FormData(form);draft.name=String(data.get('name')||'');draft.description=String(data.get('description')||'');draft.price=Number(data.get('price'));draft.active=data.has('active');
  draft.value=draft.type==='banner'?(bannerMode==='upload'?bannerImage:bannerMode):draft.type==='avatar'?'upload':String(data.get('value')||'');
  draft.animated=draft.type==='banner'&&data.has('animated');values[draft.type]=draft.value;
 }
 function preview(){readDraft();$('#adminShopPreview').innerHTML=cosmeticProfilePreview(draft);$('#shopDraftName').textContent=draft.name||'Your new cosmetic';$('#shopDraftDescription').textContent=draft.description||'A little more personality for the tribe.';$('#shopDraftPrice').textContent='◉ '+(Number.isFinite(draft.price)?draft.price:0).toLocaleString()+' points';}
 form.addEventListener('input',event=>{if(!['type','bannerSource'].includes(event.target.name)&&event.target.type!=='file')preview();});
 form.addEventListener('change',async event=>{
  const el=event.target;
  if(el.name==='type'){readDraft();draft.type=el.value;draft.value=values[draft.type];draft.animated=false;renderFields();preview();return;}
  if(el.name==='bannerSource'){bannerMode=el.value;$('#bannerFileArea').hidden=bannerMode!=='upload';preview();return;}
  if(el.id!=='shopBannerFile'){preview();return;}
  const file=el.files?.[0];if(!file)return;
  const sequence=++uploadSequence;preparing=true;save.disabled=true;error.textContent='';
  const progress=$('#shopImageProgress'),status=$('#shopImageStatus');progress.hidden=false;status.textContent='Preparing '+file.name+'…';
  try{
   const image=await prepareShopBanner(file,value=>{if(active()&&sequence===uploadSequence)progress.value=value;});
   if(!active()||sequence!==uploadSequence)return;
   bannerImage=image.value;uploadLabel=image.label;
   if(draft.type==='banner'){
    const checkbox=$('[name="animated"]',fields);if(checkbox)checkbox.checked=image.animated;
    const currentStatus=$('#shopImageStatus');if(currentStatus)currentStatus.textContent=uploadLabel;
   }
   preview();
  }catch(err){if(active()&&sequence===uploadSequence){error.textContent=err.message;status.textContent=bannerImage?'Previous image kept. Choose another file.':'No image selected.';el.value='';}}
  finally{if(active()&&sequence===uploadSequence){preparing=false;save.disabled=false;progress.hidden=true;}}
 });
 renderFields();preview();
}

/* Profiles, conversations, and daily rewards. Bundled locally; no new network assets. */
let profileUserId='',socialChannelsOpen=false,socialMembersOpen=null,dailyClaimBusy=false;
let clockServer=Date.now(),clockAnchor=performance.now();
function anchorServerClock(time){if(Number.isFinite(time)){clockServer=time;clockAnchor=performance.now();}}
function serverNow(){return clockServer+Math.max(0,performance.now()-clockAnchor);}
function collectionCount(u){return Number.isInteger(u?.collectionCount)?u.collectionCount:(u?.inventory||[]).length;}
function memberLevel(u){return 1+Math.floor(collectionCount(u)/3);}
function memberPresence(u){return u.banned?'Unavailable':u.role==='pending'?'Awaiting a role':serverNow()-u.lastSeen<120000?'Recently active':'Away';}
function profileCard(u,controls=false,items){
 const bi=itemFor(u,'banner',items),canMessage=state.me&&state.me.role!=='pending'&&state.me.id!==u.id&&!u.banned&&u.role!=='pending';
 return `<article class="profile-card steam-mini"><div class="profile-banner ${bi?.animated?'animated-banner':''}" style="background:${esc(bannerCSS(u,items))}"></div><div class="profile-main"><div class="mini-profile-identity">${avatar(u,true,items)}<span class="profile-level" title="Tribe level: +1 for every 3 collected cosmetics">${memberLevel(u)}</span></div><h3>${username(u,items)}</h3><div class="handle muted">@${esc(u.username)}</div><div class="profile-role">${rolePill(u)}<span class="presence-label">${esc(memberPresence(u))}</span></div>${u.bio?`<p class="bio">${esc(u.bio)}</p>`:''}<div class="profile-stats"><div><b>${Number(u.points||0).toLocaleString()}</b><small>Tribe points</small></div><div><b>${collectionCount(u)}</b><small>Cosmetics</small></div><div><b>${new Date(u.joined||serverNow()).getFullYear()}</b><small>Joined</small></div></div>${controls?`<div class="profile-card-actions"><button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="viewProfile" data-id="${esc(u.id)}">View profile</button>${canMessage?`<button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="messageUser" data-id="${esc(u.id)}">${icon('dm')} Message</button>`:''}</div>`:''}</div></article>`;
}

class="profile-avatar-frame">${avatar(person,true)}</div><div class="profile-identity"><span class="eyebrow">THE MONKE TRIBE</span><h1>${username(person)}</h1><div class="profile-handle">@${esc(person.username)}</div><div class="profile-hero-meta">${rolePill(person)}<span>Member since ${new class="profile-hero-level"><div class="row"><span>Tribe level</span><span class="profile-level">${memberLevel(person)}</span></div><small>+1 level per 3 collected cosmetics</small>${own?'<button class="btn secondary full" data-action="profile">Edit profile</button>':canMessage?`<button class="btn secondary full" data-action="messageUser" data-id="${esc(person.id)}">${icon('dm')} Send }

function renderDaily(){
 const u=state.me,d=Tribe.dailyStatus(u,serverNow()),eligible=u&&u.role!=='pending'&&!u.banned;
 const focus=d.claimed?d.completed:d.day,start=Math.max(1,focus-2);
 const days=Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>start+i);

Your next reward keeps getting bigger.</p></div><span class="pill neutral">${icon('calendar')} NO RESET · NO FINAL DAY</span></div><div class="daily-hero"><div class="daily-hero-copy"><span class="daily-kicker">${d.claimed?'TODAY’S REWARD COLLECTED':eligible?'YOUR NEXT CLAIM':'YOUR REWARD JOURNEY'}</span><h2>Day ${focus.toLocaleString()}<span>${d.claimed?'Nicely done, monke.':'A little loyalty goes a long way.'}</span></h2><div class="daily-amount"><span>◉</span>${(d.claimed?Tribe.dailyReward(d.completed):d.reward).toLocaleString()}<small>points</small></div>${d.claimed?`<button class="btn daily-claim" disabled>${icon('check')} Collected today</button><p class="daily-reset">Day ${d.day.toLocaleString()} unlocks in <strong id="dailyCountdown">…</strong></p>`:eligible?`<button class="btn daily-claim" data-action="daily" ${dailyClaimBusy?'disabled':''}>${dailyClaimBusy?'Collecting…':`Collect ${d.reward.toLocaleString()} points`}${dailyClaimBusy?'':icon('plus')}</button><p class="daily-reset">One claim per day. Your next day is worth <b>${Tribe.dailyReward(d.day+1).toLocaleString()} points.</b></p>`:u?'<button class="btn daily-claim" disabled>Waiting for your role</button><p class="daily-reset">An owner needs to approve your account first.</p>':'<button class="btn daily-claim" data-action="auth">Log in to collect</button><p class="daily-reset">Your journey begins with 10 points.</p>'}</div><div class="daily-hero-art" aria-hidden="true"><div class="daily-orbit orbit-one"></div><div class="daily-orbit orbit-two"></div><div class="daily-coin">◉</div><span class="daily-float float-one">+5</span><span class="daily-float float-two">∞</span><span class="daily-float float-three">✦</span><div class="daily-art-caption">A BIGGER REWARD<br>EVERY DAY FROM DAY 3</div></div></div><div class="daily-summary"><div><span>Days collected</span><b>${d.completed.toLocaleString()}</b></div><div><span>Daily points earned</span><b>${d.total.toLocaleString()}</b></div><div><span>${d.claimed?'Tomorrow’s reward':'Next claim'}</span><b>${d.reward.toLocaleString()} <small>pts</small></b></div></div><div class="section-heading daily-track-heading"><div><h2>Your reward track</h2><p class="small muted">Days 1 & 2: 10 points. Day 3: 15. Then +5 each day you claim.</p></div><span class="daily-infinity">∞</span></div><div class="daily-track">${days.map(day=>{const done=day<=d.completed,current=day===focus;return `<article class="daily-day ${done?'collected':''} ${current?'current':''}" ${current?'aria-current="step"':''}><span class="day-label">DAY ${day.toLocaleString()}</span><span class="day-symbol">${done?'✓':day===d.day?'◉':icon('lock')}</span><strong>${Tribe.dailyReward(day).toLocaleString()}</strong><small>points</small><span class="day-state">${done?'Collected':day===d.day?(d.claimed?'Tomorrow':eligible?'Ready to claim':'Start here'):'Coming up'}</span></article>`;}).join('')}</div><div class="daily-explainer"><div>${icon('spark')}<h3>It keeps growing.</h3><p>Day 10 is 50 points. Day 100 is 500. There is no weekly reset or final reward.</p></div><div>${icon('shield')}<h3>Miss a day? Keep your place.</h3><p>Progress pauses until your next claim. Missed days don’t count, and your reward never drops back to day 1.</p></div><div>${icon('clock')}<h3>A fresh claim every day.</h3><p>Claims reset at midnight UTC — <span id="dailyResetLocal">${new Date(d.nextAt).toLocaleTimeString(undefined,{hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit',timeZoneName:'short'})}</span> in your timezone. Your account saves your progress.</p></div></div></section>`;
}
function updateDailyCountdown(){
 const root=$('[data-daily-date]');if(page!=='daily'||!root)return;
 const d=Tribe.dailyStatus(state.me,serverNow());
 if(root.dataset.dailyDate!==d.date){renderPage();return;}
 const el=$('#dailyCountdown');if(el){const s=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((d.nextAt-serverNow())/1000));el.textContent=String(Math.floor(s/3600)).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(Math.floor(s/60)%60).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(s%60).padStart(2,'0');}
}

function socialRail(isDM=false){return `<nav class="social-rail" aria-label="Conversation spaces"><button class="social-space ${!isDM?'selected':''}" data-nav="chat" aria-label="Tribe channels" title="Tribe channels">${brandImage()}</button><div class="rail-divider"></div><button class="social-space ${isDM?'selected':''}" data-nav="dms" aria-label="Direct messages" title="Direct messages">${icon('dm')}</button><button class="social-space" data-action="newDM" aria-label="Start a conversation" title="Start a conversation">${icon('plus')}</button><button class="social-space" data-action="chatRules" aria-label="Community rules" title="Community rules">${icon('shield')}</button></nav>`;}
function channelAccount(){return `<div class="channel-account"><button data-action="ownProfile" class="channel-account-profile">${avatar(state.me)}<span><b>${esc(state.me.displayName)}</b><small>${esc(roleFor(state.me).name)}</small></span></button><button class="icon-btn" data-nav="personalization" aria-label="Account settings">${icon('settings')}</button></div>`;}
function renderRoster(members){
 const active=members.filter(u=>serverNow()-u.lastSeen<120000).length;
 return `<aside class="chat-members"><div class="roster-heading">${icon('users')} MEMBERS <span>${members.length}</span></div><p class="roster-active">${active} recently active</p>${state.config.roles.map(r=>{const group=members.filter(u=>u.role===r.id);return group.length?`<section class="roster-group"><h3>${esc(r.name)} — ${group.length}</h3>${group.map(u=>`<button class="chat-member" data-action="viewProfile" data-id="${esc(u.id)}"><span class="chat-avatar-wrap">${avatar(u)}<i class="presence-dot ${serverNow()-u.lastSeen<120000?'active':''}"></i></span><span><b>${username(u)}</b><small>${esc(memberPresence(u))}</small></span></button>`).join('')}</section>`:'';}).join('')}</aside>`;
}
function chatFrame(thread,isDM=false){
 const t=isDM?state.threads.find(t=>t.id===thread):null,other=t?.members.filter(id=>id!==state.me.id).map(userById),name=isDM?(t?.group?t.name:other?.[0]?.displayName||'Conversation'):thread;
 const description=isDM?'Private to conversation members · '+(t?.members.length||0)+' members':({general:'The everyday home of the tribe.',gaming:'Find a game. Find your squad.','off-topic':'For everything outside the game.'}[thread]);
 return `<section class="chat-main"><header class="chat-header"><button class="icon-btn channel-toggle" data-action="toggleChannels" aria-label="Show channels" aria-expanded="${socialChannelsOpen}">☰</button><span class="chat-header-symbol">${isDM?icon(t?.group?'users':'dm'):'#'}</span><div class="chat-header-title"><h2>${esc(name)}</h2><p>${description}</p></div><div class="chat-header-tools">${isDM&&t?.group?`${t.creator===state.me.id?`<button class="icon-btn" data-action="addGroupMember" aria-label="Add member" title="Add member">${icon('plus')}</button>`:''}<button class="text-btn" data-action="leaveGroup">Leave</button>`:''}<button class="icon-btn" data-action="toggleMembers" aria-label="Show members" aria-expanded="${socialMembersOpen??matchMedia('(min-width:1301px)').matches}" title="Members">${icon('users')}</button></div></header><div class="messages" id="messages" role="log" aria-label="${esc(name)} messages" aria-live="polite" aria-relevant="additions text"></div><div class="composer-wrap"><form id="chatForm" class="composer"><span class="composer-mark" aria-hidden="true">${isDM?'@':'#'}</span><textarea id="messageInput" name="text" rows="1" maxlength="1800" aria-label="Message ${esc(name)}" placeholder="Message ${isDM?esc(name):'#'+esc(name)}" required></textarea><button type="submit" class="icon-btn" aria-label="Send message">${icon('send')}</button></form><div class="composer-note">Enter to send <span>·</span> Shift + Enter for a new line${!isDM?' <span>·</span> Earn points by chatting':''}</div></div></section>`;
}
function renderChat(){
 if(!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return signInPanel('The conversation starts here.');
 const members=state.members.filter(u=>!u.banned&&u.role!=='pending');
 return `<div class="chat-layout discord-layout ${socialChannelsOpen?'channels-open':''} ${socialMembersOpen===null?'':socialMembersOpen?'members-open':'members-hidden'}">${socialRail()}<aside class="channel-list"><div class="server-heading">${esc(state.config.title)}<span>⌄</span></div><div class="channel-scroll"><button class="channel-rule-link" data-action="chatRules">${icon('shield')} Tribe guidelines</button><div class="channel-label">⌄ &nbsp; TEXT CHANNELS</div>${['general','gaming','off-topic'].map(ch=>`<button class="channel-button ${ch===channel?'active':''}" data-channel="${ch}" ${ch===channel?'aria-current="page"':''}><span class="hash">#</span><span>${ch}</span>${ch===channel?'<span class="channel-live-dot"></span>':''}</button>`).join('')}<div class="channel-tip"><span>✦</span><b>Good conversation pays.</b><p>Earn 2 points per eligible minute of public chat, up to 60 a day.</p><button class="text-btn" data-nav="daily">Daily Points →</button></div></div>${channelAccount()}</aside>${chatFrame(channel)}${renderRoster(members)}</div>`;
}
function renderDMs(){
 if(!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return signInPanel('Keep your people close.');
 if(!state.threads.some(t=>t.id===dmId))dmId=state.threads[0]?.id||'';
 const current=state.threads.find(t=>t.id===dmId),members=current?current.members.map(userById):[];
 return `<div class="chat-layout discord-layout dm-layout ${socialChannelsOpen?'channels-open':''} ${socialMembersOpen===null?'':socialMembersOpen?'members-open':'members-hidden'}">${socialRail(true)}<aside class="channel-list"><div class="server-heading">Messages<button class="icon-btn" data-action="newDM" aria-label="New message">${icon('plus')}</button></div><div class="channel-scroll"><button class="btn secondary full dm-start" data-action="newDM">${icon('plus')} New conversation</button><div class="channel-label">DIRECT MESSAGES · ${state.threads.length}</div>${state.threads.map(t=>`<button class="channel-button dm-channel ${t.id===dmId?'active':''}" data-thread="${esc(t.id)}">${t.system?'<span class="group-avatar">'+icon('shield')+'</span>':t.group?'<span class="group-avatar">'+icon('users')+'</span>':avatar(userById(t.members.find(id=>id!==state.me.id)))}<span class="dm-name"><b>${esc(threadName(t))}</b><small>${t.group?t.members.length+' members':'Direct message'}</small></span></button>`).join('')||'<p class="channel-note">Your private conversations will appear here.</p>'}</div>${channelAccount()}</aside>${dmId?chatFrame(dmId,true):`<section class="chat-main dm-empty"><div class="chat-header"><button class="icon-btn channel-toggle" data-action="toggleChannels" aria-label="Show conversations">☰</button><h2>Your conversations</h2></div><div class="empty">${icon('dm')}<h2>Your people, a little closer.</h2><p>Start a private conversation or make a group for the whole crew.</p><button class="btn" data-action="newDM">Start a conversation</button></div></section>`}${renderRoster(members)}</div>`;
}
function renderMessages(scroll=true){
 const el=$('#messages');if(!el)return;
 const thread=page==='chat'?channel:dmId;if(messagesKey!==page+':'+thread)chatMessages=[];
 const signature=JSON.stringify([thread,chatMessages,hasMoreMessages,state.members.map(u=>[u.id,u.displayName,u.role,u.equipped])]);
 if(el.dataset.signature===signature)return;
 const nearBottom=el.scrollHeight-el.scrollTop-el.clientHeight<90,oldScroll=el.scrollTop,oldHeight=el.scrollHeight;
 const title=page==='chat'?'Welcome to #'+channel:'Just between us.';
 el.innerHTML=`${hasMoreMessages&&chatMessages.length?'<button class="text-btn earlier-messages" data-action="olderMessages">Load earlier messages</button>':`<div class="chat-welcome"><span class="channel-mark">${page==='chat'?'#':icon('dm')}</span><h3>${esc(title)}</h3><p>${page==='chat'?'This is your place to hang out, share, and stay a little wild.':'Only conversation members can read these messages.'}</p></div>`}${chatMessages.map((m,index)=>{
  if(m.system)return renderSystemMessage(m);
  const u=userById(m.author),previous=chatMessages[index-1],date=new Date(m.at),sameDay=previous&&date.toDateString()===new Date(previous.at).toDateString();
  const grouped=previous&&sameDay&&previous.author===m.author&&m.at-previous.at<300000&&!previous.deleted&&!m.deleted;
  const canDelete=m.author===state.me.id||(page==='chat'&&roleFor(state.me).moderate),time=date.toLocaleTimeString([],{hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'});
  return `${!sameDay?`<div class="message-date"><span>${date.toLocaleDateString(undefined,{month:'long',day:'numeric',year:'numeric'})}</span></div>`:''}<article class="message ${grouped?'grouped':''}" tabindex="0" aria-label="${esc(u.displayName)}, ${esc(time)}">${grouped?`<time class="grouped-time" title="${esc(date.toLocaleString())}">${time}</time>`:`<button class="message-avatar-button" data-action="viewProfile" data-id="${esc(u.id)}" aria-label="View ${esc(u.displayName)}'s profile">${avatar(u)}</button>`}<div class="message-body">${grouped?'':`<div class="message-meta"><button class="message-author" data-action="viewProfile" data-id="${esc(u.id)}"><b>${username(u)}</b></button><span class="role-tag" style="color:${esc(roleFor(u).color)}">${esc(roleFor(u).name)}</span><time title="${esc(date.toLocaleString())}">${time}</time></div>`}<div class="message-text">${m.deleted?'<i class="muted">Message removed</i>':esc(m.text)}</div>${!m.deleted?`<div class="message-tools">${['🍌','🔥','💚','😂'].map(em=>`<button data-action="react" data-id="${esc(m.id)}" data-emoji="${em}" aria-label="React ${em}">${em}</button>`).join('')}${canDelete?`<button data-action="deleteMessage" data-id="${esc(m.id)}" aria-label="Delete message">×</button>`:''}</div><div class="reactions">${Object.entries(m.reactions||{}).filter(([em,ids])=>ids.length).map(([em,ids])=>`<button class="reaction-chip ${ids.includes(state.me.id)?'selected':''}" data-action="react" data-id="${esc(m.id)}" data-emoji="${esc(em)}">${esc(em)} ${ids.length}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:''}</div></article>`;
 }).join('')}`;
 el.dataset.signature=signature;
 if(scroll&&nearBottom)el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;else if(!scroll)el.scrollTop=oldScroll+el.scrollHeight-oldHeight;
}

function stopAIThinking(){clearInterval(aiTimer);aiTimer=null;aiStartedAt=0;}

const slotFaces=['7','BAR','♥','♦','♠','🍌'];
function resetExpansion(){resetArcade();stopAIThinking();resetSocial();aiDurations=[];marketState=null;marketError='';marketLoading=false;slotResult=null;slotError='';slotBusy=false;economyPending={};aiState=null;aiMessages=[];aiAttachment=null;aiDraft='';aiError='';aiSending=false;aiImageBusy=false;aiGeneration++;}
function expansionMe(me){if(me&&state.me?.id===me.id){state.me=me;renderShell();}}
function points(n){return Number(n||0).toLocaleString();}
function renderAnnouncement(){
 const box=$('#communityAnnouncement');if(!box)return;const a=state.config.announcement;box.hidden=!a;
 if(a){const html=`<div class="announcement-icon">${icon('spark')}</div><div><div class="eyebrow">COMMUNITY ANNOUNCEMENT</div><h2>${esc(a.title)}</h2><p>${esc(a.body)}</p><small>Posted by ${esc(a.author)} · ${new Date(a.at).toLocaleDateString()}</small></div>${Tribe.admin(state.me)?'<button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="x-announcement">Edit</button>':''}`;if(box.innerHTML!==html)box.innerHTML=html;}
}
function showAnnouncementEditor(){const a=state.config.announcement||{};showModal('Community announcement',`<form>${field('Headline','title',a.title||'','text','required maxlength="90"')}<label class="field">Message for everyone<textarea name="body" rows="6" required maxlength="1200">${esc(a.body||'')}</textarea></label><p class="form-note">Appears above every tab, including for visitors. Connected pages refresh within about a minute.</p>${formError()}<button class="btn" type="submit">Publish to the whole site</button>${a.title?'<button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-action="x-removeAnnouncement">Remove announcement</button>':''}</form>`,async d=>{await call('announcementSave',{title:d.get('title'),body:d.get('body')});closeModal();await sync(false);toast('Announcement published.');});}
function renderAnnouncementAdmin(){const a=state.config.announcement;return `<div class="panel"><div class="section-heading"><h3>Community announcement</h3><button class="btn" data-action="x-announcement">${a?'Edit announcement':'Write announcement'}</button></div><p>Owner and co-owner messages appear across the whole site.</p>${a?`<div class="announcement-preview"><h2>${esc(a.title)}</h2><p>${esc(a.body)}</p></div>`:'<div class="empty"><p>No announcement is live.</p></div>'}</div>`;}
function slotSymbol(index){return `<span class="slot-symbol s-${index}">${slotFaces[index]}</span>`;}
function renderSlots(){
 const r=slotResult,reels=r?.reels||[0,0,0];
 return `<div class="page-heading between"><div><div class="eyebrow">THE TRIBE ARCADE / 001</div><h1>One more spin<span class="accent">.</span></h1><p>A little retro luck. All monke.</p></div><span class="pill neutral">SITE POINTS ONLY</span></div><div class="slot-layout"><section class="slot-cabinet ${slotBusy?'is-spinning':''}" aria-label="Retro slot machine"><div class="slot-top"><div class="metal-label">MONKE<br><b>SLOT MACHINE</b></div><div class="slot-lights"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div></div><div class="slot-inner"><aside class="slot-labels"><span>♟ &nbsp;1 PLAYER</span><span>◉ &nbsp;3 REELS</span><span>✦ &nbsp;GOOD LUCK</span></aside><div class="reel-frame" aria-label="${slotBusy?'Spinning':reels.map(i=>slotFaces[i]).join(', ')}"><div class="reel-payline"></div>${reels.map((v,i)=>`<div class="slot-reel" style="--reel-delay:${i*95}ms"><div class="reel-strip" aria-hidden="true">${slotSymbol((v+2)%6)}${slotSymbol(v)}${slotSymbol((v+4)%6)}</div></div>`).join('')}</div></div><div class="slot-console"><div class="slot-payout-mini"><span>777 <b>×25</b></span><span>TRIPLE <b>×10</b></span><span>PAIR <b>×1</b></span></div><div class="slot-display" role="status" aria-live="polite">${slotBusy?'SPINNING…':r?(r.payout>r.stake?'YOU WIN '+points(r.payout)+' POINTS':r.payout===r.stake?'PAIR! STAKE RETURNED':'NO MATCH THIS TIME'):'READY WHEN YOU ARE'}<small>${r&&!slotBusy?'Net '+(r.payout-r.stake>=0?'+':'')+(r.payout-r.stake)+' points':'PRESS SPIN TO START'}</small></div></div><div class="slot-controls"><label>BET <select id="slotStake" ${slotBusy?'disabled':''}>${opts([1,5,10,25,50,100].map(n=>[n,n+' points']),slotStake)}</select></label><button class="slot-spin" data-action="x-spin" ${slotBusy?'disabled':''}>${slotBusy?'SPINNING':economyPending.slot?'RETRY SPIN':'SPIN'} <span>↻</span></button><div class="slot-credit">BALANCE<b>${points(state.me?.points)}</b></div></div>${slotError?`<p class="slot-error" role="alert">${esc(slotError)}</p>`:''}</section><aside class="slot-guide"><div class="eyebrow">KNOW YOUR REELS</div><h2>Three of a kind.<br>A moment of glory.</h2><p>Pick your stake and spin. A match on the center line decides the result.</p><div class="payout-table"><div><strong class="sevens">7 7 7</strong><span>25× your stake</span></div><div><strong>♥ ♥ ♥</strong><span>Any other triple · 10×</span></div><div><strong>♦ ♦ ♠</strong><span>Any pair · stake back</span></div><div><strong>♠ ♦ ♥</strong><span>No match · 0 points</span></div></div><p class="form-note">Returns include your stake. }
function economyId(){return new Date(serverNow()).toISOString().slice(0,10)+':'+crypto.randomUUID();}
function pendingEconomy(key,action,data){
 const storage='tribe.economy.'+state.me.id+'.'+key;
 if(!economyPending[key]){try{const old=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(storage)||'null');if(old&&old.action===action&&old.data.requestId.startsWith(new Date(serverNow()).toISOString().slice(0,10)+':'))economyPending[key]=old;}catch{}}
 if(!economyPending[key]){economyPending[key]={action,data:{...data,requestId:economyId()}};try{sessionStorage.setItem(storage,JSON.stringify(economyPending[key]));}catch{}}
 return {storage,request:economyPending[key]};
}
async function economyCall(key,action,data){
 const uid=state.me.id,{storage,request}=pendingEconomy(key,action,data);
 try{const result=await call(request.action,request.data);if(state.me?.id!==uid)return null;delete economyPending[key];try{sessionStorage.removeItem(storage);}catch{}expansionMe(result.me);return result;}
 catch(error){if(state.me?.id!==uid)throw error;const uncertain=/Connection lost|server did not respond|server could not finish/.test(error.message);if(!uncertain){delete economyPending[key];try{sessionStorage.removeItem(storage);}catch{}}throw new Error(error.message+(uncertain?' Retry once using the same button to recover the result.':''));}
}
async function spinSlots(){
 if(!gate()||slotBusy)return;slotStake=Number($('#slotStake')?.value||slotStake);slotBusy=true;slotError='';renderPage();const uid=state.me.id;
 try{const [result]=await Promise.all([economyCall('slot','slotSpin',{stake:slotStake}),new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,1400))]);if(result&&state.me?.id===uid)slotResult=result;}
 catch(e){if(state.me?.id===uid)slotError=e.message;}
 finally{if(state.me?.id===uid){slotBusy=false;if(page==='minigames')renderPage();}}
}
async function loadMarket(){
 if(marketLoading||!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return;const uid=state.me.id;marketLoading=true;marketError='';
 try{const r=await call('marketData',{},true);if(state.me?.id!==uid)return;marketState=r;anchorServerClock(r.serverTime);expansionMe(r.me);if(!r.stocks.some(s=>s.id===selectedStock))selectedStock=r.stocks[0]?.id;}
 catch(e){if(state.me?.id===uid)marketError=e.message;}
 finally{if(state.me?.id===uid){marketLoading=false;if(page==='stocks')renderPage();}}
}
function stockChart(s){
 const h=s.history||[];if(h.length<2)return '<div class="chart-empty">The first price is set. The chart grows as prices change.</div>';
 const low=Math.min(...h.map(p=>p.price)),high=Math.max(...h.map(p=>p.price)),range=Math.max(1,high-low),path=h.map((p,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'}${(i/(h.length-1)*540+10).toFixed(1)},${(130-(p.price-low)/range*110).toFixed(1)}`).join(' ');
 return `<svg class="stock-chart" viewBox="0 0 560 150" role="img" aria-label="${esc(s.name)} price history, ${low} to ${high} points"><path d="M10 20H550 M10 75H550 M10 130H550" class="chart-grid"/><path d="${path}" class="chart-line"/></svg><div class="chart-labels"><span>${new Date(h[0].at).toLocaleString()}</span><span>${new Date(h[h.length-1].at).toLocaleString()}</span></div>`;
}
function renderStocks(){
 if(!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return signInPanel('Your first share starts here.');
 const owner=state.me.role==='owner';
 let heading=`<div class="page-heading between"><div><div class="eyebrow">THE MONKE EXCHANGE</div><h1>Small shares. Big ideas<span class="accent">.</span></h1><p>Buy low, sell when you choose. Everything here is made up.</p></div><button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="x-refreshMarket" ${marketLoading?'disabled':''}>${icon('clock')} Refresh prices</button></div><div class="market-disclaimer">◉ Fictional stocks and bets · Site points only · No real money</div><div class="tabs">${[['stocks','Stocks'],['bets','Community bets']].map(([id,n])=>`<button class="tab ${marketTab===id?'active':''}" data-action="x-marketTab" data-id="${id}">${n}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
 if(marketError)return heading+`<div class="panel" role="alert"><p>${esc(marketError)}</p><button class="btn" data-action="x-refreshMarket">Try again</button></div>`;
 if(!marketState)return heading+'<div class="panel loading" role="status">Opening the exchange…</div>';
 if(marketTab==='bets')return heading+renderBets();
 const stocks=marketState.stocks,s=stocks.find(x=>x.id===selectedStock)||stocks[0],holdings=marketState.holdings;
 const value=stocks.reduce((v,s)=>v+(holdings[s.id]?.quantity||0)*s.price,0),cost=Object.values(holdings).reduce((v,h)=>v+h.cost,0);
 return heading+`<div class="market-stats"><div><span>Available points</span><b>${points(state.me.points)}</b></div><div><span>Your shares are worth</span><b>${points(value)} <small>pts</small></b></div><div><span>Unrealized gain / loss</span><b class="${value>=cost?'gain':'loss'}">${value-cost>=0?'+':''}${points(value-cost)}</b></div></div><div class="exchange-layout"><section class="panel stock-list"><div class="section-heading"><h3>Market watch</h3>${owner?'<button class="text-btn" data-action="x-stockEditor">+ Create</button>':''}</div>${stocks.map(t=>`<button class="stock-row ${s?.id===t.id?'selected':''}" data-action="x-selectStock" data-id="${esc(t.id)}"><span class="stock-emoji">${esc(t.emoji)}</span><span><b>${esc(t.symbol)}</b><small>${esc(t.name)}${!t.active?' · Buying paused':''}</small></span><span class="stock-emoji large">${esc(s.emoji)}</span><div><div class="eyebrow">${esc(s.symbol)} / FICTIONAL</div><h2>${esc(s.name)}</h2></div></div>${owner?`<button class="text-btn" data-action="x-stockEditor" data-id="${esc(s.id)}">Edit stock</button>`:''}</div><div class="stock-price">${points(s.price)} <span>points per share</span></div><span class="pill neutral">${s.mode==='random'?'Random movement · 15 minutes':'Price set by the OWNER'}</span>${stockChart(s)}<div class="stock-position">You own <b>${points(holdings[s.id]?.quantity)} shares</b> · Total cost <b>${points(holdings[s.id]?.cost)} pts</b></div><form id="tradeForm"><div class="form-grid"><label class="field">I want to<select name="side"><option value="buy">Buy shares</option><option name="color" value="${esc(g.color)}"></label>${g.kind==='quiz'?`${field('Maximum reward for a perfect score','reward',g.reward||0,'number','required min="0" max="50" step="1"')}${(g.questions||[]).map((q,i)=>`<fieldset class="quiz-question-editor"><legend>Question ${i+1}</legend><label class="field">Question<input name="q" value="${esc(q.text)}" required maxlength="200"></label>${q.options.map((o,n)=>field('Answer '+(n+1),'option',o,'text','required maxlength="100"')).join('')}<label class="field">Correct answer<select name="answer">${opts([[0,'Answer async function playQuiz(id){if(!gate())return;const r=await call('quizStart',{id});showModal(r.name,`<form><p class="form-note">Answer every question. async function loadAI(){if(aiLoading||!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return;const uid=state.me.id;aiLoading=true;aiError='';try{const r=await call('aiStatus',{},true);if(state.me?.id===uid)aiState=r;}catch(e){if(state.me?.id===uid)aiError=e.message;}finally{if(state.me?.id===uid){aiLoading=false;if(page==='ai')renderPage();}}}
function renderAI(){
 if(!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return signInPanel('Say hello to MonkeGPT.');
 const connected=aiState?.configured&&aiState?.enabled;
 return `<div class="page-heading between"><div><div class="eyebrow">A LITTLE HELP FROM YOUR MONKE</div><h1>MonkeGPT<span class="accent">.</span></h1><p>Ask a question. Share a picture. Make something good.</p></div><div class="row"><button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="x-newChat" ${aiSending?'disabled':''}>New chat</button>${state.me.role==='owner'?'<button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="x-aiSettings">Owner settings</button>':''}</div></div><section class="gpt-panel"><div class="gpt-top"><span class="gpt-mark">🐵</span><div><b>MonkeGPT</b><small>${connected?'Key saved · Gemini':aiLoading?'Checking connection…':'Waiting for owner setup'}</small></div><span class="pill neutral">${connected?'TEXT + IMAGES':'NOT CONNECTED'}</span></div><div class="gpt-messages" id="gptMessages" aria-live="polite">${aiMessages.length?aiMessages.map(m=>`<div class="gpt-message ${m.role}"><span class="gpt-author">${m.role==='user'?'You':m.source==='builtin'?'MonkeGPT · Built-in reply':'MonkeGPT'}</span>${m.image?`<img src="${esc(m.image)}" alt="Image you attached">`:''}<div>${esc(m.text)}</div>${m.seconds!=null?`<small class="gpt-reply-time">Answered in ${m.seconds}s</small>`:''}</div>`).join(''):`<div class="gpt-welcome"><span class="gpt-welcome-mark">✦</span><h2>What’s on your mind?</h2><p>Get help with an idea, understand a picture,<br>or ask something you’ve been wondering.</p><div class="gpt-suggestions">${['Help me brainstorm a game','Explain how fictional stocks work','Give me a fun quiz question'].map(t=>`<button data-action="x-aiSuggestion" data-id="${esc(t)}">${esc(t)} ↗</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`}${aiSending?thinkingMarkup():''}</div>${!connected&&!aiLoading?`<div class="gpt-setup notice">${!LIVE?'Local preview: AI calls are disabled. Connect a Gemini key on the deployed site.':'The owner needs to connect a Gemini API key before messages can be sent.'}${state.me.role==='owner'?'<button class="text-btn" data-action="x-aiSettings">Connect MonkeGPT</button>':''}</div>`:''}${aiError?`<p class="gpt-error" role="alert">${esc(aiError)}</p>`:''}<form id="gptForm" class="gpt-composer">${aiAttachment?`<div class="gpt-attachment"><img src="${esc(aiAttachment.data)}" alt="Selected attachment"><span>${esc(aiAttachment.name)}</span><button class="text-btn" data-action="x-removeAttachment" type="button">Remove</button></div>`:''}<label class="sr-only" for="gptPrompt">Message MonkeGPT</label><textarea id="gptPrompt" name="prompt" maxlength="6000" rows="2" placeholder="Ask MonkeGPT anything…" ${aiSending?'disabled':''}>${esc(aiDraft)}</textarea><div class="gpt-composer-bottom"><label class="gpt-upload ${aiSending?'disabled':''}">${icon('image')} Add picture<input id="gptImage" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" ${aiSending?'disabled':''}></label><button class="btn compact" type="submit" ${!connected||aiSending||aiImageBusy?'disabled':''}>${aiImageBusy?'Preparing image…':aiSending?'Sending…':aiError?'Try again':'Send'} ${icon('send')}</button></div></form><p class="gpt-footnote">Name-only replies are built in. Other questions and attached pictures are sent to Google Gemini. This chat stays only in this tab and clears on reload or logout. AI can make mistakes.</p></section>`;
}
async function showAISettings(){if(state.me?.role!=='owner')return;if(!aiState)await loadAI();const c=aiState||{model:'gemini-3.5-flash-lite',perUser:20,perSite:100};showModal('Connect MonkeGPT',`<form><p>Use a Gemini API key for text and image questions. TinyFish keys are for browser automation and cannot power this chat.</p><p class="form-note"><a href="https:\x2f\x2faistudio.google.com/api-keys" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Get a Gemini key from Google AI Studio ↗</a>. Provider limits and billing depend on your account.</p>${field(c.configured?'Replace API key (blank keeps the saved key)':'Gemini API key','key','','password','autocomplete="new-password" maxlength="256"')}${field('Gemini model ID','model',c.model||'gemini-3.5-flash-lite','text','required maxlength="78"')}<div class="form-grid">${field('AI requests per member / day','perUser',c.perUser||20,'number','required min="1" max="100"')}${field('AI requests across site / day','perSite',c.perSite||100,'number','required min="1" max="1000"')}</div><label class="check-label"><input name="enabled" type="checkbox" ${c.enabled?'checked':''}> Enable MonkeGPT</label>${c.configured?'<label class="check-label"><input name="removeKey" type="checkbox"> Remove saved key</label>':''}<p class="form-note">The key is saved privately on the server and is never sent back to members. First run AUTHORIZE_MONKEGPT once in Apps Script to allow AI requests. Temporary server failures get one automatic retry. Each provider attempt counts toward the limits; built-in name replies do not.</p>${formError()}<button class="btn full" type="submit">Save connection</button></form>`,async d=>{aiState=await call('aiSettings',{...Object.fromEntries(d),enabled:d.has('enabled'),removeKey:d.has('removeKey')});closeModal();if(page==='ai')renderPage();toast('MonkeGPT connection saved. Send a message to test it.');});}
async function prepareAIImage(file){
 if(!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp)$/.test(file.type)||file.size>8*1024*1024)throw new Error('Choose a PNG, JPG, or WebP image under 8 MB.');
 return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const img=new Image(),src=URL.createObjectURL(file);img.onload=()=>{try{if(img.width*img.height>32000000)throw new Error('Choose an image smaller than 32 megapixels.');const scale=Math.min(1,1280/Math.max(img.width,img.height)),canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.width*scale));canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.height*scale));const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);let data='';for(const quality of [.85,.7,.5,.3]){data=canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',quality);if(data.length<650000)break;}if(data.length>=650000)throw new Error('This picture is too detailed. Try a smaller one.');resolve({data,name:file.name});}catch(e){reject(e);}finally{URL.revokeObjectURL(src);}};img.onerror=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(src);reject(new Error('That image could not be opened.'));};img.src=src;});
}
async function sendAI(){
 if(aiSending||aiImageBusy||!aiState?.enabled||!aiState?.configured)return;const text=aiDraft.trim()||(aiAttachment?'Describe this picture.':'');if(!text)return;
 const uid=state.me.id,generation=aiGeneration,image=aiAttachment?.data||'';let previous=aiMessages.slice(-8).map(m=>({role:m.role,text:m.text.slice(0,6000)}));while(previous.reduce((n,m)=>n+m.text.length,0)+text.length>18000&&previous.length)previous=previous.slice(2);
 aiSending=true;aiStartedAt=performance.now();aiEstimate=aiDurations.length?Math.max(2,Math.round([...aiDurations].sort((a,b)=>a-b)[Math.floor(aiDurations.length/2)])):25;clearInterval(aiTimer);aiTimer=setInterval(updateAIThinking,250);aiError='';aiMessages.push({role:'user',text,image});aiDraft='';aiAttachment=null;renderPage();$('#gptMessages')?.scrollTo?.(0,999999);
 try{const r=await call('aiChat',{messages:[...previous,{role:'user',text}],image});if(state.me?.id===uid&&aiGeneration===generation){const seconds=Math.round((performance.now()-aiStartedAt)/100)/10;if(r.source!=='builtin'){aiDurations.push(seconds);aiDurations=aiDurations.slice(-5);}aiMessages.push({role:'assistant',source:r.source==='builtin'?'builtin':'gemini',seconds,text:r.text+(r.truncated?'\n\n[Reply length reached. Ask “continue” for more.]':'')});}}
 catch(e){if(state.me?.id===uid&&aiGeneration===generation){aiMessages.pop();aiDraft=text;aiAttachment=image?{data:image,name:'Your attached picture'}:null;aiError=e.message;}}
 finally{if(state.me?.id===uid&&aiGeneration===generation){aiSending=false;stopAIThinking();if(page==='ai'){renderPage();$('#gptMessages')?.scrollTo?.(0,999999);}}}
}
async function expansionAction(name,el){
 const id=el?.dataset.id;
 switch(name){
  case 'x-announcement':return showAnnouncementEditor();
  case 'x-removeAnnouncement':await call('announcementSave',{active:false});closeModal();await sync(false);return;
  case 'x-spin':return spinSlots();
  case 'x-refreshMarket':return loadMarket();
  case 'x-marketTab':marketTab=id;return renderPage();
  case 'x-selectStock':selectedStock=id;return renderPage();
  case 'x-stockEditor':return showStockEditor(id);
  case 'x-betEditor':return showBetEditor();
  case 'x-placeBet':return showBetEntry(id);
  case 'x-resolveBet':return showBetResolution(id);
  case 'x-claimBet':{const r=await call('betClaim',{id});expansionMe(r.me);await loadMarket();return toast(r.payout?`${r.payout} points collected.`:'Result collected. This prediction did not win.');}
  case 'x-flip':return showModal('Sun or moon?',`<form><label class="field">Pick a side<select name="choice"><option value="sun">Sun ☀</option><option value="moon">Moon ☾</option></select></label>${field('Stake in points','stake',10,'number','required min="1" max="100" step="1"')}<p>50% chance. A win returns 1.9× your stake, rounded down. A loss returns 0.</p>${formError()}<button type="submit" class="btn full">Confirm and flip</button></form>`,async d=>{const r=await economyCall('flip','randomBet',{choice:d.get('choice'),stake:Number(d.get('stake'))});if(!r)return;flipResult=r;closeModal();await loadMarket();});
  case 'x-addQuestion':readGameDraft();if(gameDraft.questions.length<5)gameDraft.questions.push({text:'',options:['','',''],answer:0});return drawGameBuilder();
  case 'x-removeQuestion':readGameDraft();gameDraft.questions.splice(Number(id),1);return drawGameBuilder();
  case 'x-playQuiz':return playQuiz(id);
  case 'x-aiSettings':return showAISettings();
  case 'x-newChat':if(aiSending)return;aiMessages=[];aiAttachment=null;aiDraft='';aiError='';aiGeneration++;return renderPage();
  case 'x-removeAttachment':aiAttachment=null;return renderPage();
  case 'x-aiSuggestion':aiDraft=id;renderPage();return $('#gptPrompt')?.focus();
 }
}
document.addEventListener('change',async e=>{
 if(e.target.id==='slotStake')slotStake=Number(e.target.value);
 if(e.target.id==='gameKind'){readGameDraft();if(gameDraft.kind==='quiz'&&!gameDraft.questions.length)gameDraft.questions=[{text:'',options:['','',''],answer:0}];drawGameBuilder();}
 if(e.target.id==='gptImage'&&e.target.files[0]){const generation=aiGeneration,uid=state.me?.id,file=e.target.files[0];aiImageBusy=true;if(page==='ai')renderPage();try{const attachment=await prepareAIImage(file);if(generation===aiGeneration&&state.me?.id===uid){aiAttachment=attachment;aiError='';}}catch(error){if(generation===aiGeneration&&state.me?.id===uid)aiError=error.message;}finally{if(generation===aiGeneration&&state.me?.id===uid){aiImageBusy=false;if(page==='ai')renderPage();}}}
});
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target.id==='gptPrompt')aiDraft=e.target.value;if(e.target.closest('#tradeForm')){const q=Number($('#tradeForm [name=quantity]').value),s=marketState.stocks.find(x=>x.id===selectedStock);$('#tradeEstimate').textContent=`${q||0} shares × ${points(s.price)} = ${points((q||0)*s.price)} points`;}});
document.addEventListener('submit',e=>{if(e.target.id==='tradeForm'){e.preventDefault();reviewTrade(e.target);}if(e.target.id==='gptForm'){e.preventDefault();sendAI();}});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.target.id==='gptPrompt'&&e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey&&!e.isComposing){e.preventDefault();sendAI();}});

function resetSocial(){socialData=null;socialLoading=false;socialError='';approvalData=null;approvalLoading=false;if(typeof closeDuelSession==='function')closeDuelSession();}
async function loadSocial(){if(socialLoading||!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return;socialLoading=true;const who=state.me.id;try{const r=await call('socialState',{},true);if(state.me?.id!==who)return;socialData=r;socialError='';expansionMe(r.me);if(typeof updateDuelRoom==='function')updateDuelRoom(r);}catch(e){if(state.me?.id===who)socialError=e.message;}finally{if(state.me?.id===who){socialLoading=false;if(['trading','duels'].includes(page))renderPage();}}}
async function loadApprovals(){if(approvalLoading||!Tribe.admin(state.me))return;approvalLoading=true;const who=state.me.id;try{const r=await call('approvalList',{},true);if(state.me?.id===who)approvalData=r.requests;}finally{if(state.me?.id===who){approvalLoading=false;if(page==='admin'&&adminTab==='approvals')renderPage();}}}
function bundleHTML(b){return `<strong class="asset-points">◉ ${points(b.points)} points</strong><div class="asset-items">${b.items.map(id=>{const i=state.shop.find(x=>x.id===id);return `<span class="asset-chip">${esc(i?.name||'Archived cosmetic')}</span>`;}).join('')||'<small class="muted">No cosmetics</small>'}</div>`;}

class="row">${incoming?`<button class="btn compact" data-action="s-acceptTrade" data-id="${esc(r.id)}">Review & accept</button>`:''}<button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="s-cancelTrade" data-id="${esc(r.id)}">${incoming?'Decline':'Cancel offer'}</button></div>`:''}</footer></article>`;}).join('')||'<div class="panel empty"><h3>Your next exchange starts here.</h3><p>Create an offer function selectableItems(name,items){return `<div class="trade-item-picker">${items.map(i=>`<label><input name="${name}" value="${esc(i.id)}" type="checkbox"><span>${esc(i.name)}<small>${esc(i.type)}</small></span></label>`).join('')||'<p class="muted small">No eligible cosmetics.</p>'}</div>`;}
function showTradeCreator(){

}
function readableApproval(v){if(typeof v==='string'&&v.startsWith('data:image/'))return '[Uploaded image]';if(Array.isArray(v))return v.map(readableApproval);if(v&&typeof v==='object'){const out={};Object.keys(v).forEach(k=>out[k]=readableApproval(v[k]));return out;}return v;}
async function showApproval(id){const who=state.me?.id,r=(await call('approvalGet',{id})).request;if(state.me?.id!==who)return;const images=[r.payload.logo,r.action==='shopSave'&&r.payload.type==='banner'?r.payload.value:''].filter(v=>typeof v==='string'&&(/^(data:image\/|https:)/.test(v)));

If the affected settings changed, approval is blocked and the co-owner must submit a fresh request.</p></div>${r.status==='pending'&&state.me.role==='owner'?`<form><label class="field">Decision<select name="decision"><option value="approve">Approve this change</option><option value="reject">Reject this change</option></select></label>${formError()}<button class="btn full" type="submit">Confirm decision</button></form>`:''}`,async d=>{await call('approvalDecide',{id,decision:d.get('decision')});closeModal();await sync(false);await loadApprovals();if(page==='dms')await loadMessages();toast(d.get('decision')==='approve'?'Change approved and applied.':'Request rejected.');});
}

 showModal('Delete @'+u.username,`<form><p>Permanently delete <b>${esc(u.displayName)}</b>'s account and sign-in sessions. Their messages are removed. Type <b>${esc(u.username)}</b> below.</p>${field('Exact username','confirm','','text','required autocomplete="off"')}${formError()}<button class="btn danger full" type="submit">Delete account permanently</button></form>`,async d=>{let r;do{r=await call('accountDelete',{userId:id,confirm:d.get('confirm')});const b=$('#dialog button[type=submit]');if(b)b.textContent=r.pending?'Removing account data…':'Account removed';}while(r.pending);closeModal();state.loaded.members=false;await ensureData(['members']);await afterAdmin();toast('Account deleted.');});
}
async function socialAction(name,el){const id=el?.dataset.id;switch(name){
 case 's-refreshSocial':return loadSocial();case 's-createTrade':return showTradeCreator();case 's-tradeFilter':tradeMode=id;return renderPage();
 case 's-cancelTrade':{await call('tradeCancel',{id});await loadSocial();return toast('Offer closed. Held assets returned.');}
 case 's-acceptTrade':{const r=socialData?.trades.find(t=>t.id===id);if(!r)return;return showModal('Review your exchange',`<form><div class="trade-sides"><div><span class="eyebrow">YOU GIVE</span>${bundleHTML(r.want)}</div><span class="trade-arrow">⇄</span><div><span class="eyebrow">YOU RECEIVE</span>${bundleHTML(r.offer)}</div></div><p>Accepting completes this exchange with ${esc(userById(r.from).displayName)}. Review both sides carefully.</p>${formError()}<button class="btn full" type="submit">Accept & exchange</button></form>`,async()=>{await call('tradeAccept',{id});closeModal();await loadSocial();toast('Exchange complete.');});}
 case 's-refreshApprovals':return loadApprovals();case 's-reviewApproval':return showApproval(id);case 's-deleteAccount':return showDeleteAccount(id);
 default:if(name.startsWith('d-'))return duelAction(name,el);
 }}
