/* Monke Arena: an original two-human, deterministic WebRTC arena. No bots. */
let duelSession=null;
const duelOpen=r=>['challenged','connecting','playing'].includes(r.status);
function renderDuels(){
 if(!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return signInPanel('Your rival is waiting.');
 if(duelSession)return renderArena();
 const rooms=socialData?.duels||[];
 return `<div class="page-heading between"><div><div class="eyebrow">MONKE ARENA / PLAYER VS PLAYER</div><h1>Duels<span class="accent">.</span></h1><p>Two players. Equal stakes. One winner.</p></div><button class="btn" data-action="d-create">${icon('plus')} Challenge a player</button></div><section class="duel-hero"><div><span class="eyebrow">ORIGINAL 1V1 ARENA SHOOTER</span><h2>Take cover.<br>Take the round.</h2><p>Move through the jungle outpost, switch between three weapons, and outplay another tribe member. Both players need to open this tab.</p><div class="duel-rules"><span>FIRST TO 3 ROUNDS</span><span>90 SECOND LIMIT</span><span>3 WEAPONS</span><span>1–1,000 POINT STAKE</span></div></div><div class="arena-graphic" aria-hidden="true"><b>1v1</b></div></section><div class="section-heading"><h3>Your challenges</h3><button class="text-btn" data-action="s-refreshSocial">Refresh</button></div>${socialError?`<p class="inline-error">${esc(socialError)}</p>`:''}<div class="duel-cards">${rooms.map(r=>{const slot=r.players.indexOf(state.me.id),other=userById(r.players[1-slot]);return `<article class="duel-card"><div><div class="row">${avatar(other)}<b>${esc(other.displayName)}</b><span class="pill neutral">${esc(r.status)}</span></div><p>${r.status==='challenged'?(slot===0?'Waiting for your opponent to accept.':'You have been challenged. Review the stake before accepting.'):r.status==='connecting'?'Both stakes held. Open the arena to connect.':r.status==='playing'?'The match has started.':esc(r.reason||'Match closed.')}</p><small>${!duelOpen(r)&&r.winner!=null?(r.winner===slot?'You won the pot.':'Your opponent won the pot.'):'Each player stakes '+points(r.stake)+' points.'}</small></div><div class="duel-pot">◉ ${points(r.stake*2)}<small style="display:block">TOTAL POT</small></div><div class="row">${r.status==='challenged'&&slot===1?`<button class="btn compact" data-action="d-accept" data-id="${esc(r.id)}">Review & accept</button>`:''}${r.status==='connecting'?`<button class="btn compact" data-action="d-join" data-id="${esc(r.id)}">Enter arena</button>`:''}${r.status==='playing'?`<button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="d-forfeit" data-id="${esc(r.id)}">Forfeit</button>`:duelOpen(r)?`<button class="btn secondary compact" data-action="d-cancel" data-id="${esc(r.id)}">${slot===1&&r.status==='challenged'?'Decline':'Cancel'}</button>`:''}</div></article>`;}).join('')||`<div class="panel empty"><h3>${socialLoading?'Loading challenges…':'Call out your next rival.'}</h3><p>Send a challenge and ask your opponent to open Duels.</p></div>`}</div><div class="panel" style="margin-top:22px"><h3>Know the match before you stake.</h3><p class="form-note">Both stakes are held before play. The winner receives the whole pot; ties refund both players. At the time limit, round wins decide, then remaining health. Leaving a live match can lose your stake. A disconnect lasting 60 seconds can award the pot to the remaining player.</p><p class="form-note">Live play uses a direct connection between your browsers. Some school, work, or restricted networks cannot connect. Failed setup and unverifiable matches refund both stakes when next checked. Keep this tab visible during a match. Site points have no cash value.</p></div>`;
}
function renderArena(){const s=duelSession,r=s.room;return `<div class="page-heading between"><div><div class="eyebrow">MONKE ARENA / JUNGLE OUTPOST</div><h1>Match room<span class="accent">.</span></h1></div><span class="pill">◉ ${points(r.stake*2)} point pot</span></div><section class="arena-wrap"><div class="arena-toolbar"><div><b>You</b> <small>vs</small> <b>${esc(userById(r.players[1-s.slot]).displayName)}</b></div><div id="arenaStatus">Connecting players…</div><button class="text-btn" data-action="d-leave">${r.status==='playing'?'Forfeit':'Leave room'}</button></div><canvas id="arenaCanvas" class="arena-canvas" width="960" height="600" tabindex="0" aria-label="First person duel arena. WASD to move, Q and E to turn, space to fire, R to reload, 1 to 3 for weapons."></canvas><div id="arenaNetwork" class="arena-network" role="status"></div><div class="arena-controls"><span>WASD move · right-drag or Q/E aim · click/space fire · R reload · Shift sprint</span><div class="weapon-choices">${DuelCore.guns.map((g,i)=>`<button data-action="d-weapon" data-id="${i}" aria-label="Select ${g.name}">${i+1} ${g.short}</button>`).join('')}</div></div><div class="arena-touch"><div><button data-duel-key="left">◀</button> <button data-duel-key="forward">▲</button> <button data-duel-key="back">▼</button> <button data-duel-key="right">▶</button></div><div><button data-duel-key="reload">R</button> <button class="fire" data-duel-key="fire">FIRE</button></div></div></section><div id="arenaResult"></div><p class="form-note">Drag the arena with your right mouse button or a finger to aim. Touch users can hold movement and fire buttons together. Keep the arena focused and this tab visible. Your browser shares connection information directly with your opponent.</p>`;}
function duelRequestId(){return crypto.randomUUID();}
async function duelAction(name,el){const id=el?.dataset.id;switch(name){
 case 'd-create':{if(!gate())return;const others=state.members.filter(u=>u.id!==state.me.id&&!u.banned&&u.role!=='pending');let requestId=duelRequestId(),signature='';return showModal('Challenge a player',`<form><label class="field">Opponent<select name="to" required>${opts(others.map(u=>[u.id,u.displayName+' (@'+u.username+')']))}</select></label>${field('Each player stakes','stake',25,'number','min="1" max="1000" step="1" required')}<p class="form-note">Your stake is held when you send this challenge. Your opponent stakes the same amount when accepting. Invitations expire after 5 minutes. Both of you must open Duels to play.</p>${formError()}<button class="btn full" type="submit" ${others.length?'':'disabled'}>Hold stake & challenge</button></form>`,async d=>{const data={to:d.get('to'),stake:Number(d.get('stake'))},next=JSON.stringify(data);if(signature&&signature!==next)requestId=duelRequestId();signature=next;const r=await call('duelCreate',{...data,requestId});closeModal();expansionMe(r.me);await loadSocial();toast('Challenge sent. Ask your opponent to open Duels.');});}
 case 'd-accept':{const r=socialData?.duels.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!r)return;return showModal('Accept this duel?',`<form><p>You and <b>${esc(userById(r.players[0]).displayName)}</b> each stake <b>${points(r.stake)} points</b>.</p><p>The winner receives <b>${points(r.stake*2)} points</b>. First to 3 rounds; 90-second limit. Leaving after play starts can lose your stake.</p>${formError()}<button class="btn full" type="submit">Hold ${points(r.stake)} points & enter arena</button></form>`,async()=>{const result=await call('duelAccept',{id});closeModal();expansionMe(result.me);await loadSocial();if(result.duel.status==='connecting')await openDuelSession(result.duel,result.serverTime);});}
 case 'd-join':{const r=await call('duelSync',{id},true);if(r.duel.status!=='connecting')return loadSocial();return openDuelSession(r.duel,r.serverTime);}
 case 'd-cancel':await call('duelCancel',{id});await loadSocial();return toast('Challenge closed. Held stakes refunded.');
 case 'd-forfeit':case 'd-leave':{const r=duelSession?.room||socialData?.duels.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!r)return;if(!duelOpen(r)){closeDuelSession();renderPage();return loadSocial();}return showModal(r.status==='playing'?'Forfeit your stake?':'Cancel this connection?',`<form><p>${r.status==='playing'?'Your opponent receives the pot if the match is still active when confirmed.':'Both held stakes will be refunded if play has not started. If play has started, you must explicitly forfeit instead.'}</p>${formError()}<button class="btn ${r.status==='playing'?'danger':''} full" type="submit">${r.status==='playing'?'Forfeit match':'Cancel & refund'}</button></form>`,async()=>{const result=await call(r.status==='playing'?'duelForfeit':'duelCancel',{id:r.id});closeModal();expansionMe(result.me);closeDuelSession();await loadSocial();renderPage();});}
 case 'd-weapon':if(duelSession)duelSession.weapon=Math.max(0,Math.min(2,Number(id)));return;
 case 'd-back':closeDuelSession();renderPage();return loadSocial();
 }}
function closeDuelSession(){const s=duelSession;if(!s)return;duelSession=null;clearTimeout(s.poll);cancelAnimationFrame(s.raf);s.abort?.abort();try{s.channel?.close();s.pc?.close();}catch{}s.keys={};}
function updateDuelRoom(result){const s=duelSession;if(!s)return;const r=result.duel||(result.duels||[]).find(r=>r.id===s.room.id);if(!r)return;s.room=r;if(result.serverTime)s.offset=result.serverTime-Date.now();if(result.me)expansionMe(result.me);if(r.status==='playing'&&!s.activated){s.activated=true;s.lastFrameAt=performance.now();}if(!duelOpen(r)){try{s.channel?.close();s.pc?.close();}catch{}s.keys={};}updateArenaHUD();}
function waitICE(pc){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const finish=()=>{if(!/a=candidate:/.test(pc.localDescription?.sdp||''))reject(new Error('No direct network route was found. Leave the room to refund held stakes.'));else resolve();};if(pc.iceGatheringState==='complete')return finish();const done=()=>{clearTimeout(timer);pc.removeEventListener('icegatheringstatechange',changed);finish();},changed=()=>{if(pc.iceGatheringState==='complete')done();},timer=setTimeout(done,6500);pc.addEventListener('icegatheringstatechange',changed);});}
async function openDuelSession(room,serverTime){
 if(duelSession?.room.id===room.id)return;if(duelSession)throw Error('Finish your current match first.');if(!window.RTCPeerConnection)throw Error('This browser does not support live duels. Cancel the challenge to refund stakes.');
 const s={room,slot:room.players.indexOf(state.me.id),offset:(serverTime||Date.now())-Date.now(),sim:DuelCore.initial(),log:'',inputs:[new Map(),new Map()],nextSend:0,keys:{},yaw:0,weapon:0,syncBusy:false,signalBusy:false,error:'',network:'Connecting directly to your opponent…',lastFrameAt:performance.now(),lastTick:performance.now(),abort:new AbortController()};s.yaw=s.sim.players[s.slot].yaw;duelSession=s;renderPage();
 try{s.pc=new RTCPeerConnection({iceServers:[{urls:'stun:stun.l.google.com:19302'},{urls:'stun:stun1.l.google.com:19302'}]});s.pc.onconnectionstatechange=()=>{if(duelSession!==s)return;if(['failed','disconnected'].includes(s.pc.connectionState))s.network='Peer connection interrupted. Waiting for the match server…';};s.pc.ondatachannel=e=>bindDuelChannel(s,e.channel);if(s.slot===0){bindDuelChannel(s,s.pc.createDataChannel('monke-arena-v1',{ordered:true}));await s.pc.setLocalDescription(await s.pc.createOffer());await waitICE(s.pc);if(duelSession!==s)return;const r=await call('duelSignal',{id:room.id,signal:{type:s.pc.localDescription.type,sdp:s.pc.localDescription.sdp}},true);if(duelSession===s)updateDuelRoom(r);}if(duelSession===s)pollDuel(s);
 }catch(e){if(duelSession===s){s.error=e.message;s.network='Could not create a direct connection. Leave the room to request a refund before play.';pollDuel(s);}}
}
function bindDuelChannel(s,ch){if(s.channel){ch.close();return;}s.channel=ch;ch.onopen=async()=>{if(duelSession!==s)return;s.network='Connected. Waiting for both players to be ready…';try{const r=await call('duelReady',{id:s.room.id},true);if(duelSession===s)updateDuelRoom(r);}catch(e){s.error=e.message;}};
 ch.onclose=()=>{if(duelSession===s&&duelOpen(s.room))s.network='Connection closed. Checking the match result…';};
 ch.onmessage=e=>{if(duelSession!==s||typeof e.data!=='string'||e.data.length>150)return;try{const m=JSON.parse(e.data);if(m.t!=='i'||!Number.isInteger(m.f)||m.f<0||m.f>=DuelCore.maxFrames||m.f>s.sim.frame+30)return;DuelCore.decode(m.v);const other=1-s.slot,old=s.inputs[other].get(m.f);if(old&&old!==m.v){s.error='Your opponent sent conflicting input. Verification will check the match.';return;}if(m.f>=s.sim.frame)s.inputs[other].set(m.f,m.v);}catch{}};
}
async function pollDuel(s){if(duelSession!==s||s.syncBusy||!duelOpen(s.room))return;s.syncBusy=true;try{
 const r=await call('duelSync',{id:s.room.id,...(s.room.status==='playing'?{log:s.log}:{})},true);if(duelSession!==s)return;updateDuelRoom(r);
 if(s.room.status==='connecting'&&s.pc&&!s.signalBusy){const remote=s.room.signals?.[1-s.slot];if(remote&&!s.pc.remoteDescription){s.signalBusy=true;await s.pc.setRemoteDescription(remote);if(s.slot===1){await s.pc.setLocalDescription(await s.pc.createAnswer());await waitICE(s.pc);if(duelSession!==s)return;const answer=await call('duelSignal',{id:s.room.id,signal:{type:s.pc.localDescription.type,sdp:s.pc.localDescription.sdp}},true);if(duelSession===s)updateDuelRoom(answer);}s.signalBusy=false;}}
 s.error='';
 }catch(e){if(duelSession===s)s.error=e.message;}finally{s.syncBusy=false;if(duelSession===s&&duelOpen(s.room))s.poll=setTimeout(()=>pollDuel(s),s.room.status==='playing'&&!s.sim.done?9000:2500);}
}
function mountDuelArena(){const s=duelSession,canvas=$('#arenaCanvas');if(!s||!canvas||s.canvas===canvas)return;s.canvas=canvas;s.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');const signal=s.abort.signal,keys={KeyW:'forward',ArrowUp:'forward',KeyS:'back',ArrowDown:'back',KeyA:'left',KeyD:'right',Space:'fire',KeyR:'reload',ShiftLeft:'sprint',ShiftRight:'sprint',KeyQ:'turnLeft',ArrowLeft:'turnLeft',KeyE:'turnRight',ArrowRight:'turnRight'};
 const key=(e,down)=>{if(page!=='duels'||$('#dialog').open||/INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT/.test(e.target.tagName))return;if(keys[e.code]){e.preventDefault();s.keys[keys[e.code]]=down;}if(down&&/^Digit[123]$/.test(e.code))s.weapon=Number(e.code.slice(-1))-1;};document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>key(e,true),{signal});document.addEventListener('keyup',e=>key(e,false),{signal});window.addEventListener('blur',()=>s.keys={},{signal});document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)s.keys={};},{signal});
 canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault(),{signal});canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{canvas.focus();if(e.button===2||e.pointerType==='touch'){s.lookPointer=e.pointerId;s.lookX=e.clientX;canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}else if(e.button===0)s.keys.fire=true;},{signal});canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(s.lookPointer===e.pointerId){s.yaw+=(e.clientX-s.lookX)*.007;s.lookX=e.clientX;}},{signal});window.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{if(s.lookPointer===e.pointerId)s.lookPointer=null;if(e.button===0)s.keys.fire=false;},{signal});canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{s.keys={};s.lookPointer=null;},{signal});
 $$('[data-duel-key]').forEach(b=>{b.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();b.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);s.keys[b.dataset.duelKey]=true;},{signal});for(const evt of ['pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'])b.addEventListener(evt,()=>{s.keys[b.dataset.duelKey]=false;},{signal});});canvas.focus();
 const frame=t=>{if(duelSession!==s)return;const dt=Math.min(.05,(t-s.lastTick)/1000);s.lastTick=t;s.yaw+=((s.keys.turnRight?1:0)-(s.keys.turnLeft?1:0))*dt*2.3;advanceDuel(s,t);if(!s.lastDraw||t-s.lastDraw>=33){s.lastDraw=t;drawArena(s);}if(!s.lastHUD||t-s.lastHUD>=200){s.lastHUD=t;updateArenaHUD();}s.raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);};s.raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
function advanceDuel(s,t){if(s.room.status!=='playing'||s.sim.done||s.channel?.readyState!=='open')return;const elapsed=Date.now()+s.offset-s.room.startAt;if(elapsed<0)return;const target=Math.min(DuelCore.maxFrames,Math.floor(elapsed/DuelCore.tickMs));
 while(s.nextSend<=Math.min(target+2,s.sim.frame+5,DuelCore.maxFrames-1)){const f=s.nextSend++,v=DuelCore.input(s.keys,s.yaw,s.weapon);s.inputs[s.slot].set(f,v);s.channel.send(JSON.stringify({t:'i',f,v}));}
 let count=0;while(s.sim.frame<target&&count++<8){const f=s.sim.frame,a=s.inputs[0].get(f),b=s.inputs[1].get(f);if(!a||!b)break;const round=s.sim.round;DuelCore.step(s.sim,a,b);s.log+=a+b;s.inputs[0].delete(f);s.inputs[1].delete(f);s.lastFrameAt=t;if(round!==s.sim.round)s.yaw=s.sim.players[s.slot].yaw;if(s.sim.done){clearTimeout(s.poll);if(!s.syncBusy)pollDuel(s);break;}}
}
function updateArenaHUD(){const s=duelSession;if(!s||!s.canvas)return;const r=s.room,waiting=$('#arenaNetwork'),status=$('#arenaStatus'),leave=$('[data-action="d-leave"]');if(!waiting||!status)return;let headline='',detail='';
 if(!duelOpen(r)){status.textContent='Match complete';const result=$('#arenaResult');if(result&&!result.dataset.done){result.dataset.done='1';result.innerHTML=`<div class="arena-result"><strong>${r.winner==null?'Stakes refunded':r.winner===s.slot?'You won!':'Opponent wins'}</strong><p>${esc(r.reason)}</p><p>${r.winner===s.slot?'The '+points(r.stake*2)+' point pot is in your balance.':r.winner==null?'Each funded player received their stake back.':'Your opponent received the '+points(r.stake*2)+' point pot.'}</p><button class="btn" data-action="d-back">Back to Duels</button></div>`;}if(leave)leave.textContent='Back to lobby';headline='Match complete';detail=r.winner==null?'Stakes refunded':r.winner===s.slot?'Victory':'Defeat';}
 else if(r.status==='connecting'){status.textContent='Connecting';headline=s.network;detail=s.error||'Both players must enter the arena. Cancel before play to refund stakes.';}
 else if(r.status==='playing'){if(leave)leave.textContent='Forfeit';const remaining=Math.ceil((r.startAt-Date.now()-s.offset)/1000);status.textContent='Live · '+s.sim.wins[s.slot]+' : '+s.sim.wins[1-s.slot];if(remaining>0){headline='Ready in '+remaining;detail='Click the arena to focus. WASD to move, Q/E or right-drag to aim.';}else if(s.sim.done){headline='Verifying both match records…';detail=s.error||'The server checks the result before awarding points.';}else if(s.channel?.readyState!=='open'||performance.now()-s.lastFrameAt>2200){headline='Waiting for your opponent…';detail=s.error||'Keep the tab open. A 60-second disconnect can forfeit the match.';}else if(s.sim.pause){headline=s.sim.players[s.slot].hp<=0?'Round lost':'Round complete';detail='Next round in '+Math.ceil(s.sim.pause/20)+'s';}}
 const html=headline?`<b>${esc(headline)}</b><small>${esc(detail)}</small>`:'';if(waiting.innerHTML!==html)waiting.innerHTML=html;$$('[data-action="d-weapon"]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',Number(b.dataset.id)===s.weapon));
}
function drawArena(s){
 const c=s.ctx,w=960,h=600,p=s.sim.players[s.slot],enemy=s.sim.players[1-s.slot],yaw=s.yaw,fov=Math.PI/2.8,depth=[],columns=240,strip=w/columns;
 const sky=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h/2);sky.addColorStop(0,'#081322');sky.addColorStop(1,'#386076');c.fillStyle=sky;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h/2);const floor=c.createLinearGradient(0,h/2,0,h);floor.addColorStop(0,'#15252a');floor.addColorStop(1,'#314544');c.fillStyle=floor;c.fillRect(0,h/2,w,h/2);c.strokeStyle='#4c675044';for(let y=h/2+12;y<h;y+=(y-h/2)*.3){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(0,y);c.lineTo(w,y);c.stroke();}
 for(let i=0;i<columns;i++){const angle=yaw+Math.atan((i/columns-.5)*2*Math.tan(fov/2)),dx=Math.cos(angle),dy=Math.sin(angle);let mx=Math.floor(p.x),my=Math.floor(p.y),sx=dx<0?-1:1,sy=dy<0?-1:1,ddx=Math.abs(1/dx),ddy=Math.abs(1/dy),tx=(dx<0?p.x-mx:mx+1-p.x)*ddx,ty=(dy<0?p.y-my:my+1-p.y)*ddy,side=0,type='1',d=20;
  for(let j=0;j<40;j++){if(tx<ty){mx+=sx;d=tx;tx+=ddx;side=0;}else{my+=sy;d=ty;ty+=ddy;side=1;}type=DuelCore.map[my]?.[mx]||'1';if(type!=='0')break;}d=Math.max(.05,d*Math.cos(angle-yaw));depth[i]=d;const size=Math.min(h*3,h*.85/d),top=(h-size)/2,shade=Math.max(.2,1-d/23)*(side?.75:1),crate=type==='2',rgb=crate?[127,110,64]:[57,108,120];c.fillStyle=`rgb(${rgb.map(x=>Math.round(x*shade)).join(',')})`;c.fillRect(i*strip,top,strip+1,size);let hit=side?p.x+(d/Math.cos(angle-yaw))*dx:p.y+(d/Math.cos(angle-yaw))*dy;hit-=Math.floor(hit);if(hit<.035||hit>.965){c.fillStyle='#0005';c.fillRect(i*strip,top,strip+1,size);}c.fillStyle=crate?'#b6a27344':'#7cbdc744';c.fillRect(i*strip,top,strip+1,Math.max(1,size*.025));c.fillStyle='#0003';c.fillRect(i*strip,top+size*.5,strip+1,Math.max(1,size*.012));
 }
 let ea=Math.atan2(enemy.y-p.y,enemy.x-p.x)-yaw;while(ea>Math.PI)ea-=Math.PI*2;while(ea<-Math.PI)ea+=Math.PI*2;const ed=Math.hypot(enemy.x-p.x,enemy.y-p.y)*Math.cos(ea);
 if(Math.abs(ea)<fov&&ed>.2&&enemy.hp>0){const ex=w/2+Math.tan(ea)/Math.tan(fov/2)*w/2,eh=h*.7/ed,ew=eh*.5,top=h/2-eh*.45;for(let x=Math.max(0,Math.floor(ex-ew/2));x<Math.min(w,ex+ew/2);x+=strip){if(ed>depth[Math.min(columns-1,Math.floor(x/strip))]+.12)continue;const u=(x-ex)/ew; c.fillStyle='#092d3d';c.fillRect(x,top+eh*.29,strip+1,eh*.55);if(Math.abs(u)<.35){c.fillStyle='#a7d8e7';c.fillRect(x,top,strip+1,eh*.3);c.fillStyle='#d2f75b';c.fillRect(x,top+eh*.11,strip+1,eh*.075);}c.fillStyle='#405f78';c.fillRect(x,top+eh*.32,strip+1,eh*.33);if(Math.abs(u)>.1&&Math.abs(u)<.4){c.fillStyle='#152734';c.fillRect(x,top+eh*.78,strip+1,eh*.25);}}}
 // Angular, illustrated weapon silhouette. The three guns have distinct shapes.
 const gun=DuelCore.guns[s.weapon],bob=(s.keys.forward||s.keys.back||s.keys.left||s.keys.right)?Math.sin(s.sim.frame*.6)*4:0,gx=w*.66,gy=h+bob+(p.reloading?25:0);c.save();c.translate(gx,gy);c.fillStyle='#14212d';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(-75,0);c.lineTo(-35,-135);c.lineTo(14,-192);c.lineTo(48,-191);c.lineTo(95,-110);c.lineTo(165,0);c.closePath();c.fill();c.fillStyle='#49626c';c.fillRect(-15,-150,s.weapon===1?80:50,90);c.fillStyle='#0b1823';c.fillRect(10,-206,s.weapon===1?35:20,95);c.fillStyle=gun.color;c.fillRect(-15,-118,50,7);c.fillStyle='#89a8b4';c.fillRect(2,-160,35,14);if(s.weapon===2){c.fillStyle='#202c42';c.fillRect(-9,-181,58,22);c.strokeStyle=gun.color;c.strokeRect(-5,-181,10,22);}if(p.flash&&s.room.status==='playing'&&!s.sim.pause){c.fillStyle='#fff7bc';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(20,-203);c.lineTo(3,-242);c.lineTo(24,-229);c.lineTo(41,-248);c.lineTo(39,-210);c.closePath();c.fill();}c.restore();
 c.strokeStyle='#e6f4da';c.lineWidth=2;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(w/2-11,h/2);c.lineTo(w/2-4,h/2);c.moveTo(w/2+4,h/2);c.lineTo(w/2+11,h/2);c.moveTo(w/2,h/2-11);c.lineTo(w/2,h/2-4);c.moveTo(w/2,h/2+4);c.lineTo(w/2,h/2+11);c.stroke();if(s.sim.events.some(e=>e.type==='hit'&&e.player===s.slot)){c.fillStyle='#ff49662a';c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);}
 c.fillStyle='#08121dd9';c.fillRect(w/2-95,16,190,52);c.textAlign='center';c.font='bold 25px sans-serif';c.fillStyle='#d2f75b';c.fillText(s.sim.wins[s.slot]+'   :   '+s.sim.wins[1-s.slot],w/2,45);c.font='11px sans-serif';c.fillStyle='#b6ccd6';c.fillText(Math.max(0,90-Math.floor(s.sim.frame/20))+'s · FIRST TO 3',w/2,60);c.textAlign='left';c.fillStyle='#08121ddd';c.fillRect(20,h-78,196,56);c.fillStyle='#d2f75b';c.font='bold 24px sans-serif';c.fillText('+ '+p.hp,34,h-45);c.fillStyle='#33474b';c.fillRect(34,h-37,168,5);c.fillStyle='#b9f451';c.fillRect(34,h-37,168*p.hp/100,5);c.textAlign='right';c.fillStyle='#edf6f9';c.font='bold 27px sans-serif';c.fillText(p.reloading?'RELOADING':p.ammo[s.weapon]+' / '+gun.ammo,w-24,h-42);c.font='12px sans-serif';c.fillStyle=gun.color;c.fillText(gun.name.toUpperCase(),w-24,h-23);c.textAlign='left';
 c.fillStyle='#07111cc9';c.fillRect(20,18,106,106);DuelCore.map.forEach((row,y)=>[...row].forEach((cell,x)=>{if(cell!=='0'){c.fillStyle=cell==='2'?'#8c815b':'#416573';c.fillRect(23+x*6.7,21+y*6.7,6,6);}}));c.fillStyle='#d2f75b';c.beginPath();c.arc(23+p.x*6.7,21+p.y*6.7,3,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();c.strokeStyle='#d2f75b';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(23+p.x*6.7,21+p.y*6.7);c.lineTo(23+(p.x+Math.cos(yaw))*6.7,21+(p.y+Math.sin(yaw))*6.7);c.stroke();
}

/* v1.6 skill arcade. Coins use the existing account point balance. */
let arcadeData=null,arcadeLoading=false,arcadeError='',arcadeView='hub',arcadeRun=null,arcadeGeneration=0,arcadeSelection={game:'lock',difficulty:'easy'},arcadeStartRequest=null,arcadePending=null,arcadeSound=false,arcadeAudio=null;
function stopArcadeLocal(){const s=arcadeRun;if(s){cancelAnimationFrame(s.raf);s.abort?.abort();}arcadeRun=null;}
function resetArcade(){arcadeGeneration++;stopArcadeLocal();arcadeData=null;arcadeLoading=false;arcadeError='';arcadePending=null;arcadeStartRequest=null;arcadeView='hub';if(document.querySelector('#dialog')?.classList.contains('arcade-dialog'))closeModal();}
function arcadePendingKey(){return 'tribe.arcade.claim.'+state.me.id;}
function restoreArcadePending(){if(arcadePending||!state.me)return;try{const p=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(arcadePendingKey())||'null');if(p?.runId&&Array.isArray(p.inputs))arcadePending=p;}catch{}}
async function loadArcade(){if(arcadeLoading||!state.me||state.me.role==='pending')return;const who=state.me.id;arcadeLoading=true;restoreArcadePending();try{const r=await call('arcadeState',{},true);if(state.me?.id!==who)return;arcadeData=r.arcade;arcadeError='';expansionMe(r.me);}catch(e){if(state.me?.id===who)arcadeError=e.message;}finally{if(state.me?.id===who){arcadeLoading=false;if(page==='minigames')renderPage();}}}
function lockArt(){return `<svg viewBox="0 0 320 280" role="img" aria-label="A glowing circular timing lock"><circle cx="160" cy="145" r="108" fill="none" stroke="#c8f66b" stroke-opacity=".07" stroke-width="25"/><circle cx="160" cy="145" r="100" fill="none" stroke="#35503a" stroke-width="10"/><circle cx="160" cy="145" r="100" fill="none" stroke="#c8f66b" stroke-width="10" stroke-dasharray="225 404" transform="rotate(-90 160 145)" stroke-linecap="round"/><circle cx="245" cy="92" r="14" fill="#ffc971"/><path d="M160 38v25" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M141 131v-15a19 19 0 0 1 38 0v15" fill="none" stroke="#d6eac0" stroke-width="6"/><rect x="130" y="127" width="60" height="51" rx="14" fill="#c8f66b"/><circle cx="160" cy="150" r="6" fill="#172510"/><path d="M160 151v10" stroke="#172510" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round"/><text x="160" y="213" fill="#abc69f" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" letter-spacing="3">ONE TAP. STAY LOCKED IN.</text></svg>`;}
function arcadeArt(id){return id==='echo'?'<div class="echo-art" aria-hidden="true"><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i></div>':id==='dodge'?'<div class="dodge-art" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i><i></i><b></b><span>▧</span><span>▧</span></div>':'<div class="mini-slot-art" aria-hidden="true"><b>7</b><b>7</b><b>7</b></div>';}
function bestLabel(game){if(!arcadeData)return 'Your next personal best starts here';for(const d of [...ArcadeCore.tiers].reverse())if(arcadeData.bests[game+':'+d.id]?.wins)return d.name+' cleared · '+arcadeData.bests[game+':'+d.id].wins+' wins';return 'No clears yet. Make the first one yours.';}
function renderMinigames(){
 if(arcadeView==='slots')return `<button class="text-btn arcade-back" data-action="a-hub">← All minigames</button>`+renderSlots();
 const daily=arcadeData,member=state.me&&state.me.role!=='pending';return `<div class="page-heading between"><div><div class="eyebrow">THE TRIBE ARCADE / PICK YOUR CHALLENGE</div><h1>Small games.<br>Big monke energy<span class="accent">.</span></h1><p>Find your rhythm. Beat your best. Bring home the coins.</p></div><span class="pill neutral">4 GAMES · INSTANT PLAY</span></div><section class="arcade-feature"><div class="arcade-feature-copy"><div class="row"><span class="arcade-new">NEW</span><span class="eyebrow">TIMING / ONE BUTTON</span></div><h2>Lock Pop<span>.</span></h2><p>A Pop the Lock–style challenge. Tap as the white marker meets the yellow target. Every hit reverses direction. Clear every pin to crack it.</p><div class="arcade-tags"><span>FREE TO PLAY</span><span>4 DIFFICULTIES</span><span>+10–60 COINS</span></div><button class="btn" data-action="a-setup" data-id="lock">${icon('lock')} Play Lock Pop <span>↗</span></button><small>${esc(bestLabel('lock'))}</small></div><div class="arcade-lock-art">${lockArt()}</div></section><section class="arcade-rewards"><div><span class="eyebrow">HARDER CLEAR. BIGGER REWARD.</span><h3>Make every win count.</h3><p>Coins are your existing site points. Skill games have no entry cost.</p></div><div class="arcade-tier-strip">${ArcadeCore.tiers.map(d=>`<div><span>${d.name}</span><b>+${d.reward}<small> coins</small></b></div>`).join('')}</div></section><div class="section-heading"><h2>More ways to play</h2><span class="small muted">Keyboard + touch</span></div><div class="arcade-grid">${ArcadeCore.games.slice(1).map(g=>`<article class="arcade-card" style="--game-accent:${g.color}"><div class="arcade-card-art">${arcadeArt(g.id)}</div><div class="arcade-card-body"><span class="eyebrow">${g.tag} / FREE PLAY</span><h3>${g.name}</h3><p>${g.description}</p><div class="arcade-card-meta"><b>+10–60 coins</b><span>4 difficulties</span></div><button class="btn secondary full" data-action="a-setup" data-id="${g.id}">Play ${g.name} ↗</button><small>${esc(bestLabel(g.id))}</small></div></article>`).join('')}<article class="arcade-card" style="--game-accent:#d7dcd9"><div class="arcade-card-art">${arcadeArt('slots')}</div><div class="arcade-card-body"><span class="eyebrow">CHANCE / CLASSIC CABINET</span><h3>Slot machine</h3><p>Three retro reels. Pick a stake and see where the symbols land.</p><div class="arcade-card-meta"><b>1–100 point stake</b><span>Random returns</span></div><button class="btn secondary full" data-action="a-slots">Open slot machine ↗</button><small>Uses your existing points. A spin can lose its stake.</small></div></article></div><section class="arcade-daily"><div><span class="eyebrow">TODAY’S SKILL GAME COINS</span><strong>${member?points(daily?.earned||0):'—'} <small>/ 300</small></strong><p>${member?(daily?points(daily.remaining)+' coins available · '+daily.startsRemaining+' rewarded starts left':'Loading your arcade progress…'):'Log in with an approved account to play and collect coins.'}</p></div><div class="arcade-daily-note"><p>Wins add coins straight to your balance. Up to 300 skill-game coins and 40 rewarded starts per UTC day; practice stays free after the limit. Losing costs no points.</p><button class="text-btn" data-action="a-refresh">Refresh progress</button></div></section>${arcadeError?`<p class="inline-error">${esc(arcadeError)}</p>`:''}${arcadePending?'<div class="notice arcade-unclaimed"><div><b>A game result still needs collecting.</b><p>Retry the saved result before starting another rewarded run.</p></div><button class="btn compact" data-action="a-retryClaim">Retry collection</button><button class="text-btn" data-action="a-discard">Dismiss result</button></div>':''}`;
}
function arcadeSetupBody(){const {game,difficulty}=arcadeSelection,g=ArcadeCore.games.find(g=>g.id===game),r=ArcadeCore.challenge(game,difficulty,1),available=arcadeData?.remaining??300,starts=arcadeData?.startsRemaining??40,award=starts?Math.min(r.reward,available):0;
 const detail=game==='lock'?`${r.goal} pins · ${['Relaxed','Steady','Fast','Very fast'][r.level]} marker · ${['Wide','Medium','Small','Tiny'][r.level]} hit window`:game==='echo'?`${r.rounds} rounds · Patterns grow from ${r.base} to ${r.base+r.rounds-1} pads · Faster flashes at higher difficulty`:`Survive ${r.duration/1000} seconds · 3 lanes · ${r.level>1?'Some waves block two lanes':'One blocked lane per wave'}`;
 return `<div class="arcade-setup"><span class="eyebrow">${g.tag} / CHOOSE YOUR CHALLENGE</span><h2>${g.name}</h2><p>${g.description}</p><div class="arcade-difficulties" role="group" aria-label="Difficulty">${ArcadeCore.tiers.map(t=>`<button class="${t.id===difficulty?'selected':''}" data-action="a-difficulty" data-id="${t.id}" aria-pressed="${t.id===difficulty}"><span>${t.name}</span><strong>+${t.reward}</strong><small>coins / win</small></button>`).join('')}</div><div class="arcade-difficulty-detail">${esc(detail)}</div><div class="arcade-how"><b>How to play</b><p>${game==='lock'?'Press Space, Enter, or POP when the white marker reaches the yellow target. Tapping early or letting the target pass ends the run. Each hit reverses direction.':game==='echo'?'Watch the numbered pads light up, then repeat the sequence using 1–4 or tap the pads. The pattern grows after each correct round.':'Use ← → or A/D to switch one lane at a time. On a phone, tap the left and right controls. Dodge every crate until the timer finishes.'}</p></div><p class="form-note">${award?'This run can earn up to '+award+' coins.':'Your reward limit is reached. This will be a free practice run.'} No entry fee. Losing costs no points. Closing or hiding the game ends the attempt.</p><div class="inline-error" id="arcadeSetupError" role="alert"></div><button class="btn full" data-action="a-start">${award?'Play for up to '+award+' coins':'Start free practice'} ↗</button>${award?'<button class="text-btn arcade-practice" data-action="a-practice">Just practice · no coins</button>':''}</div>`;
}
function showArcadeSetup(game){if(!ArcadeCore.games.some(g=>g.id===game)||!gate())return;restoreArcadePending();if(arcadePending){toast('Collect or dismiss your saved game result first.');arcadeView='hub';renderPage();return;}arcadeSelection={game,difficulty:arcadeSelection.difficulty};arcadeStartRequest=null;showModal('The tribe arcade',arcadeSetupBody(),null,true);$('#dialog').classList.add('arcade-dialog');cleanupGame=()=>{arcadeGeneration++;stopArcadeLocal();};}
async function startArcade(practice){if(arcadeRun||!gate())return;const generation=arcadeGeneration,who=state.me.id,{game,difficulty}=arcadeSelection,signature=game+':'+difficulty+':'+practice;if(!arcadeStartRequest||arcadeStartRequest.signature!==signature)arcadeStartRequest={signature,data:{game,difficulty,practice,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()}};$$('#dialog button').forEach(b=>{if(b.dataset.action!=='closeModal')b.disabled=true;});const error=$('#arcadeSetupError');if(error)error.textContent='Getting your challenge ready…';
 try{const r=await call('arcadeStart',arcadeStartRequest.data);if(state.me?.id!==who||generation!==arcadeGeneration)return;arcadeData=r.arcade;arcadeStartRequest=null;const challenge=ArcadeCore.challenge(r.run.game,r.run.difficulty,r.run.seed),s={run:r.run,challenge,who,generation,inputs:[],startAt:performance.now()+2500,phase:'countdown',view:ArcadeCore.evaluate(challenge,[],0),abort:new AbortController(),lastDraw:0,lastStatus:0,flash:0};arcadeRun=s;$('#dialogBody').innerHTML=arcadePlayBody(s);mountArcade(s);}
 catch(e){if(state.me?.id===who&&generation===arcadeGeneration&&error)error.textContent=e.message;}finally{if(generation===arcadeGeneration)$$('#dialog button').forEach(b=>b.disabled=false);}
}
function arcadePlayBody(s){const g=ArcadeCore.games.find(g=>g.id===s.run.game),t=ArcadeCore.tiers.find(t=>t.id===s.run.difficulty);return `<div class="arcade-play"><div class="arcade-play-top"><div><span class="eyebrow">${t.name.toUpperCase()} / ${s.run.eligible?'REWARDED RUN':'PRACTICE'}</span><h2>${g.name}</h2></div><span class="arcade-prize">${s.run.eligible?'◉ +'+s.run.reward+' possible':'Free practice'}</span></div><div class="arcade-board-wrap">${g.id==='echo'?'<div class="echo-board"><div id="echoPrompt" class="echo-prompt">Get ready</div><div class="echo-pads">'+[0,1,2,3].map(n=>`<button class="echo-pad pad-${n}" data-action="a-pad" data-id="${n}" aria-label="Pad ${n+1}"><span>${n+1}</span></button>`).join('')+'</div><div class="echo-progress" id="echoProgress"></div></div>':'<canvas id="arcadeCanvas" width="600" height="470" aria-label="'+g.name+' game board"></canvas>'}<div class="arcade-countdown" id="arcadeCountdown" role="status">3</div></div><div class="arcade-live-stats"><span id="arcadeProgress">Get ready</span><span id="arcadeTime">0.0s</span></div>${g.id==='lock'?'<button class="arcade-pop" data-action="a-tap">POP <small>Space / Enter / tap</small></button>':g.id==='dodge'?'<div class="arcade-lane-controls"><button data-action="a-lane" data-id="-1" aria-label="Move left">← <small>A / LEFT</small></button><button data-action="a-lane" data-id="1" aria-label="Move right">→ <small>D / RIGHT</small></button></div>':''}<div class="arcade-game-footer"><span>Closing or hiding ends this attempt.</span><button class="text-btn" data-action="a-sound">Sound: ${arcadeSound?'on':'off'}</button></div><div id="arcadeOutcome" aria-live="polite"></div></div>`;}
function arcadeTime(s){return Math.min(180000,Math.max(0,Math.floor((performance.now()-s.startAt)/10)*10));}
function arcadeTone(good){if(!arcadeSound)return;try{arcadeAudio??=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();const o=arcadeAudio.createOscillator(),v=arcadeAudio.createGain(),now=arcadeAudio.currentTime;o.type='sine';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(good?660:160,now);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(good?980:80,now+.12);v.gain.setValueAtTime(.045,now);v.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,now+.16);o.connect(v);v.connect(arcadeAudio.destination);o.start();o.stop(now+.17);}catch{}}
function mountArcade(s){const signal=s.abort.signal;s.canvas=$('#arcadeCanvas');s.ctx=s.canvas?.getContext('2d');document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(arcadeRun!==s||e.repeat||!$('#dialog').open)return;const game=s.run.game;if(game==='lock'&&['Space','Enter'].includes(e.code)){if(e.target.closest('button')&&e.target.dataset.action!=='a-tap')return;e.preventDefault();arcadeInput();}if(game==='echo'&&/^Digit[1-4]$/.test(e.code)){e.preventDefault();arcadeInput(Number(e.code.slice(-1))-1);}if(game==='dodge'&&['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','KeyA','KeyD'].includes(e.code)){e.preventDefault();arcadeInput(['ArrowLeft','KeyA'].includes(e.code)?-1:1);}},{signal});document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden&&arcadeRun===s&&['countdown','playing'].includes(s.phase)){s.phase='abandoned';cancelAnimationFrame(s.raf);$('#arcadeCountdown').hidden=true;$('#arcadeOutcome').innerHTML='<div class="arcade-outcome"><h3>Come back for a fresh run.</h3><p>This attempt ended when the game was hidden. No points were spent.</p><button class="btn" data-action="a-again">Try again</button></div>';}},{signal});
 const frame=stamp=>{if(arcadeRun!==s)return;if(s.phase==='countdown'&&stamp>=s.startAt)s.phase='playing';if(s.phase==='playing'){s.view=ArcadeCore.evaluate(s.challenge,s.inputs,arcadeTime(s));if(s.view.terminal){finishArcadeLocal(s);return;}}if(!s.lastDraw||stamp-s.lastDraw>=25){s.lastDraw=stamp;drawArcade(s);updateArcadeHUD(s);}if(['countdown','playing'].includes(s.phase))s.raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);};s.raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
function arcadeInput(value){const s=arcadeRun;if(!s||s.phase!=='playing')return;const time=arcadeTime(s);if(time<=(s.inputs.at(-1)?.t??-1))return;const before=ArcadeCore.evaluate(s.challenge,s.inputs,time);if(before.terminal){s.view=before;finishArcadeLocal(s);return;}const e={t:time};if(s.run.game==='echo'){if(before.flashing||value<0||value>3)return;e.v=value;}if(s.run.game==='dodge'){e.v=Math.max(0,Math.min(2,before.lane+value));if(e.v===before.lane||s.inputs.length>=600)return;}s.inputs.push(e);s.view=ArcadeCore.evaluate(s.challenge,s.inputs,time);s.flash=performance.now()+180;arcadeTone(!s.view.terminal||s.view.won);drawArcade(s);updateArcadeHUD(s);if(s.view.terminal)finishArcadeLocal(s);}
function updateArcadeHUD(s){const time=arcadeTime(s),v=s.view,c=s.challenge,count=$('#arcadeCountdown');if(!count)return;count.hidden=s.phase!=='countdown';if(s.phase==='countdown')count.textContent=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((s.startAt-performance.now())/1000));$('#arcadeTime').textContent=(Math.min(time,s.run.game==='dodge'?c.duration:180000)/1000).toFixed(1)+'s';$('#arcadeProgress').textContent=s.run.game==='lock'?v.progress+' / '+v.total+' pins popped':s.run.game==='echo'?'Round '+Math.min(v.progress+1,c.rounds)+' / '+c.rounds:Math.max(0,Math.ceil((c.duration-time)/1000))+'s to survive';if(s.run.game==='echo'){const active=s.phase==='playing'&&!v.terminal;$('#echoPrompt').textContent=v.terminal?(v.won?'Pattern mastered!':'Give it another try'):!active?'Get ready':v.flashing?(time<v.stageStart?'Nice! Next round…':'Watch the pattern'):'Your turn · repeat '+v.length+' pads';$('#echoProgress').textContent=active&&!v.flashing?v.index+' / '+v.length+' correct · '+Math.max(0,Math.ceil((v.deadline-time)/1000))+'s left':'';$$('.echo-pad').forEach((b,i)=>{b.classList.toggle('lit',active&&v.flashing&&v.lit===i);b.disabled=!active||v.flashing;});}}
function drawArcade(s){if(!s.ctx)return;const c=s.ctx,w=600,h=470,t=arcadeTime(s),r=s.challenge,v=s.view;c.clearRect(0,0,w,h);const bg=c.createRadialGradient(300,215,15,300,215,400);bg.addColorStop(0,r.game==='lock'?'#203724':'#243238');bg.addColorStop(1,'#0b1410');c.fillStyle=bg;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
 if(r.game==='lock'){const x=300,y=224,R=153;c.strokeStyle='#9acd6720';c.lineWidth=1;for(const rad of [180,202]){c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,rad,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();}c.strokeStyle='#28432c';c.lineWidth=12;c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,R,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.strokeStyle='#c8f66b';c.lineWidth=5;c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,175,-Math.PI/2,-Math.PI/2+(v.progress/v.total)*Math.PI*2);c.stroke();if(!v.won){c.strokeStyle='#ffca7060';c.lineWidth=26;c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,R,v.target-r.tolerance,v.target+r.tolerance);c.stroke();c.fillStyle='#ffca70';c.beginPath();c.arc(x+Math.cos(v.target)*R,y+Math.sin(v.target)*R,14,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();}c.shadowColor='#e9ffe3';c.shadowBlur=12;c.strokeStyle='#f3ffee';c.lineWidth=7;c.lineCap='round';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+Math.cos(v.angle)*(R-16),y+Math.sin(v.angle)*(R-16));c.lineTo(x+Math.cos(v.angle)*(R+16),y+Math.sin(v.angle)*(R+16));c.stroke();c.shadowBlur=0;c.lineCap='butt';c.fillStyle='#c8f66b';c.textAlign='center';c.font='bold 68px sans-serif';c.fillText(Math.max(0,v.total-v.progress),x,y+17);c.fillStyle='#a0b59c';c.font='11px sans-serif';c.fillText(v.won?'LOCK OPEN':'PINS TO UNLOCK',x,y+48);c.font='13px sans-serif';c.fillText(v.dir===1?'↻ CLOCKWISE':'↺ COUNTERCLOCKWISE',x,435);if(performance.now()<s.flash){c.strokeStyle='#c8f66b80';c.lineWidth=3;c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,90+(180-(s.flash-performance.now()))*.25,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();}}
 else{const left=96,laneW=136,top=-30,bottom=382;c.fillStyle='#152326';c.fillRect(left,0,laneW*3,h);for(let i=1;i<3;i++){c.strokeStyle='#51685b66';c.setLineDash([14,20]);c.lineDashOffset=-t*.05;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(left+i*laneW,0);c.lineTo(left+i*laneW,h);c.stroke();}c.setLineDash([]);c.strokeStyle='#c8f66b44';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(left,bottom+24);c.lineTo(left+laneW*3,bottom+24);c.stroke();r.waves.forEach(wave=>{const age=(t-(wave.at-r.fall))/r.fall;if(age<-.1||age>1.25)return;const y=top+age*(bottom-top);wave.lanes.forEach(lane=>{const x=left+lane*laneW+laneW/2;c.fillStyle='#b4824f';c.fillRect(x-36,y-29,72,58);c.strokeStyle='#f3be7e';c.lineWidth=3;c.strokeRect(x-34,y-27,68,54);c.strokeStyle='#755332';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-29,y-22);c.lineTo(x+29,y+22);c.moveTo(x+29,y-22);c.lineTo(x-29,y+22);c.stroke();});});const px=left+(v.lane??1)*laneW+laneW/2;c.fillStyle='#c8f66b25';c.beginPath();c.ellipse(px,bottom+22,34,10,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();c.save();c.translate(px,bottom-5);c.rotate(-.45);c.fillStyle='#ffe166';c.strokeStyle='#c4942c';c.lineWidth=2;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(-20,-26);c.bezierCurveTo(-31,5,-13,35,20,19);c.bezierCurveTo(-1,20,-12,1,-11,-23);c.closePath();c.fill();c.stroke();c.fillStyle='#71552b';c.fillRect(-20,-29,10,6);c.beginPath();c.arc(19,19,3,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();c.restore();c.textAlign='center';c.fillStyle='#092015';c.fillRect(190,14,220,32);c.fillStyle='#ccf68c';c.font='bold 12px sans-serif';c.fillText('DODGE THE CRATES · STAY IN PLAY',300,35);}
}
function finishArcadeLocal(s){if(s.phase!=='playing')return;s.phase='claiming';cancelAnimationFrame(s.raf);drawArcade(s);updateArcadeHUD(s);const request={runId:s.run.id,inputs:s.inputs.map(e=>({...e})),duration:arcadeTime(s)};arcadePending=request;try{sessionStorage.setItem(arcadePendingKey(),JSON.stringify(request));}catch{}$('#arcadeOutcome').innerHTML=`<div class="arcade-outcome"><h3>${s.view.won?'Challenge cleared!':'So close. Go again.'}</h3><p>${esc(s.view.reason)}</p><span class="muted">Checking your result…</span></div>`;collectArcade(s).catch(()=>{});}
async function collectArcade(s=arcadeRun){restoreArcadePending();if(!arcadePending||!state.me)return;const who=state.me.id,request=arcadePending;try{const r=await call('arcadeFinish',request);if(state.me?.id!==who)return;arcadeData=r.arcade;arcadePending=null;try{sessionStorage.removeItem(arcadePendingKey());}catch{}expansionMe(r.me);if(s&&arcadeRun===s){s.phase='finished';$('#arcadeOutcome').innerHTML=`<div class="arcade-outcome ${r.result.won?'win':''}"><span class="eyebrow">${r.result.won?'CHALLENGE COMPLETE':'NEXT RUN IS YOURS'}</span><h3>${r.result.won?'You cracked it.':'Keep that monke focus.'}</h3><strong>${r.result.earned?'+'+r.result.earned+' coins':r.result.practice?'Practice complete':'0 coins'}</strong><p>${esc(r.result.reason)} ${r.result.earned?'Added to your points balance.':r.result.won&&!r.result.practice?'Daily reward limit reached.':'No points lost.'}</p><div class="row"><button class="btn" data-action="a-again">Play again</button><button class="btn secondary" data-action="closeModal">Back to arcade</button></div></div>`;$('#arcadeOutcome').scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});}else toast(r.result.earned?'+'+r.result.earned+' arcade coins collected.':'Arcade result saved.');if(page==='minigames')renderPage();}
 catch(e){if(state.me?.id!==who)return;if(s&&arcadeRun===s){s.phase='claim-error';$('#arcadeOutcome').innerHTML=`<div class="arcade-outcome"><h3>Your result is saved in this tab.</h3><p>${esc(e.message)}</p><button class="btn" data-action="a-retryClaim">Retry collection</button><button class="text-btn" data-action="a-discard">Dismiss result</button></div>`;}else{arcadeError=e.message;if(page==='minigames')renderPage();}throw e;}}
async function arcadeAction(name,el){const id=el?.dataset.id;switch(name){case 'a-hub':arcadeView='hub';renderPage();return loadArcade();case 'a-slots':arcadeView='slots';return renderPage();case 'a-refresh':return loadArcade();case 'a-setup':return showArcadeSetup(id);case 'a-difficulty':if(!ArcadeCore.tiers.some(t=>t.id===id))return;arcadeSelection.difficulty=id;arcadeStartRequest=null;$('#dialogBody').innerHTML=arcadeSetupBody();return;case 'a-start':return startArcade(false);case 'a-practice':return startArcade(true);case 'a-tap':return arcadeInput();case 'a-pad':return arcadeInput(Number(id));case 'a-lane':return arcadeInput(Number(id));case 'a-again':{const game=arcadeRun?.run.game||arcadeSelection.game;stopArcadeLocal();return showArcadeSetup(game);}case 'a-retryClaim':return collectArcade();case 'a-discard':arcadePending=null;try{sessionStorage.removeItem(arcadePendingKey());}catch{}closeModal();return renderPage();case 'a-sound':arcadeSound=!arcadeSound;if(arcadeSound){arcadeTone(true);arcadeAudio?.resume?.();}el.textContent='Sound: '+(arcadeSound?'on':'off');return;}}

function setToken(v,remember=false){token=v;try{sessionStorage.removeItem('tribe.session');localStorage.removeItem('tribe.session');if(v)(remember?localStorage:sessionStorage).setItem('tribe.session',v);}catch{}}
function toast(text,error=false){const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='toast'+(error?' error':'');e.textContent=text;$('#toasts').appendChild(e);setTimeout(()=>e.remove(),5000);}
function progress(delta){busy=Math.max(0,busy+delta);$('#requestProgress').hidden=busy===0;}
function rawCall(action,data={}){
 if(!LIVE)return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>setTimeout(()=>{try{resolve(demoCall(action,data));}catch(e){reject(e);}},75));
 return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
  let finished=false,timer;
  const finish=(error,value)=>{if(finished)return;finished=true;clearTimeout(timer);error?reject(error):resolve(value);};
  // Time out reads only. A timed-out write may still commit on the server.
  if(['boot','messages','activity','pageData'].includes(action))timer=setTimeout(()=>finish(new Error('The connection is taking too long. Please try again.')),25000);
  try{google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r=>r&&r.ok?finish(null,r.data):finish(new Error(r?.error||'The server did not respond.'))).withFailureHandler(()=>finish(new Error('Connection lost. Please try again.'))).api({action,data,token});}catch(e){finish(e);}
 });
}
async function call(action,data={},quiet=false){const requestedToken=token;if(!quiet)progress(1);try{const result=await rawCall(action,data);
 if(result?.approvalQueued){closeModal();applyTheme();approvalData=null;toast(result.message);renderPage();const e=new Error(result.message);e.approvalQueued=true;throw e;}
 if(state?.loaded){const invalid={shopSave:['shop'],gameSave:['games'],assignRole:['members'],points:['members'],moderate:['members'],profile:['members']}[action]||[];invalid.forEach(k=>{state.loaded[k]=false;delete state.dataErrors?.[k];});}
 return result;}catch(e){if(e.message==='SESSION_EXPIRED'&&token===requestedToken){setToken('');if(ready){toast('Your session expired. Please log in again.',true);showAuth('login');}}throw e;}finally{if(!quiet)progress(-1);}}
function createMemoryDB(){const tables={};return {all:n=>Object.values(tables[n]||{}),get:(n,id)=>tables[n]?.[id]||null,put:(n,v)=>(tables[n]??={})[v.id]=v,remove:(n,id)=>{if(tables[n])delete tables[n][id];}};}

function demoCall(action,data){if(action==='aiStatus')return {enabled:false,configured:false,model:'gemini-3.5-flash-lite',perUser:20,perSite:100,owner:demoUser?.role==='owner'};if(action==='aiSettings'||action==='aiChat')throw new Error('Connect MonkeGPT on your deployed Apps Script site. Use Explore preview here.');return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(Tribe.run({db:demoDB,user:demoUser,now:Date.now(),id:()=>crypto.randomUUID(),random:Math.random},action,data))); }
function userById(id){return state?.members.find(u=>u.id===id)||{id,displayName:'Former member',username:'former',role:'member',equipped:{}};}
function roleFor(u){return Tribe.role(state.config,u||{role:'pending'});}
function itemFor(u,type,items=state?.shop||[]){const id=u?.equipped?.[type];return items.find(s=>s.id===id);}
function cssURL(v){return String(v).replace(/["'\\()<>\r\n]/g,c=>'%'+c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16));}
function bannerCSS(u,items){let i=itemFor(u,'banner',items);return i?Tribe.presets[i.value]||`url("${cssURL(i.value)}") center / cover`:'linear-gradient(120deg,#172815,#3b4e29,#1c291b)';}
function nameStyle(u,items){const c=itemFor(u,'color',items),f=itemFor(u,'font',items);return esc(`color:${c?.value||roleFor(u).color};font-family:${fonts[f?.value]||'inherit'}`);}
function avatar(u,large=false,items){u=u||{displayName:'?',equipped:{}};let frame=itemFor(u,'frame',items);return `<span class="avatar${large?' large':''}${frame?' frame-'+esc(frame.value):''}">${u.avatar?`<img src="${esc(u.avatar)}" alt="${esc(u.displayName)}" loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="no-referrer">`:esc((u.displayName||u.username||'?').slice(0,1).toUpperCase())}</span>`;}
function username(u,items){const badge=itemFor(u,'badge',items);return `<span style="${nameStyle(u,items)}">${esc(u.displayName)}${badge?' '+esc(badge.value):''}</span>`;}
function rolePill(u){const r=roleFor(u);return `<span class="pill neutral" style="color:${esc(r.color)}">${esc(r.name)}</span>`;}
function opts(values,selected){return values.map(v=>{let [value,label]=Array.isArray(v)?v:[v,v];return `<option value="${esc(value)}" ${String(value)===String(selected)?'selected':''}>${esc(label)}</option>`;}).join('');}

async function submitForm(form,fn){const buttons=$$('button[type=submit]',form);buttons.forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);const e=$('[data-form-error]',form);if(e)e.textContent='';try{await fn(new FormData(form));}catch(err){if(!err.approvalQueued){if(e)e.textContent=err.message;else toast(err.message,true);}}finally{buttons.forEach(b=>b.disabled=false);}}

let lastSync=0,lastHeartbeat=0,syncSequence=0;
function mergeRecords(before=[],updates=[]){const byId=new Map(before.map(x=>[x.id,x]));updates.forEach(x=>byId.set(x.id,x));return [...byId.values()];}
function acceptBoot(next,render=true){
 anchorServerClock(next.serverTime);
 const previous=state,sameUser=previous?.me?.id===next.me?.id;
 if(next.loaded){
  if(sameUser){
   for(const k of ['members','shop','games'])next[k]=mergeRecords(previous?.[k],next[k]);
   next.loaded={...next.loaded,...previous?.loaded};next.dataAt={...previous?.dataAt};next.dataErrors={...previous?.dataErrors};next.threads=previous?.threads||[];
  }else{next.dataAt={};next.dataErrors={};}
 }else{next.loaded={members:true,shop:true,games:true};next.dataAt={members:Date.now(),shop:Date.now(),games:Date.now()};next.dataErrors={};}
 next.preview=sameUser?previous?.preview||[]:[];
 next.previewLoaded=!next.me||next.me.role==='pending'||(sameUser&&previous?.previewLoaded)||false;
 next.previewAt=sameUser?previous?.previewAt||0:0;
 if(!next.me||next.me.role==='pending'){next.threads=[];next.preview=[];}
 if(!sameUser)resetExpansion();
 state=next;ready=true;connectionError='';lastSync=Date.now();applyTheme();renderShell();renderConnection();
 if(render||page==='daily'||previous?.me?.role!==state.me?.role)renderPage();
 if(page==='home'&&state.me&&state.me.role!=='pending'&&Date.now()-state.previewAt>30000)loadActivity('preview').catch(()=>{});
}
async function sync(render=true){
 const sequence=++syncSequence,requestedToken=token;
 const next=await call('boot',{compact:!!LIVE,threads:page==='dms',preview:false},true);
 if(sequence!==syncSequence||requestedToken!==token)return;
 acceptBoot(next,render);
}
function pageNeeds(id=page){
 if(id==='profile')return profileUserId&&profileUserId!==state.me?.id?['members','shop']:['shop'];
 if(id==='games')return ['games'];
 if(id==='shop'||(id==='personalization'&&state.me))return ['shop'];
 if((['chat','dms','admin','trading','duels'].includes(id)&&state.me&&state.me.role!=='pending')||(id==='other'&&otherTab==='members'))return ['members','shop'];
 return [];
}
const dataRequests=new Map();
function ensureData(parts=pageNeeds()){
 if(!ready)return Promise.resolve();
 parts=parts.filter(k=>!state.loaded[k]||Date.now()-(state.dataAt?.[k]||0)>60000);
 if(!parts.length)return Promise.resolve();
 const requestedToken=token,key=parts.slice().sort().join(',')+':'+requestedToken;
 if(dataRequests.has(key))return dataRequests.get(key);
 const promise=call('pageData',{parts},true).then(result=>{
  if(token!==requestedToken)return;
  for(const k of parts){state[k]=result[k]||[];state.loaded[k]=true;(state.dataAt??={})[k]=Date.now();delete state.dataErrors?.[k];}
  renderShell();
 }).catch(error=>{if(token===requestedToken)for(const k of parts)(state.dataErrors??={})[k]=error.message;throw error;}).finally(()=>dataRequests.delete(key));
 dataRequests.set(key,promise);return promise;
}
const activityRequests={};
function loadActivity(kind){
 const uid=state?.me?.id,requestedToken=token;
 if(!uid||state.me.role==='pending')return Promise.resolve();
 if(activityRequests[kind]?.uid===uid)return activityRequests[kind].promise;
 const request={uid};activityRequests[kind]=request;
 request.promise=call('activity',{threads:kind==='threads',preview:kind==='preview'},true).then(result=>{
  if(state?.me?.id!==uid||token!==requestedToken)return;
  state.members=mergeRecords(state.members,result.members);state.shop=mergeRecords(state.shop,result.shop);
  if(kind==='threads'){
   const changed=JSON.stringify(state.threads)!==JSON.stringify(result.threads);state.threads=result.threads;
   if(page==='dms'&&changed&&$('.channel-list')){const previousDM=dmId,container=document.createElement('div');container.innerHTML=renderDMs();const list=$('.channel-list',container);if(list)$('.channel-list').replaceWith(list);if(previousDM!==dmId){renderPage();loadMessages().catch(()=>{});}}
  }
  else{state.preview=result.preview;state.previewLoaded=true;state.previewError=false;state.previewAt=Date.now();if(page==='home')renderPage();}
 }).catch(error=>{
  if(state?.me?.id===uid&&token===requestedToken&&kind==='preview'){state.previewError=true;if(page==='home')renderPage();}
  throw error;
 }).finally(()=>{if(activityRequests[kind]===request)delete activityRequests[kind];});
 return request.promise;
}

function bindThemePreview(){const f=$('#themeForm');if(!f)return;

function showAuth(mode='login'){
 const signup=mode==='signup',recover=mode==='recover';
 showModal(signup?'Join the tribe':recover?'Recover your account':'Welcome back',`<div class="auth-heading"><div class="brandmark">${brandImage()}</div><h3>${signup?'Your branch is waiting.':recover?'Back to your tribe.':'Good to see you, monke.'}</h3><p>${signup?'Create your account. Find your people.':recover?'Use the recovery key you saved when signing up.':'The jungle missed you.'}</p></div>${!LIVE?'<div class="notice">This is the local preview. Real accounts and shared messages connect when you deploy to Google Apps Script.<div class="spacer"></div><button class="btn full" data-action="enterDemo">Explore preview as owner</button></div>':''}<form id="authForm">${field('Username','username',recover?state.me?.username||'':'','text','required minlength="3" maxlength="20" autocomplete="username" pattern="[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{3,20}"')}${signup?field('Display name','displayName','','text','required minlength="2" maxlength="24" autocomplete="nickname"'):''}${field(recover?'New password':'Password','password','','password',`required minlength="10" maxlength="64" autocomplete="${signup||recover?'new-password':'current-password'}"`)}${signup||recover?field('Confirm password','confirm','','password','required minlength="10" maxlength="64" autocomplete="new-password"'):''}${recover?field('Recovery key','recoveryKey','','text','required autocomplete="off"'):''}${signup?`<details class="auth-setup"><summary>Have an invite code?</summary>${field('Invite code','inviteCode','','text','maxlength="64"')}</details><details class="auth-setup"><summary>Owner setup (first account only)</summary><p class="form-note">Paste the one-time key from SETUP_TRIBE in your Apps Script execution log.</p>${field('Owner setup key','ownerKey','','password','autocomplete="off"')}</details>`:''}<label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="remember"> Keep me logged in on this device</label>${formError()}<button type="submit" class="btn full" ${!LIVE?'disabled':''}>${signup?'Create my account':recover?'Reset password':'Log in'}</button></form><div class="auth-links"><button class="text-btn" data-action="${signup?'auth':'signup'}">${signup?'Already a monke? Log in':'New here? Create an account'}</button>${!recover?'<button class="text-btn" data-action="recover">Forgot password?</button>':''}</div>`,async d=>{
   if((signup||recover)&&d.get('password')!==d.get('confirm'))throw new Error('Your passwords do not match.');
   const r=await call(signup?'signup':recover?'recover':'login',Object.fromEntries(d));setToken(r.token,d.get('remember')==='on');closeModal();if(r.boot){++syncSequence;acceptBoot(r.boot);}else await sync();
   if(r.recoveryKey)showModal('Save your recovery key',`<p class="game-instructions">Keep this key somewhere private. It lets you reset your password without email. It is shown only once; a password reset replaces the old key.</p><div class="code-display" id="recoveryKeyValue">${esc(r.recoveryKey)}</div><div class="spacer"></div><button class="btn full" data-action="copyRecovery">Copy recovery key</button><div class="spacer"></div><button class="btn secondary full" data-action="closeModal">I've saved it</button>`);
   else toast('Welcome back to the tribe.');
 });
}
function showProfile(){if(!state.me)return showAuth();let u=state.me,unlocked=Tribe.admin(u)||u.inventory.some(id=>state.shop.find(i=>i.id===id)?.type==='avatar');showModal('Your profile',`<form id="profileForm"><div class="row" style="margin-bottom:24px">${avatar(u,true)}<div><h3>${esc(u.displayName)}</h3><p class="small">@${esc(u.username)}</p></div></div>${field('Display name','displayName',u.displayName,'text','required minlength="2" maxlength="24"')}<label class="field">About you<textarea name="bio" maxlength="180" placeholder="A few words. A little personality.">${esc(u.bio)}</textarea></label><label class="field">Profile picture<input type="file" id="avatarFile" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" ${unlocked?'':'disabled'}></label><p class="form-note">${unlocked?'PNG, JPG, or WebP. Your photo is cropped and resized to a small square.':'Unlock “Your own face” in the shop to upload a photo.'}</p><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="removeAvatar"> Remove current photo</label>${formError()}<button class="btn full" type="submit">Save profile</button></form>`,async d=>{const p={displayName:d.get('displayName'),bio:d.get('bio')};const file=$('#avatarFile').files[0];if(file)p.avatar=await resizeAvatar(file);if(d.get('removeAvatar'))p.avatar='';await call('profile',p);closeModal();await sync();toast('Looking good, monke.');});}
function resizeLogo(file){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
 if(!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp)$/.test(file.type)||file.size>8*1024*1024)return reject(new Error('Choose a PNG, JPG, or WebP under 8 MB.'));
 const img=new Image(),src=URL.createObjectURL(file);
 img.onload=()=>{try{const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=canvas.height=128;const scale=128/Math.max(img.width,img.height),w=img.width*scale,h=img.height*scale;canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,(128-w)/2,(128-h)/2,w,h);const output=canvas.toDataURL('image/webp',.8);if(output.length>30000)throw new Error('Choose a smaller or simpler logo image.');resolve(output);}catch(e){reject(e);}finally{URL.revokeObjectURL(src);}};
 img.onerror=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(src);reject(new Error('That image could not be opened.'));};img.src=src;
});}
function resizeAvatar(file){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{if(!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp)$/.test(file.type)||file.size>8*1024*1024)return reject(new Error('Choose a PNG, JPG, or WebP under 8 MB.'));const img=new Image(),src=URL.createObjectURL(file);img.onload=()=>{try{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=128;const ctx=c.getContext('2d'),s=Math.min(img.width,img.height);ctx.drawImage(img,(img.width-s)/2,(img.height-s)/2,s,s,0,0,128,128);const output=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.7);if(output.length>30000)throw new Error('That image is too detailed. Choose a simpler image.');resolve(output);}catch(e){reject(e);}finally{URL.revokeObjectURL(src);}};img.onerror=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(src);reject(new Error('That image could not be opened.'));};img.src=src;});}
function showGameEditor(id){return openGameBuilder(id);}
function newDM(preselected){if(!gate())return;let members=state.members.filter(u=>u.id!==state.me.id&&!u.banned&&u.role!=='pending');showModal('Start a conversation',`<p class="form-note">Choose one member for a DM, or several for a group. Groups can have up to 12 people. New group members can see earlier messages.</p><form>${field('Group name (for 3+ people)','name','','text','maxlength="40" placeholder="The banana council"')}<div class="select-members">${members.map(u=>`<label class="member-choice">${avatar(u)}<span><b>${esc(u.displayName)}</b><br><small class="muted">@${esc(u.username)}</small></span><input type="checkbox" name="members" value="${esc(u.id)}" ${u.id===preselected?'checked':''}></label>`).join('')||'<p class="small muted">No other approved members yet.</p>'}</div>${formError()}<button type="submit" class="btn full" ${members.length?'':'disabled'}>Start conversation</button></form>`,async d=>{const ids=d.getAll('members');if(!ids.length)throw new Error('Choose at least one member.');if(ids.length>1&&String(d.get('name')).trim().length<2)throw new Error('Give your group a name.');const r=await call('dmCreate',{members:ids,name:d.get('name')});dmId=r.thread.id;closeModal();await sync(false);await navigate('dms');});}
function showContentEditor(key){showModal('Edit '+({rules:'rules',upcoming:'upcoming updates',updates:'update log',credits:'credits'}[key]||'page'),`<form><label class="field">Page content<textarea name="text" rows="13" maxlength="6000">${esc(state.config.content[key])}</textarea></label><p class="form-note">Plain text. Use blank lines to separate paragraphs and updates.</p>${formError()}<button class="btn full" type="submit">Publish changes</button></form>`,async d=>{await call('contentSave',{key,text:d.get('text')});closeModal();await sync();toast('Page updated for the tribe.');});}
async function afterAdmin(){await sync();await loadAdmin();}
async function action(name,el){
 if(name==='retryConnection')return start().catch(error=>window.TribeStartup.fail(error));
 if(!ready)return toast(connectionError?'Use Try again above to reconnect.':'Your account is still connecting. One moment…');
 const id=el?.dataset.id;
 if(name.startsWith('a-'))return arcadeAction(name,el);
 if(name.startsWith('x-'))return expansionAction(name,el);
 if(name.startsWith('s-')||name.startsWith('d-'))return socialAction(name,el);
 if(name==='logout'&&duelSession&&duelOpen(duelSession.room))return duelAction('d-leave');
 switch(name){
  case 'auth':return showAuth('login');case 'signup':return showAuth('signup');case 'recover':return showAuth('recover');case 'closeModal':return closeModal();
  case 'retryData':pageNeeds().forEach(k=>{delete state.dataErrors?.[k];});return renderPage();
  case 'retryPreview':state.previewError=false;renderPage();return loadActivity('preview');
  case 'enterDemo':if(LIVE)return;demoUser=demoDB.get('Users','demo-owner');closeModal();await sync();toast('Preview account active. Changes reset when this page reloads.');return;
  case 'previewInfo':return showModal('Take a look around',`<p class="game-instructions">This local copy lets you try the design, games, shop, and owner controls. Preview data resets when you reload.</p><p class="game-instructions">Deploy the included Google Apps Script files to connect real accounts, shared chat, DMs, and saved points.</p><button class="btn full" data-action="enterDemo">Explore owner preview</button>`);
  case 'logout':await call('logout');setToken('');prefsDraft=null;adminState=null;closeModal();page='home';await sync();return toast('See you around, monke.');
  case 'copyRecovery':try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText($('#recoveryKeyValue').textContent);toast('Recovery key copied. Keep it private.');}catch{toast('Select the key and copy it with Ctrl+C.');}return;
  case 'profile':await ensureData(['shop']);return showProfile();
  case 'ownProfile':profileUserId='';closeModal();return navigate('profile');
  case 'viewProfile':profileUserId=id;closeModal();return navigate('profile',true);
  case 'daily':if(!gate()||dailyClaimBusy)return;{dailyClaimBusy=true;renderPage();try{const r=await call('daily');state.me=r.me;anchorServerClock(r.serverTime);renderShell();if(page==='daily')renderPage();return toast('Day '+r.day+' collected! +'+r.earned+' points.');}catch(error){await sync(false).catch(()=>{});throw error;}finally{dailyClaimBusy=false;if(page==='daily')renderPage();}}
  case 'previewShopItem':return selectShopPreview(id);
  case 'buy':return showPurchase(id);
  case 'ownedItem':personalTab='cosmetics';return navigate('personalization');
  case 'equip':await call('equip',{id:id||'',type:el.dataset.type});await sync();return toast(id?'Cosmetic equipped.':'Cosmetic removed.');
  case 'siteWallpaper':prefsDraft={...getPrefs(),wallpaper:'site'};applyPrefs();return renderPage();
  case 'savePreferences':{const p={...getPrefs()};if(p.wallpaper==='custom'&&!/^https:\/\/[^\s]+$/.test(p.custom||''))throw new Error('Add a valid HTTPS wallpaper image URL.');if(state.me)await call('preferences',{prefs:p});else{try{localStorage.setItem('tribe.preferences',JSON.stringify(p));}catch{}}prefsDraft=null;await sync();return toast(state.me?'Your space is saved.':'Wallpaper saved on this device.');}
  case 'addGame':if(!gate())return;return showGameEditor();case 'editGame':return showGameEditor(id);
  case 'claimVisit':if(!activeVisit)return;{const r=await call('externalClaim',{nonce:activeVisit.nonce});activeVisit=null;await sync(false);renderPage();return toast('Game visit rewarded: +'+r.earned+' points.');}
  case 'mini':return navigate('minigames');
  case 'react':await call('react',{id,emoji:el.dataset.emoji},true);return loadMessages();
  case 'deleteMessage':return showModal('Remove this message?',`<p class="game-instructions">The message will be replaced with “Message removed.”</p><form>${formError()}<button class="btn danger full" type="submit">Remove message</button></form>`,async()=>{await call('deleteMessage',{id});closeModal();await loadMessages();});
  case 'olderMessages':return loadMessages(chatMessages[0]?.at);
  case 'newDM':await ensureData(['members','shop']);return newDM();case 'messageUser':await ensureData(['members','shop']);return newDM(id);
  case 'toggleChannels':socialChannelsOpen=!socialChannelsOpen;if(socialChannelsOpen)socialMembersOpen=false;return renderPage();
  case 'toggleMembers':socialMembersOpen=!(socialMembersOpen??matchMedia('(min-width:1301px)').matches);if(socialMembersOpen)socialChannelsOpen=false;return renderPage();
  case 'chatRules':otherTab='rules';return navigate('other');
  case 'profileCosmetics':personalTab='cosmetics';return navigate('personalization');
  case 'addGroupMember':{const th=state.threads.find(t=>t.id===dmId),members=state.members.filter(u=>!th.members.includes(u.id)&&u.role!=='pending'&&!u.banned);return showModal('Add to the group',`<form><label class="field">Member<select name="userId">${opts(members.map(u=>[u.id,u.displayName]),'')}</select></label><p class="form-note">They will be able to read earlier messages in this group.</p>${formError()}<button type="submit" class="btn full" ${members.length?'':'disabled'}>Add member</button></form>`,async d=>{await call('dmAdd',{thread:dmId,userId:d.get('userId')});closeModal();await sync();});}
  case 'leaveGroup':return showModal('Leave this group?',`<p class="game-instructions">You will lose access to this conversation until a group creator adds you again.</p><form>${formError()}<button type="submit" class="btn danger full">Leave group</button></form>`,async()=>{await call('dmLeave',{thread:dmId});dmId='';closeModal();await sync();await loadMessages();});
  case 'adjustPoints':{const u=userById(id);return showModal('Adjust '+u.displayName+'’s points',`<form><p class="form-note">Current balance: ${u.points} points. Use a negative number to subtract.</p>${field('Point adjustment','amount','','number','required step="1" min="-100000" max="100000" placeholder="100 or -50"')}${field('Reason','reason','','text','required minlength="3" maxlength="160"')}${formError()}<button type="submit" class="btn full">Apply adjustment</button></form>`,async d=>{await call('points',{userId:id,amount:Number(d.get('amount')),reason:d.get('reason')});closeModal();await afterAdmin();toast('Balance updated.');});}
  case 'muteUser':{const u=adminState.users.find(u=>u.id===id);await call('moderate',{userId:id,kind:'mute',value:!(u.mutedUntil>Date.now())});await afterAdmin();return toast('Member moderation updated.');}
  case 'banUser':{const u=adminState.users.find(u=>u.id===id);return showModal(u.banned?'Restore account?':'Suspend account?',`<p class="game-instructions">${u.banned?'Restore access for':'Suspend access for'} @${esc(u.username)}. ${u.banned?'':'They will be unable to log in or use the site.'}</p><form>${formError()}<button type="submit" class="btn ${u.banned?'':'danger'} full">${u.banned?'Restore':'Suspend'} account</button></form>`,async()=>{await call('moderate',{userId:id,kind:'ban',value:!u.banned});closeModal();await afterAdmin();});}
  case 'newRole':return showModal('Create a tribe role',`<form>${field('Role name','name','','text','required minlength="2" maxlength="24"')}<label class="color-row">Role color<input name="color" type="color" value="#72ceba"></label><div class="spacer"></div><p class="form-note">Only the OWNER can add or create games.</p><label class="check-label"><input type="checkbox" name="moderate"> Can remove public messages</label><p class="form-note">Custom roles never receive owner controls or access to other members' DMs.</p>${formError()}<button class="btn full" type="submit">Create role</button></form>`,async d=>{await call('roleSave',{name:d.get('name'),color:d.get('color'),games:d.has('games'),moderate:d.has('moderate')});closeModal();await afterAdmin();});
  case 'newShopItem':return showShopEditor();case 'editShopItem':return showShopEditor(id);case 'editContent':return showContentEditor(el.dataset.key);
  case 'resetTheme':{const t=Tribe.config();await call('themeSave',{theme:t.theme,title:state.config.title,tagline:state.config.tagline,wallpaper:'particles'});await afterAdmin();return toast('Original theme restored.');}
 }
}
document.addEventListener('click',async e=>{const el=e.target.closest('[data-nav],[data-action],[data-shop-filter],[data-personal-tab],[data-wallpaper],[data-other-tab],[data-admin-tab],[data-channel],[data-thread],[data-game-open]');if(!el)return;if(el.disabled)return;try{
 if(el.dataset.gameOpen){if(!gate()){e.preventDefault();return;}call('externalStart',{id:el.dataset.gameOpen}).then(r=>{activeVisit=r.visit;renderVisit();}).catch(e=>toast(e.message,true));return;}
 e.preventDefault();if(el.dataset.nav)return await navigate(el.dataset.nav);
 if(el.dataset.action){el.disabled=true;try{return await action(el.dataset.action,el);}finally{if(el.isConnected)el.disabled=false;}}
 if(el.dataset.shopFilter){shopFilter=el.dataset.shopFilter;return renderPage();}
 if(el.dataset.personalTab){personalTab=el.dataset.personalTab;return renderPage();}
 if(el.dataset.wallpaper){prefsDraft={...getPrefs(),wallpaper:el.dataset.wallpaper};applyPrefs();return renderPage();}
 if(el.dataset.otherTab){otherTab=el.dataset.otherTab;return renderPage();}
 if(el.dataset.adminTab){applyTheme();adminTab=el.dataset.adminTab;return renderPage();}
 if(el.dataset.channel){socialChannelsOpen=false;channel=el.dataset.channel;chatMessages=[];hasMoreMessages=false;renderPage();return await loadMessages();}
 if(el.dataset.thread){socialChannelsOpen=false;dmId=el.dataset.thread;chatMessages=[];hasMoreMessages=false;renderPage();return await loadMessages();}
 }catch(err){if(!err.approvalQueued)toast(err.message,true);}});
document.addEventListener('change',async e=>{const uid=e.target.dataset.roleUser;if(!uid)return;e.target.disabled=true;try{await call('assignRole',{userId:uid,role:e.target.value});await afterAdmin();toast('Role assigned.');}catch(err){if(!err.approvalQueued)toast(err.message,true);renderPage();}finally{e.target.disabled=false;}});
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target.id==='gameSearch'){const pos=e.target.selectionStart;gameSearch=e.target.value;renderPage();$('#gameSearch').focus();$('#gameSearch').setSelectionRange(pos,pos);}if(e.target.id==='messageInput'){e.target.style.height='30px';e.target.style.height=Math.min(110,e.target.scrollHeight)+'px';}});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.target.id==='messageInput'&&e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey&&!e.isComposing){e.preventDefault();$('#chatForm').requestSubmit();}});
document.addEventListener('submit',e=>{if(e.target.closest('#dialog'))return;const f=e.target;if(f.id==='chatForm'){e.preventDefault();if(f.dataset.sending)return;const input=$('#messageInput'),text=input.value;if(!text.trim())return;f.dataset.sending='1';$('[type=submit]',f).disabled=true;call('send',{thread:page==='chat'?channel:dmId,text}).then(async r=>{input.value='';input.style.height='30px';state.me=r.me;renderShell();await loadMessages();const ms=$('#messages');if(ms)ms.scrollTop=ms.scrollHeight;}).catch(e=>toast(e.message,true)).finally(()=>{delete f.dataset.sending;if($('[type=submit]',f))$('[type=submit]',f).disabled=false;input.focus();});}
 if(f.id==='themeForm'){e.preventDefault();submitForm(f,async d=>{const theme={};for(const k of ['accent','secondary','bg','surface','text','muted','radius','sidebar','font'])theme[k]=d.get(k);let logo=state.config.logo||'';const file=$('#logoFile').files[0];if(d.has('resetLogo'))logo='';else if(file)logo=await resizeLogo(file);else if(String(d.get('logoUrl')||'').trim())logo=String(d.get('logoUrl')).trim();else if(/^https:/.test(logo))logo='';await call('themeSave',{theme,title:d.get('title'),tagline:d.get('tagline'),wallpaper:d.get('wallpaper'),logo});await afterAdmin();toast('New look, for the whole tribe.');});}
 if(f.id==='signupSettings'){e.preventDefault();submitForm(f,async d=>{await call('settingsSave',{registration:d.has('registration'),inviteCode:d.get('inviteCode')});await afterAdmin();toast('Signup settings saved.');});}
});
$('#menuToggle').addEventListener('click',()=>{document.body.classList.toggle('menu-open');$('#scrim').hidden=!document.body.classList.contains('menu-open');});$('#scrim').addEventListener('click',()=>{document.body.classList.remove('menu-open');$('#scrim').hidden=true;});

function startWallpaper(){const canvas=$('#particles'),ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return;let width=innerWidth,height=innerHeight,points=[],last=0;let mouse={x:width/2,y:height/2};function resize(){width=innerWidth;height=innerHeight;canvas.width=width;canvas.height=height;points=Array.from({length:80},()=>({x:Math.random()*width,y:Math.random()*height,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*.24,vy:Math.random()*.25+.04,r:Math.random()*1.5+.7}));}window.addEventListener('resize',resize);window.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{mouse={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};if(!state)return;const p=getPrefs(),motion=p.motion&&!matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;if(motion){document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--mx',(e.clientX/width-.5)*10+'px');document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--my',(e.clientY/height-.5)*8+'px');}if(p.glow)$('#cursorGlow').style.transform=`translate(${e.clientX-200}px,${e.clientY-200}px)`;},{passive:true});resize();function draw(time){requestAnimationFrame(draw);if(!state||document.hidden||time-last<33)return;last=time;const p=getPrefs(),mode=p.wallpaper==='site'?state.config.wallpaper:p.wallpaper;if(!p.motion||matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches){ctx.clearRect(0,0,width,height);return;}ctx.clearRect(0,0,width,height);if(mode==='still'||mode==='custom')return;const color=state.config.theme.accent;ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.strokeStyle=color;const amount=Math.min(points.length,p.particles||40);for(let i=0;i<amount;i++){const a=points[i],dx=a.x-mouse.x,dy=a.y-mouse.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);a.x+=a.vx*(mode==='rain'?.2:1);a.y+=a.vy*(mode==='rain'?13:1);if(dist<140&&dist>1){a.x+=dx/dist*.35;a.y+=dy/dist*.35;}if(a.x<0)a.x=width;if(a.x>width)a.x=0;if(a.y>height)a.y=0;ctx.globalAlpha=mode==='rain'?.22:.32;ctx.beginPath();if(mode==='rain'){ctx.moveTo(a.x,a.y);ctx.lineTo(a.x-1,a.y-18);ctx.stroke();}else{ctx.arc(a.x,a.y,a.r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();if(dist<150){ctx.globalAlpha=.04;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a.x,a.y);ctx.lineTo(mouse.x,mouse.y);ctx.stroke();}}}ctx.globalAlpha=1;}requestAnimationFrame(draw);}

 renderVisit();if(document.hidden||!ready||pollBusy||!LIVE)return;pollBusy=true;
 try{
  if(Date.now()-lastSync>30000){await sync(false);await ensureData();if(page==='dms'&&state.me&&state.me.role!=='pending')await loadActivity('threads');}
  if(['chat','dms'].includes(page)&&state.me&&state.me.role!=='pending')await loadMessages();
  if(state.me&&Date.now()-lastHeartbeat>60000){await call('heartbeat',{},true);lastHeartbeat=Date.now();}
  $('#connectionText').textContent='Connected to Google Apps Script';
 }catch{$('#connectionText').textContent='Reconnecting…';}finally{pollBusy=false;}
},6000);

let starting=false,wallpaperStarted=false,demoInitialized=false;
async function start(){
 if(starting||ready)return;starting=true;connectionError='';progress(1);
 // Render the real layout before the first network response. No private data is cached here.
 if(!state){
  state={config:Tribe.config(),me:null,members:[],shop:[],games:[],threads:[],preview:[],previewLoaded:false,needsOwner:false,loaded:{members:false,shop:false,games:false}};
  applyTheme();
 }
 renderShell();renderPage();renderConnection();
 window.TribeStartup.painted();
 if(!wallpaperStarted){startWallpaper();wallpaperStarted=true;}
 const slow=setTimeout(()=>{$('#connectionStatus').textContent='Still connecting. You can keep browsing the homepage.';},8000);
 try{
  if(!LIVE&&!demoInitialized){initDemo();demoInitialized=true;}
  try{await sync();}catch(e){if(e.message!=='SESSION_EXPIRED')throw e;setToken('');await sync();}
  if(state.needsOwner&&LIVE)toast('Finish owner setup using your one-time setup key.');
  if(['chat','dms'].includes(page))navigate(page).catch(e=>toast(e.message,true));
 }catch(e){connectionError=e.message;renderShell();renderPage();}
 finally{clearTimeout(slow);starting=false;progress(-1);renderConnection();}
}
/* Optional browser tooling uses the same visible navigation and preferences. */
if(document.modelContext?.registerTool){const life=new AbortController();try{Promise.resolve(document.modelContext.registerTool({name:'navigate_tribe',title:'Open a tribe tab',description:'Navigate to an existing tab in The Monke Tribe.',inputSchema:{type:'object',properties:{tab:{type:'string',enum:navItems.map(n=>n[0])}},required:['tab'],additionalProperties:false},annotations:{readOnlyHint:true,untrustedContentHint:false},execute:async input=>{if(!state||!navItems.some(n=>n[0]===input.tab))throw new Error('Unknown tab or site not ready.');if(input.tab==='admin'&&!Tribe.admin(state.me))throw new Error('Admin access required.');await navigate(input.tab);return {tab:page};}},{signal:life.signal})).catch(()=>{});}catch{}window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>life.abort(),{once:true});}
start().catch(error=>window.TribeStartup.fail(error));
</script>
